| Where a list holds a determined law's orbit, its registration repeats periodically from some step on (`lasso_of_held`). That it comes to rest cannot be refuted (`held_rest_unrefuted`, a double negation). It rests only on such sequences (`lasso_of_rest`), and its record never shows that it has come to rest | Where a list holds a determined law's orbit, its course through any map repeats periodically from some step on (`lasso_of_held`). | **What a learner can learn through its registration.** A learner reads what a registration registers and claims what it does not register. Take a registration `reg` of configurations and a target `val`. A learner claims at step t from the registered record before t, and rests on the target from step T when it claims the target at every step from T on | **What an extrapolation can learn through a map.** An extrapolation reads a course through a map and proposes what the map does not give. Take a map `reg` of configurations and a feature `val`. An extrapolation proposes at step t from the prefix before t of the course through the map, and rests on the feature from step T when it proposes the feature's value at every step from T on | L-extrap (the record id stays) |
| - *No premise.* Two configurations with the same registered future give every learner the same record at every step (`prefixes_same`). So at a step where their targets differ, a claim is right on at most one of them (`claim_not_right_on_both`). With the target's values decidable, it is refuted on one, while the two records are equal everywhere and do not show which | - *No premise.* Two configurations with the same future through the map give every extrapolation the same prefix at every step (`prefixes_same`). So at a step where their features differ, a proposed value is right on at most one of them (`claim_not_right_on_both`). With the feature's values decidable, it misses on one, while the two prefixes are equal everywhere and do not show which | L-feature |
| - A learner resting on both has targets that agree from some step on (`agree_of_rests_both`). In `Clearing` the ending within it is not selected by a selection through the selector's couplings, and is selected through the dweller's couplings and through the couplings taken together. | \| learning by refutation (按否证学习) \| Hypotheses are put in order, and the learner moves to the next exactly where the one it holds is refuted. | \| lasso (套索) \| A sequence that repeats periodically from some step on: exactly the registration of a world a list holds. On it the lasso learner's refutations are bounded, | \| lasso (套索) \| A sequence that repeats periodically from some step on: exactly the course, through a map, of a world a list holds. On it the lasso extrapolation's misses are bounded, | L-miss |
| \| diagonal course (对角序列) \| A sequence that at every step answers the learner's claim so as to refute it. It is the registration of a law that keeps the record, and so is open. | \| diagonal course (对角序列) \| A sequence that at every step answers the extrapolation's proposed value so that it misses. It is the course of a law that keeps the whole prefix, and so is open. | L-miss |
| \| merged enumeration, at the meta level (合并枚举) \| Classes of hypotheses merged along pairs | \| merged enumeration, at the meta level (合并枚举) \| Classes of candidates merged along pairs | L-elim |
| \| subthreshold (阈下) \| A condition depended on but not observed is registered only as whether the requirement is met. Where the rest responds through registration, | \| responsive reach (回应所及) \| The part's closure, between two flows, under being affected through what couples in: a member exchanges in one of the two flows with a condition coupled into another formation, which exchanges with one coupled into a third, and so on link by link. Dual to the coupling closure; where coupling is by threshold only and there is one flow, it is exactly the union of the members' reach closures. Where the rest responds through its couplings, | S2 |

| **识与检验（第一部分）。** 一个形成识它的支持（水平是否接纳它）与它所观察之条件的水平（Becoming.Testing <`../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Testing.lean`>）。 | **耦合与检验（第一部分）。** 耦合入一个形成的，有它的支持（水平是否接纳它），经阈，与经水平耦合入它之条件的水平（Becoming.Testing <`../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Testing.lean`>）。“耦合入”取代先前的“识”（英文作 registers）；依 10 月 18 日的命名决定，识只用于一个形成经其耦合所成之归并的自身历程。 | S2 |
| 经依存，识是一个阈值：依存而不观察的条件只以是否满足所需被识（`Web.alike_of_threshold`）；经观察，识其水平（`Web.observed_level_couples`） | 只经阈耦合入的条件只传递它是否满足所需（`Web.alike_of_threshold`）；经水平耦合入时，传递其水平（`Web.observed_level_couples`） | S2 |
| 主张只经所识的差别被否证（`Web.Excludes.not_of_alike`）：主张与其对手必须在能被识之处不同，这是可否证性的结构 | 主张只经耦合入者的差别被排除（`Web.Excludes.not_of_alike`）：主张与其对手必须在有所耦合入之处不同；作者把这读作可否证性的结构 | S5 |

| 相对于模型的否证是对模型与主张一起的否证，错的模型否证成立的，识否证模型 | 相对于模型的排除是对模型与主张一起的排除：错的模型排除成立的，耦合入者排除模型 | S5 |

| \| 经识的选择（不加前提）：两段历史被每个经选择者之识的选择选得相同，恰当选择者识得相同，合起来的识亦然；其余交换固定时，选择与评分在识闭包之外不见，与闭包内者合起来也不见；缺口是所及闭包减去识闭包：清除者的选择不见栖居者的终结，栖居者之识与合起来的识选出它；伸出识闭包之外而抬高所识者的流被选中 | \| 经耦合的选择（不加前提）：两段历史被每个经选择者之耦合的选择选得相同，恰当它们经这些耦合无别，合起来的耦合亦然；其余交换固定时，选择与评分在耦合闭包之外不见，与闭包内者合起来也不见；缺口是所及闭包减去耦合闭包：清除者的选择不见栖居者的终结，栖居者的耦合与合起来的耦合选出它；伸出耦合闭包之外而抬高耦合入者的流被选中 | S3 |

| \| 候选者的所及闭包减去选择者的识闭包；`Clearing` 中其中的终结不被经选择者之识的选择选出，被栖居者之识与合起来的识选出；缺口只经依存、观察或他者之识合起来才合上 | \| 候选者的所及闭包减去选择者的耦合闭包；`Clearing` 中其中的终结不被经选择者之耦合的选择选出，被栖居者的耦合与合起来的耦合选出；缺口只经依存、经水平的耦合或他者的耦合合起来才合上 | S4 |

| \| 按否证学习 \| learning by refutation \| 假设依次排列，恰在所持者被否证时移向下一个；从不越过成立的假设，越过的每个都在记录中被否证 | \| 逐除外推 \| extrapolation by elimination \| 候选依次排列，恰在所持者不合时移向下一个；从不越过相合的候选，越过的每个都在前段中不合 | L-elim |

| the class; a kept history returns up to likeness where its present does and nothing registers   the past (`keepFrame_alike_iff`, `keepHistory_returns_alike`), so `keepHistory_new` and   `keepHistory_fixes_nothing_else` need the past registered (`keepHistory_new_named`); with   nothing claimed, every description carries a law and no confound is refuted   (`carries_unregistered`, `Confound.refutes_registered`), so `carries_law_iff`,   `Switching.no_state_law` and `Confound.varied_refutes` need the description registered. | the class; a kept history returns up to likeness where its present does and no claim tells   the past apart (`keepFrame_alike_iff`, `keepHistory_returns_alike`), so `keepHistory_new` and   `keepHistory_fixes_nothing_else` need the past claimed (`keepHistory_new_named`); with   nothing claimed, every description carries a law and no confound is excluded   (`carries_unregistered`, `Confound.refutes_registered`), so `carries_law_iff`,   `Switching.no_state_law` and `Confound.varied_refutes` need the description claimed. | S6 |
| those that need equality depend on   what is registered: an intervention on one name among alike configurations, a past nothing   registers, a description nothing registers. What nothing registers is neither fixed nor   refuted (compare the registers of `Becoming.Testing`). | those that need equality depend on   what is claimed: an intervention on one name among alike configurations, a past no claim tells   apart, a description no claim tells apart. What no claim tells apart is neither fixed nor   excluded (compare what couples into a formation in `Becoming.Testing`). | S6 (the AI's reading (D32)) |
| /-- **Where nothing registers the past, kept configurations | /-- **Where no claim tells the past apart, kept configurations | S6 |
| where its present does and nothing registers the past.** | where its present does and no claim tells the past apart.** | S6 |
| equality, or claims that register the past. -/ | equality, or claims that tell the past apart. -/ | S6 |
| /-! ### What nothing registers is not refuted -/ | /-! ### What no claim tells apart is not excluded -/ | S6 |
| description, or claims that register it; | description, or claims that tell it apart; | S6 |
| /-! ### A confound not registered is not refuted -/ | /-! ### A confound not claimed is not excluded -/ | S6 |
| /-- **Refuting a model needs the outcome registered.** | /-- **Excluding a model needs the outcome claimed.** | S6 |
| so `varied_refutes` fails up to likeness; with the outcome named by the claims, determinacy up to likeness is determinacy and the refutation stands. -/ | so `varied_refutes` fails up to likeness; with the outcome named by the claims, determinacy up to likeness is determinacy and the exclusion stands. -/ | S5 |

## `lean:Context`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| # Contextual worlds: claims registered in context  A `Frame` fixes whether each claim is realized by the configuration alone: what is registered of a claim does not depend on what else is registered with it. This module states that as a commitment of the representation and what follows where it fails, with no premise.  A claim is registered in a context (`Contextual`): `within k c` says that context `k` registers claim `c`, and `shows s k c` is what `k` registers of `c` at configuration `s`. Nothing is decided and no choice is made.  * **Gluing is agreement where contexts meet.** The registrations at a configuration glue, one   context-free reading agreeing with every context (`Contextual.Glues`), exactly where every   two contexts registering a claim register it alike (`Contextual.glues_iff_agree`, given a   context registering each claim). * **Context-free claims are a commitment.** Read with its context, every registration is a claim   of a frame (`Contextual.indexed`, `Contextual.indexed_fits`). A frame of context-free claims   fits exactly where the registrations glue at every configuration (`Contextual.fitsFree_iff`). * **Where the possibilities do not glue, no configuration does.** If what each context can show   depends only on the claims it registers (`Local`) and no context-free reading lies within every | # Contextual worlds: claims shown in context  A `Frame` fixes whether each claim is realized by the configuration alone: what is shown of a claim does not depend on what else is shown with it. This module states that as a commitment of the representation and what follows where it fails, with no premise.  A claim is shown in a context (`Contextual`): `within k c` says that claim `c` lies within context `k`, and `shows s k c` is what `k` shows of `c` at configuration `s`. Nothing is decided and no choice is made.  * **Gluing is agreement where contexts meet.** What the contexts show at a configuration glues,   one context-free reading agreeing with every context (`Contextual.Glues`), exactly where every   two contexts a claim lies within show it alike (`Contextual.glues_iff_agree`, given a   context each claim lies within). * **Context-free claims are a commitment.** Read with its context, everything a context shows is   a claim of a frame (`Contextual.indexed`, `Contextual.indexed_fits`). A frame of context-free   claims fits exactly where what the contexts show glues at every configuration   (`Contextual.fitsFree_iff`). * **Where the possibilities do not glue, no configuration does.** If what each context can show   depends only on the claims within it (`Local`) and no context-free reading lies within every | S7 (a claim lies within a context and the context shows a value of it (`within`, `shows`)) |
| * **The Mermin–Peres square** (Mermin 1990, Peres 1990). Nine claims in three rows and three columns, each line   registering three; a row's three values have even parity, as do the first two columns', and | * **The Mermin–Peres square** (Mermin 1990, Peres 1990). Nine claims in three rows and three columns, each line   holding three; a row's three values have even parity, as do the first two columns', and | S7 |
|   (`Square.noGlobal`). Two-qubit observables realize the square, so quantum registrations are   of this kind: `Becoming.Quantum` (P72q), which imports this module, checks it |   (`Square.noGlobal`). Two-qubit observables realize the square, so what quantum measurements   show is of this kind: `Becoming.Quantum` (P72q), which imports this module, checks it | S7 |
| So a frame of context-free claims is a commitment of the representation: it holds of a world exactly where its registrations glue, and some worlds' registrations cannot glue at any configuration. Claims indexed by the context in which they are registered always fit, at the cost that a claim registered in two contexts is two claims. Readings of this as wave and particle, as the Kochen–Specker theorem, or as values being relations registered in context belong to the study, not to this module.  **Classical sources.** Reading a family of registrations in context as gluing to one context-free | So a frame of context-free claims is a commitment of the representation: it holds of a world exactly where what its contexts show glues, and in some worlds it cannot glue at any configuration. Claims indexed by the context in which they are shown always fit, at the cost that a claim shown in two contexts is two claims. Readings of this as wave and particle, as the Kochen–Specker theorem, or as values being relations shown in context belong to the study, not to this module.  **Classical sources.** Reading a family of values shown in context as gluing to one context-free | S7 |

## `lean:Diagonal`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| # A registration within what it registers  What a world registers of itself is registered by parts of it. Here a registration is a family `ψ : A → A → B`: the member `a` registers `ψ a x` of each `x`, and the members are among what is registered. `same` says when two registered values are alike: equality, or for claims `Iff`. Nothing about the members or the values is assumed, and nothing is decided except where a law names it.  * **The gap.** Moving each member's registration of itself by a map `f` that leaves no value   alike with its image gives a reading no member registers (`gap_unsaid`). This is Lawvere's   diagonal argument; `RootCoverageBoundary`, outside the core, proves it for functions and   predicates. A registration that registers every reading gives every map a value alike with   its image (`fixed_of_complete`). | # A family of readings within what it reads  What a world reads of itself is read by parts of it. Here the readings are a family `ψ : A → A → B`: the member `a` takes the value `ψ a x` at each `x`, and the members are among what is read. `same` says when two values are alike: equality, or for claims `Iff`. Nothing about the members or the values is assumed, and nothing is decided except where a law names it.  * **The gap.** Moving each member's value at itself by a map `f` that leaves no value   alike with its image gives a reading no member says (`gap_unsaid`). This is Lawvere's   diagonal argument; `RootCoverageBoundary`, outside the core, proves it for functions and   predicates. A family that says every reading gives every map a value alike with   its image (`fixed_of_complete`). | S7 (a member says a reading (`Says`): its values are alike with the reading's at every point) |
| compared by equality, a registration within | compared by equality, a family within | S7 |
| * **Completion is an unfolding.** A registration that keeps what it registered and next   registers what it missed (`Unfolds`) is incomplete at every step (`Unfolds.incomplete`).   Each step's gap is registered at no step up to its own (`Unfolds.gap_new`), no two gaps are   alike (`Unfolds.gaps_differ`), and the registration never returns: every later step   registers something no earlier step did (`Unfolds.never_returns`). No finite stock of | * **Completion is an unfolding.** A family that keeps what it said and next says what it missed   (`Unfolds`) is incomplete at every step (`Unfolds.incomplete`).   Each step's gap is said at no step up to its own (`Unfolds.gap_new`), no two gaps are   alike (`Unfolds.gaps_differ`), and the family never returns: every later step   says something no earlier step did (`Unfolds.never_returns`). No finite stock of | S7 |
|   contradiction; `Becoming.Description` reads the same rhythm off its registrations as two   states, each defined through the other (`Negation.two_states`).  So, with no premise: a registration within what it registers is complete exactly where there is nothing to tell apart. Where there is, what it misses is definite, and the next step can register it, but that step misses something new; completing it is an unfolding that never |   contradiction; `Becoming.Description` reads the same rhythm off its course as two   states, each defined through the other (`Negation.two_states`).  So, with no premise: a family of readings within what it reads is complete exactly where there is nothing to tell apart. Where there is, what it misses is definite, and the next step can say it, but that step misses something new; completing it is an unfolding that never | S7 |

## `lean:Runaway`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| # Runaway: what a loop through its own registration cannot register  On 11 October 2026 Ren asked for "wisdoms to avoid run away feedbacks". `Becoming.Testing` states threshold blindness for an exchange that follows a formation's registration (`Flow.ExchangeFollows`), and reads a runaway as a loop running inside it. This module states, with no premise beyond balance on the conditions registered, what that means for every policy and how far the blindness reaches. Ren's reply to the proposal was "no extra review needed, merge, then continue".  * **Every policy through registration.** A formation's policy goes through its registration   when its own exchange on what it registers is alike wherever it has registered alike   (`Flow.PolicyThrough`). With the rest of the exchange on what it registers fixed   (`Flow.OthersFixed`), the exchange then follows its registration   (`Flow.exchangeFollows_of_policy`). So for every such policy, a difference in a stock the   formation depends on and does not observe is never used while the lower web supports it   (`Flow.policy_keeps_difference`), and wherever the difference has been used, the lower web   failed to support the formation at some earlier step (`Flow.policy_uses_after_failure`). * **The distance to the break is not registered.** Raising a stock by `d` (`Web.raise`) keeps   balance on every condition (`Flow.BalancesOn.raise`). At every step at which a web supports   a formation that does not observe the stock, the raised web registers alike for it   (`Web.alikeThrough_raise`). So before the break nothing the formation registers bounds how   near the break is: for every horizon, where the stock is the only requirement that may be   unmet up to it, some raised web registers alike at every step at which the actual web   supports the formation, and supports it at every step up to the horizon   (`Web.break_not_bounded`); the raised web is dependent where the web is   (`Web.Dependent.raise`). `SupportDrawn.break_uncoupled` is an instance. * **Observation registers it.** Observing the stock, a formation registers any raise at once   (`Web.observed_raise_couples`).  **Readings (the AI's, adopted on Ren's delegation of 15 October 2026, DELEGATED-CHOICES D26).** - A runaway is a loop whose registration cannot register the distance to its break: before the   break, its own registration is the same as in a world with as much more stock as one likes.   Its policy, however clever, cannot use what it does not register. - To avoid a runaway without forgoing what is there, a formation needs what registers the   stock (measurement), or a model of it (理, a model answerable to registration), or another   formation whose registration does (`Becoming.Testing`); experiment needs a return and a model   of what is irrelevant (`Becoming.Experiment`). | # Runaway: what does not couple into a loop through its own couplings  On 11 October 2026 Ren asked for "wisdoms to avoid run away feedbacks". `Becoming.Testing` states threshold blindness for an exchange that follows what couples into a formation (`Flow.ExchangeFollows`), and reads a runaway as a loop running inside it. This module states, with no premise beyond balance on the conditions coupled in, what that means for every policy and how far the blindness reaches. Ren's reply to the proposal was "no extra review needed, merge, then continue".  * **Every policy through the couplings.** A formation's policy goes through its couplings   when its own exchange on what couples into it is alike wherever the flows have been alike   through its couplings (`Flow.PolicyThrough`). With the rest of the exchange on what couples   into it fixed (`Flow.OthersFixed`), the exchange then follows what couples in   (`Flow.exchangeFollows_of_policy`). So for every such policy, a difference in a stock coupled   into the formation by threshold only is never used while the lower web supports it   (`Flow.policy_keeps_difference`), and wherever the difference has been used, the lower web   failed to support the formation at some earlier step (`Flow.policy_uses_after_failure`). * **The distance to the break does not couple in.** Raising a stock by `d` (`Web.raise`) keeps   balance on every condition (`Flow.BalancesOn.raise`). At every step at which a web supports   a formation into which the stock couples by threshold only, the raised web is alike through   the formation's couplings (`Web.alikeThrough_raise`). So before the break nothing that couples   into the formation bounds how near the break is: for every horizon, where the stock is the only   requirement that may be unmet up to it, some raised web is alike through its couplings at every   step at which the actual web supports the formation, and supports it at every step up to the   horizon (`Web.break_not_bounded`); the raised web is dependent where the web is   (`Web.Dependent.raise`). `SupportDrawn.break_uncoupled` is an instance. * **Coupling by level passes it.** With the stock coupled into a formation by level, any raise   passes at once (`Web.observed_raise_couples`).  **Readings (the AI's, adopted on Ren's delegation of 15 October 2026, DELEGATED-CHOICES D26).** - A runaway is a loop into which the distance to its break does not couple: before the break,   what couples into it is the same as in a world with as much more stock as one likes. Its   policy, however clever, cannot use what does not couple into it. - To avoid a runaway without forgoing what is there, a formation needs the stock coupled into it   by level (measurement), or a model of it (理, a model answerable to what couples in), or another   formation into which the stock couples (`Becoming.Testing`); experiment needs a return and a   model of what is irrelevant (`Becoming.Experiment`). | S3+S2 |
| /-! ## Every policy through registration -/ | /-! ## Every policy through the couplings -/ | S2 |
| /-- `g`'s policy goes through its registration: wherever `g` has registered alike up to `t`, its own take from and give to every condition it registers are alike across the step from `t`. -/ | /-- `g`'s policy goes through its couplings: wherever the flows have been alike through `g`'s couplings up to `t`, its own take from and give to every condition coupled into it are alike across the step from `t`. -/ | S3+S2 |
| /-- The rest of the exchange on what `g` registers is fixed: every other formation takes from and gives to every condition `g` registers alike in the two flows, at every step. -/ | /-- The rest of the exchange on what couples into `g` is fixed: every other formation takes from and gives to every condition coupled into `g` alike in the two flows, at every step. -/ | S2 |
| /-- **A policy through registration, with the rest fixed, is exchange following registration.** -/ | /-- **A policy through the couplings, with the rest fixed, is exchange following what couples in.** -/ | S2 |
| /-- **A policy that keeps its support never uses a larger stock.** For every policy through registration, with the rest fixed: where the starts differ only in a stock `g` depends on and does not observe, `d` higher in the second, and the first web supports `g` at every step, the stock stands `d` higher at every step and `g` registers alike. -/ | /-- **A policy that keeps its support never uses a larger stock.** For every policy through the couplings, with the rest fixed: where the starts differ only in a stock coupled into `g` by threshold only, `d` higher in the second, and the first web supports `g` at every step, the stock stands `d` higher at every step and the flows are alike through `g`'s couplings. -/ | S3+S2 |
| /-- **A larger stock is used only after a failure.** For every policy through registration, with the rest fixed, starting at least as high on what `g` registers and alike on what it observes: wherever a difference on a condition `g` registers has changed by a step, the first web did not support `g` at every earlier step. -/ | /-- **A larger stock is used only after a failure.** For every policy through the couplings, with the rest fixed, starting at least as high on what couples into `g` and alike on what couples into it by level: wherever a difference on a condition coupled into `g` has changed by a step, the first web did not support `g` at every earlier step. -/ | S2 |
| /-! ## The distance to the break is not registered -/ | /-! ## The distance to the break does not couple in -/ | S2 |
| /-- **Before the break, a raise does not register.** At every step at which the web supports `g`, a raise of a stock `g` does not observe registers alike for it. -/ | /-- **Before the break, a raise does not pass.** At every step at which the web supports `g`, a raise of a stock not coupled into `g` by level leaves the webs alike through `g`'s couplings. -/ | S3+S2 |
| /-- **Observation registers a raise at once.** Observing the stock, any positive raise is registered at every step. -/ | /-- **Coupling by level passes a raise at once.** With the stock coupled into `g` by level, any positive raise sets the webs apart through `g`'s couplings at every step. -/ | S2 |
| /-- **No registered bound on the break.** Let `g` not observe a stock `c`, and let `c` be the only requirement of `g` that may be unmet at the steps up to a horizon `u`. Then some raise of `c` registers alike for `g` at every step at which the web supports `g`, and supports `g` at every step up to `u`. So before the break nothing `g` registers bounds how far off it is. -/ | /-- **No bound on the break couples in.** Let a stock `c` not couple into `g` by level, and let `c` be the only requirement of `g` that may be unmet at the steps up to a horizon `u`. Then some raise of `c` is alike through `g`'s couplings at every step at which the web supports `g`, and supports `g` at every step up to `u`. So before the break nothing that couples into `g` bounds how far off it is. -/ | S3+S2 |
| /-- **The break, unregistered.** In the lower web the formation is unsupported at the third step; a raise of its stock registers alike for it at every step at which the lower web supports it, supports it at the third step, and keeps the flow balanced. -/ | /-- **The break, uncoupled.** In the lower web the formation is unsupported at the third step; a raise of its stock is alike through its couplings at every step at which the lower web supports it, supports it at the third step, and keeps the flow balanced. -/ | S3+S2 |

Completion is a process: its configurations are registrations `A → A → B`, and a step keeps every reading registered and registers the gap of the one before | # Diagonal runs: completing a family of readings within what it reads, as a process  `Becoming.Diagonal` states that a family of readings within what it reads misses a definite reading, that a family keeping what it said and next saying what it missed (`Diagonal.Unfolds`) never closes and never returns, and that revising a value by a map moving every value never rests. Completion is a process: its configurations are families `A → A → B`, and a step keeps every reading said and says the gap of the one before | S7 |

| finite record, and naming the classes of the split that makes sense without an axiom. `Becoming.Description` answers the first: every registered history is a law read through a registration, and its states are read off it (`alike_moments_iff`, `finitely_many_states_iff`). `Becoming.Learning` shows that no record fixes the next value, not even among worlds a list holds; that a learner by refutation that is not given how many configurations there are comes to rest is unrefuted (`held_rest_unrefuted`), and its record never shows that it has: given two values, every claim it makes is refuted, at that step, on a course of a world held by a list sharing its record (`no_prefix_fixes_next_finite`). This module states what the situation adds: a bound on its states. With no premise beyond the bound: | finite prefix, and naming the classes of the split that makes sense without an axiom. `Becoming.Description` answers the first: every history read through a map is a law read through a map, and its states are read off it (`alike_moments_iff`, `finitely_many_states_iff`). `Becoming.Learning` shows that no prefix fixes the next value, not even among worlds a list holds; that the extrapolation by elimination, not given the number of configurations, comes to rest is unrefuted (`held_rest_unrefuted`), and its prefix never shows that it has: given two values, every value it proposes misses, at that step, on a course of a world held by a list sharing its prefix (`no_prefix_fixes_next_finite`). This module states what the situation adds: a bound on its states. With no premise beyond the bound: | S1+L-prefix+L-extrap+L-miss+L-elim |
| * **A bounded record fixes the course.** A history has at most `N` states exactly where it | * **A bounded prefix fixes the course.** A history has at most `N` states exactly where it | L-prefix |
|   `N + N' - 1` agree at every step (`bounded_agree`): their records before `N + N' - 1` fix   them (`prefix_fixes`). Before `N + N' - 2` they do not: for every `T` a history with one   state and one with `T + 1` share their record before `T` and differ at `T`   (`prefix_short`), for every `N ≥ 2` two histories with at most `N` states share their   record before `N + N - 2` and differ there (`prefix_short_twice`), and for every `N` and `N'`   a history with at most `N` states and one with at most `N'` share their record before   `N + N' - 2` and differ there (`bounded_agree_exact`): the length `N + N' - 1` is exact for   every pair of bounds. Records are equal exactly where the courses agree before their length   (`prefix_eq_iff`). * **Induction is deduction where the bound is given.** On a history with at most `N` states, a   lasso hypothesis within `N` that the record before `N + N - 1` bears out is the law of the   whole course (`lasso_of_fits`, `bounded_induction`), and one always is   (`bounded_hypothesis`). The learner given `N` (`withBound`), silent before step   `N + N - 1` and from then on claiming by the first hypothesis within `N` that the record bears   out, is refuted at no step of any history with at most `N` states and is right at every step   from `N + N - 1` on (`withBound_accords`, `withBound_exact`). No learner refuted at no step of every   history with at most `N ≥ 2` states claims at step `N + N - 2` on each of them   (`withBound_earliest`): the learner given the bound speaks as early as any can. The   two-valued negation, on which no fixed value is borne out (`Diagonal.revision_never_rests`), is   derived from step `3` on by the learner given two states (`negation_derived`). * **The learner not given the bound rests within it.** A lasso hypothesis within `N`   refuted at no step of a stretch of `N + N - 1` steps is refuted at no later step   (`no_miss_persists`). So on a history with at most `N` states the learner by refutation   over the lasso hypotheses, given neither `N` nor the course, claims at every step from   `N + (N + N - 1) * tri N` on and is right (`byElimination_rests`); in a world a list holds, `N` is   the list's length (`held_byElimination_rests`), and on a lasso course the rest that   `Becoming.Learning` leaves unrefuted is found (`rests_of_lasso`). The step is a function of   the bound, which the learner does not have: its record does not show it. The step is cubic in   `N`: on the two-valued negation along a cycle of `N = 2m + 3` places, which is broken once in   every turn, the learner is refuted at step `(N³ - N) / 4` (`byElimination_missed_late`), each even   shift it passes costing a whole turn, so no earlier step bounds its rest on every history with   at most `N` states (`byElimination_rest_cubic`). * **The rest step needs no bound.** The learner by the least bound (`leastBound`), given no   bound, claims by the first lasso hypothesis the record bears out within the least bound within   which one is; it is right at every step from `N + N - 1` on of every history with at most `N`   states, for every `N` (`leastBound_exact`). No learner, given the bound or not, is right at step   `N + N - 2` on every such history for `N ≥ 2` (`rest_earliest`), so `N + N - 1` is the optimal   rest step and being given the bound does not move it. What the bound buys is silence: no learner   right at step `1` on every history with one state is refuted at no step of every history with   two states (`rest_without_bound_missed`), while the learner given the bound is silent before   `N + N - 1` and refuted nowhere (`withBound_accords`). * **A world answering its own learner exceeds the bound.** The course that answers every claim   of the learner given `N` has more than `N` states (`answer_exceeds_bound`): a world that   includes the learner and answers it is not within the bound the learner is given. |   `N + N' - 1` agree at every step (`bounded_agree`): their prefixes before `N + N' - 1` fix   them (`prefix_fixes`). Before `N + N' - 2` they do not: for every `T` a history with one   state and one with `T + 1` share their prefix before `T` and differ at `T`   (`prefix_short`), for every `N ≥ 2` two histories with at most `N` states share their   prefix before `N + N - 2` and differ there (`prefix_short_twice`), and for every `N` and `N'`   a history with at most `N` states and one with at most `N'` share their prefix before   `N + N' - 2` and differ there (`bounded_agree_exact`): the length `N + N' - 1` is exact for   every pair of bounds. Prefixes are equal exactly where the courses agree before their length   (`prefix_eq_iff`). * **Induction is deduction where the bound is given.** On a history with at most `N` states, a   lasso candidate within `N` that the prefix before `N + N - 1` bears out is the law of the   whole course (`lasso_of_fits`, `bounded_induction`), and one always is   (`bounded_hypothesis`). The extrapolation given the bound `N` (`withBound`), silent before step   `N + N - 1` and from then on proposing by the first candidate within `N` that the prefix bears   out, misses at no step of any history with at most `N` states and is right at every step   from `N + N - 1` on (`withBound_accords`, `withBound_exact`). No extrapolation that misses at no   step of every history with at most `N ≥ 2` states proposes at step `N + N - 2` on each of them   (`withBound_earliest`): the extrapolation given the bound proposes as early as any can. The   two-valued negation, on which no fixed value is borne out (`Diagonal.revision_never_rests`), is   derived from step `3` on by the extrapolation given two states (`negation_derived`). * **Elimination not given the bound rests within it.** A lasso candidate within `N` that misses   at no step of a stretch of `N + N - 1` steps misses at no later step   (`no_miss_persists`). So on a history with at most `N` states the extrapolation by elimination   over the lasso candidates, given neither `N` nor the course, proposes at every step from   `N + (N + N - 1) * tri N` on and is right (`byElimination_rests`); in a world a list holds, `N` is   the list's length (`held_byElimination_rests`), and on a lasso course the rest that   `Becoming.Learning` leaves unrefuted is found (`rests_of_lasso`). The step is a function of   the bound, which the extrapolation is not given: its prefix does not show it. The step is   cubic in `N`: on the two-valued negation along a cycle of `N = 2m + 3` places, which is broken   once in every turn, elimination misses at step `(N³ - N) / 4` (`byElimination_missed_late`), each   even shift it passes costing a whole turn, so no earlier step bounds its rest on every history   with at most `N` states (`byElimination_rest_cubic`). * **The rest step needs no bound.** The extrapolation by the least bound (`leastBound`), given   no bound, proposes by the first lasso candidate the prefix bears out within the least   bound within which one is; it is right at every step from `N + N - 1` on of every history with   at most `N` states, for every `N` (`leastBound_exact`). No extrapolation, given the bound or   without, is right at step `N + N - 2` on every such history for `N ≥ 2` (`rest_earliest`), so   `N + N - 1` is the optimal rest step and being given the bound does not move it. What the bound buys is   silence: no extrapolation right at step `1` on every history with one state misses at no step   of every history with two states (`rest_without_bound_missed`), while the extrapolation with the   bound is silent before `N + N - 1` and misses nowhere (`withBound_accords`). * **The diagonal world of an extrapolation exceeds its bound.** The course on which every value   the extrapolation given the bound `N` proposes misses has more than `N` states   (`answer_exceeds_bound`): a world that contains the extrapolation and moves off every value it   proposes is not within the bound the extrapolation is given. | L-prefix+L-extrap+L-miss+L-elim |

| premise. The learners here are learners in the limit in Gold's sense (Gold 1967, identification in the limit): the learner by refutation over the lasso hypotheses is identification by enumeration, and the learner given the bound (`withBound`) and the learner by the least bound (`leastBound`) identify every history with at most `N` states from step `N + N - 1` on, which is the Moore and Fine–Wilf bound read as a learning bound. The cubic step of the learner by refutation (`byElimination_rest_cubic`) is stated for the diagonal order in which `Learning.lassos` enumerates the hypotheses; another order is not covered. | premise. The extrapolations here are what Gold calls learners in the limit (Gold 1967, identification in the limit): elimination over the lasso candidates is his identification by enumeration, and the extrapolation given the bound (`withBound`) and the extrapolation by the least bound (`leastBound`) fix every history with at most `N` states from step `N + N - 1` on, which is the Moore and Fine–Wilf bound read as a learning bound. The cubic step of elimination (`byElimination_rest_cubic`) is stated for the diagonal order in which `Learning.lassos` enumerates the candidates; another order is not covered. | L-extrap+L-elim+L-fix |

| /-- **The states of a bounded history are read off a finite record.** Two moments of a history with at most `N` states are one state exactly where what is registered from them agrees for `N + N - 1` steps. -/ | /-- **The states of a bounded history are read off a finite prefix.** Two moments of a history with at most `N` states are one state exactly where the courses from them agree for `N + N - 1` steps. -/ | L-prefix |
| /-- **With the bound given, a hypothesis the record bears out is the law of the course.** On a history with at most `N` states, a lasso `(i, q)` within `N` that holds on the record before `N + N - 1` holds at every step. The lasso world reading the record is a second history within `N` agreeing with the first before `N + N - 1`. -/ | /-- **With the bound given, a candidate the prefix bears out is the law of the course.** On a history with at most `N` states, a lasso `(i, q)` within `N` that accords with the prefix before `N + N - 1` accords at every step. The lasso world read off the prefix is a second history within `N` agreeing with the first before `N + N - 1`. -/ | L-prefix+L-miss+L-elim |
| /-- A lasso hypothesis refuted at no step before `T` holds on the record before `T`. -/ | /-- A lasso candidate that misses at no step before `T` accords with the prefix before `T`. -/ | L-prefix+L-miss+L-elim |
| lasso hypothesis within `N` refuted at no step before `N + N - 1` is refuted at no step. -/ | lasso candidate within `N` that misses at no step before `N + N - 1` misses at no step. -/ | L-miss+L-elim |
| /-- On a history with at most `N` states some lasso hypothesis within `N` holds. -/ | /-- On a history with at most `N` states some lasso candidate within `N` accords. -/ | L-miss+L-elim |

| /-- The claim made by a hypothesis, if one is held. -/ | /-- The value proposed by a candidate, if one is found. -/ | L-extrap+L-elim |

| /-- **The liar's truth is derived.** The learner given that there are two states is right about the two-valued negation at every step from `3` on: what no fixed value answers (`Diagonal.revision_never_rests`) is a rhythm read off the record. -/ | /-- **The liar's truth is derived.** The extrapolation given two states is right about the two-valued negation at every step from `3` on: what no fixed value accords with (`Diagonal.revision_never_rests`) is a rhythm read off the prefix. -/ | L-prefix+L-extrap |

| /-- **A lasso hypothesis unrefuted for `N + N - 1` steps is refuted at no later step.** On a history with at most `N` states, a lasso `(a, b)` within `N` refuted at no step from `s ≥ a` to before `s + (N + N - 1)` is refuted at no step from `s` on: the stretch from `s` repeats with period `b + 1` on its record before `N + N - 1`, so at every step (`lasso_of_fits`). -/ | /-- **A lasso candidate without a miss for `N + N - 1` steps misses at no later step.** On a history with at most `N` states, a lasso `(a, b)` within `N` that misses at no step from `s ≥ a` to before `s + (N + N - 1)` misses at no step from `s` on: the stretch from `s` repeats with period `b + 1` on its prefix before `N + N - 1`, so at every step (`lasso_of_fits`). -/ | L-prefix+L-miss+L-elim |
| /-- **The learner not given the bound comes to rest within it.** On a history with at most `N` states, the learner by refutation over the lasso hypotheses, given neither `N` nor the course (`Becoming.Learning`'s `learner lassos`), claims at every step from `N + (N + N - 1) * tri N` on and is right. Its hypotheses all lie within `N`; in each stretch of `N + N - 1` steps from `N` on it is refuted, or the hypothesis it holds is never refuted again (`no_miss_persists`); it is refuted fewer than `tri N` times. Its record does not show the step (`Becoming.Learning`'s `no_prefix_fixes_next_finite`); the bound does. -/ | /-- **Elimination not given the bound comes to rest within it.** On a history with at most `N` states, the extrapolation by elimination over the lasso candidates, given neither `N` nor the course (`Becoming.Learning`'s `byElimination lassos`), proposes at every step from `N + (N + N - 1) * tri N` on and is right. Its candidates all lie within `N`; in each stretch of `N + N - 1` steps from `N` on it misses, or its current candidate never misses again (`no_miss_persists`); it misses fewer than `tri N` times. Its prefix does not show the step (`Becoming.Learning`'s `no_prefix_fixes_next_finite`); the bound does. -/ | L-prefix+L-extrap+L-miss+L-elim |

## `lean:Keeping`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| # Keeping: a guard kept through what is registered  `Becoming.Viability` states where no course leaves a guard: every course is counted, whatever moves it takes. Here the moves are not all alike. A move is usable from a class of configurations where it is allowed and enabled at each of them, and after it what the step registers narrows the class. This module states, for every process, every registration of its steps, every guard and every condition on moves, when a class is kept for `n` steps, and how that keeping is carried by plans whose later moves follow what was registered.  * **Kept for `n` steps.** A class is kept for no steps where the guard holds throughout it, and   for `n + 1` steps where, besides, some move usable from it leaves, whatever the step   registers, a class kept for `n` steps (`KeptFor`). The class after a move and a registration   is what can stand after the move from the class where the step registers it (`Seen`); where   the registrations are listed (`Fin k`), it is `InquirySufficiency.After` (`seen_eq_after`). * **Plans.** A plan of `n` moves gives a first move and, for each registration of its step, a   plan of `n - 1` moves (`Plan`); it keeps the guard from a class where the guard holds there,   its first move is usable and each continuation keeps the guard from the class seen   (`Plan.Keeps`). A plan that keeps the guard keeps the class for its length, with no premise   (`Plan.Keeps.keptFor`). Conversely, a kept class has a plan that keeps it wherever the   registrations have choice into plans (`KeptFor.plan_of_choice`,   `keptFor_iff_plan_of_choice`). Listed registrations have it with no premise   (`choiceOver_fin`), so there a class is kept exactly where some plan keeps it   (`keptFor_iff_plan`). * **The boundary.** For arbitrary registrations the converse is exactly choice: kept classes   have plans in every process exactly where every relation total on the registrations has a   choice function (`plans_iff_choice`). A relation is answered by a two-step keeping problem   whose second move must be related to what the first step registered (`Answering`). Choice   is a hypothesis of the law where it is needed, never assumed. * **Order.** A narrower class is kept wherever a wider one is (`KeptFor.mono`), a longer   horizon keeps every shorter one (`KeptFor.shorter`), and a finer registration keeps what a   coarser one keeps, the moves unchanged (`KeptFor.finer`). The empty class is kept for every   horizon once there is a move (`keptFor_empty`). * **Classes kept for every horizon.** A family of classes within the guard, each with a usable   move leading, whatever is registered, into the family, keeps each of its classes for every | # Keeping: a guard kept through what the steps keep  `Becoming.Viability` states where no course leaves a guard: every course is counted, whatever moves it takes. Here the moves are not all alike. A move is usable from a class of configurations where it is allowed and enabled at each of them, and after it the value the step keeps (`sense`, a map of steps) narrows the class. This module states, for every process, every map of its steps to the values they keep, every guard and every condition on moves, when a class is kept for `n` steps, and how that keeping is carried by plans whose later moves follow the values kept.  * **Kept for `n` steps.** A class is kept for no steps where the guard holds throughout it, and   for `n + 1` steps where, besides, some move usable from it leaves, whatever value the step   keeps, a class kept for `n` steps (`KeptFor`). The class after a move and a value kept is what   can stand after the move from the class where the step keeps that value (`Seen`); where the   values kept are listed (`Fin k`), it is `InquirySufficiency.After` (`seen_eq_after`). * **Plans.** A plan of `n` moves gives a first move and, for each value its step keeps, a   plan of `n - 1` moves (`Plan`); it keeps the guard from a class where the guard holds there,   its first move is usable and each continuation keeps the guard from the class seen   (`Plan.Keeps`). A plan that keeps the guard keeps the class for its length, with no premise   (`Plan.Keeps.keptFor`). Conversely, a kept class has a plan that keeps it wherever the   values kept have choice into plans (`KeptFor.plan_of_choice`,   `keptFor_iff_plan_of_choice`). Listed values have it with no premise   (`choiceOver_fin`), so there a class is kept exactly where some plan keeps it   (`keptFor_iff_plan`). * **The boundary.** For arbitrary values kept the converse is exactly choice: kept classes   have plans in every process exactly where every relation total on the values has a   choice function (`plans_iff_choice`). A relation is answered by a two-step keeping problem   whose second move must be related to the value the first step kept (`Answering`). Choice   is a hypothesis of the law where it is needed, never assumed. * **Order.** A narrower class is kept wherever a wider one is (`KeptFor.mono`), a longer   horizon keeps every shorter one (`KeptFor.shorter`), and a finer map of the steps keeps what a   coarser one keeps, the moves unchanged (`KeptFor.finer`). The empty class is kept for every   horizon once there is a move (`keptFor_empty`). * **Classes kept for every horizon.** A family of classes within the guard, each with a usable   move leading, whatever value is kept, into the family, keeps each of its classes for every | S1+S7 |
| * **What is registered matters.** In `Matching`, a hidden bit must be matched by the second   move. Registering the bit, the starts are kept for two steps; registering nothing, they are   not (`Matching.keeping_matters`). | * **What a step keeps matters.** In `Matching`, a hidden bit must be matched by the second   move. Where the step keeps the bit, the starts are kept for two steps; where it keeps nothing,   they are not (`Matching.keeping_matters`). | S7 |
|   plan keeps both guards, each part registering its own step (`Plan.zip_keeps`); and every |   plan keeps both guards, each part keeping the value of its own step (`Plan.zip_keeps`); and every | TEXT |

| /-- **A finer registration keeps what a coarser one keeps**, the moves unchanged: a registration read off the finer one by `forget`. -/ | /-- **A finer map of the steps keeps what a coarser one keeps**, the moves unchanged: the coarser read off the finer one by `forget`. -/ | S1 |

| * **Responsive reach.** A formation lies within a part's responsive reach between two flows when it   lies within a member's closure under affecting through what is registered, in either flow: a   member exchanges with a condition another formation registers, that formation exchanges with a   condition a third registers, and so on (`Flow.WithinResponse`). Every member lies within it   (`Flow.member_within_response`). A formation lies within it exactly when some member lies within   the formation's registration closure drawn through both flows, the closure of   `Flow.alike_within_coupling` (`Flow.withinResponse_iff_coupling`, through   `WithinClosure.converse`); with one flow, within its registration closure   (`Flow.withinResponse_self_iff`). It holds what the part reaches and each member's reach closure   of P42 (`Flow.withinResponse_of_reaches`, `Flow.withinResponse_of_withinReach`); registering   support only, with one flow, it is the union of the members' reach closures   (`Flow.withinResponse_thresholdOnly_iff`). The reach closure is the converse of the influence   closure (`Flow.withinReach_iff_withinInfluence`). * **Responding through registration.** The rest responds through its registration when each   formation outside the part exchanges alike in the two flows across every step up to which it has   registered alike (`Flow.RespondsThroughCoupling`). It is a supplied premise. With the rest   of the exchange fixed it holds whatever registers (`Flow.respondsThroughCoupling_withhold`). * **Confinement.** The formations beyond the responsive reach form a region closed in both flows   under being affected through what is registered, and hold no member   (`Flow.beyond_response_closed`, `Flow.outside_part_of_beyond_response`). So where the two flows   have the same formations present, balance on what those formations register and start alike   there, and the rest responds through its registration, every formation beyond the responsive   reach registers alike, exchanges alike, and meets alike totals on every condition it registers,   at every step (`Flow.alike_beyond_response`, an instance of `Flow.alike_within_closed`; | * **Responsive reach.** A formation lies within a part's responsive reach between two flows when it   lies within a member's closure under affecting through what couples in, in either flow: a   member exchanges with a condition coupled into another formation, that formation exchanges with   a condition coupled into a third, and so on (`Flow.WithinResponse`). Every member lies within it   (`Flow.member_within_response`). A formation lies within it exactly when some member lies within   the formation's coupling closure drawn through both flows, the closure of   `Flow.alike_within_coupling` (`Flow.withinResponse_iff_coupling`, through   `WithinClosure.converse`); with one flow, within its coupling closure   (`Flow.withinResponse_self_iff`). It holds what the part reaches and each member's reach closure   of P42 (`Flow.withinResponse_of_reaches`, `Flow.withinResponse_of_withinReach`); where coupling   is by threshold only, with one flow, it is the union of the members' reach closures   (`Flow.withinResponse_thresholdOnly_iff`). The reach closure is the converse of the influence   closure (`Flow.withinReach_iff_withinInfluence`). * **Responding through the couplings.** The rest responds through its couplings when each   formation outside the part exchanges alike in the two flows across every step up to which the   flows have been alike through its couplings (`Flow.RespondsThroughCoupling`). It is a supplied   premise. With the rest of the exchange fixed it holds whatever couples in   (`Flow.respondsThroughCoupling_withhold`). * **Confinement.** The formations beyond the responsive reach form a region closed in both flows   under being affected through what couples in, and hold no member   (`Flow.beyond_response_closed`, `Flow.outside_part_of_beyond_response`). So where the two flows   have the same formations present, balance on what couples into those formations and start alike   there, and the rest responds through its couplings, the flows are alike through the couplings   of every formation beyond the responsive reach, and it exchanges alike and meets alike totals on   every condition coupled into it, at every step (`Flow.alike_beyond_response`, an instance of   `Flow.alike_within_closed`; | S4+S3+S2 |

| Each formation requires its paired condition and observes it. -/ | Each formation requires its paired condition, which couples into it by level. -/ | S2 |

The first's act takes from the condition the second observes, and the second's response takes from the condition the first | they start alike, and the second responds through its couplings. The first's act takes from the condition coupled into the second by level, and the second's response takes from the condition the first | S2 |

## `lean:Learnable`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| # Learnable: what a learner can learn through its registration  A learner reads what a registration registers and claims something it does not register: a registration `reg : S → R` of configurations, a target `val : S → V`, and a learner `pred : List R → Option V` that claims at step `t` of the orbit from `s`, from the registered record before `t` (`Becoming.Learning`'s `record`). It rests on the target from step `T` where it claims the target at every later step (`RestsFrom`, `Rests`). `Becoming.Testing` shows that a formation can test only a claim that differs in what registers in it; `Becoming.Learning` that no record fixes the next value; `Becoming.Identification` that in a world a list holds, the learner over the lasso hypotheses comes to rest on what it registers within a bound. This module states, with no premise, what a registration lets a learner come to rest on.  * **Alike to the registration, learned alike.** Two configurations with the same registered   future give every learner the same record at every step (`prefixes_same`). So at a step where   their targets differ, a claim is right on at most one (`claim_not_right_on_both`); with the   targets decided it is refuted on one, while the two records stay equal at every step   (`missed_on_one`). A learner resting on both has targets that agree from some step on   (`agree_of_rests_both`); where they differ at arbitrarily late steps, no learner rests on   both (`not_rests_on_both`), already for two configurations the law leaves unchanged   (`still_not_rests_on_both`). * **What is read off the registration is learned by the learner given nothing.** Where the   target is read off what is registered (`val s = g (reg s)`), the learner over the lasso   hypotheses on the registered values, its claims read by `g`, rests on the target in every   world a list holds, from `Becoming.Identification`'s bound on (`rests_through`). It is given   neither the configurations, nor how many there are, nor any value of the target. * **Exactly.** In a world a list holds, some learner reading the registration rests on the   target at every configuration exactly where configurations with the same registered future   have targets that agree from some step on (`learnable_iff`). The learner of the second   direction is given the world and the target (`fromLaw`): no learner does better, whatever it   is given, since the first direction holds of every learner. Targets that go alike across the   registration (`Learning.Lumped`) are always learnable (`learnable_of_lumped`). Where the law   leaves every configuration unchanged, they are exactly the learnable ones   (`still_learnable_iff_lumped`): what can be learned through a registration is what goes alike   across it, the lumping by which `Becoming.Learning` states generalization. * **Two still configurations** (`Pair`). Registering the first of two coordinates, no learner   rests on the second at both `(true, false)` and `(true, true)` (`Pair.first_not_rests`), the   second not going alike across the first (`Pair.not_lumped`). Registering both, the learner over   the lasso hypotheses rests on the second at every configuration from step 2   (`Pair.whole_rests`).  Resting on the target from some step on is identification in the limit (Gold 1967), and `learnable_iff` is an observability condition in that setting: its first direction holds of every learner, while the learner of its second direction is given the world and the target, so that direction shows existence, not a learner that is given nothing.  Readings of this as registration bounding what is learned, as the limit of learning without feedback that registers the target, or as a condition on cognition, belong to the study, not to this module. No ethical predicate, ranking or axiom occurs. | # Learnable: what can be learned through a map  An extrapolation proposes, from values through a map, a value of a feature the map need not take: a map `reg : S → R` of configurations, a feature `val : S → V`, and an extrapolation `pred : List R → Option V` that proposes at step `t` of the orbit from `s`, from the prefix before `t` of the course through the map (`Becoming.Learning`'s `prefixOf`). It rests in accord with the feature from step `T` where it proposes the feature's value at every later step (`RestsFrom`, `Rests`). `Becoming.Testing` shows that a claim is excluded at a formation only through a difference that couples into it; `Becoming.Learning` that no prefix fixes the next value; `Becoming.Identification` that in a world a list holds, elimination over the lasso candidates comes to rest in accord with the course through the map within a bound. This module states, with no premise, what a map lets an extrapolation come to rest in accord with.  * **Alike through the map, learned alike.** Two configurations with the same future through the   map give every extrapolation the same prefix at every step (`prefixes_same`). So at a step   where their features differ, a proposed value is right on at most one   (`claim_not_right_on_both`); with the features decided it misses on one, while the two   prefixes stay equal at every step (`missed_on_one`). An extrapolation resting on both has   features that agree from some step on (`agree_of_rests_both`); where they differ at   arbitrarily late steps, no extrapolation rests on both (`not_rests_on_both`), already for two   configurations the law leaves unchanged (`still_not_rests_on_both`). * **What is read off the map is learned by an extrapolation given nothing.** Where the   feature is read off the map (`val s = g (reg s)`), elimination over the lasso candidates on the   values through the map, its proposals read by `g`, rests in accord with the feature in every   world a list holds, from `Becoming.Identification`'s bound on (`rests_through`). It is given   neither the configurations, nor their number, nor any value of the feature. * **Exactly.** In a world a list holds, some extrapolation from prefixes through the map rests in   accord with the feature at every configuration exactly where configurations with the same   future through the map have features that agree from some step on (`learnable_iff`). The   extrapolation of the second direction is built from the law, the map, the feature and the list   (`fromLaw`): no extrapolation does better, whatever it is given, since the first direction   holds of every extrapolation. Features that go alike across the map's lumping   (`Learning.Lumped`) are always learnable (`learnable_of_lumped`). Where the law leaves every   configuration unchanged, they are exactly the learnable ones (`still_learnable_iff_lumped`):   what can be learned through a map is what goes alike across its lumping, the lumping by which   `Becoming.Learning` states generalization. * **Two still configurations** (`Pair`). Through the map to the first of two coordinates, no   extrapolation rests in accord with the second at both `(true, false)` and `(true, true)`   (`Pair.first_not_rests`), the second not going alike across the first (`Pair.not_lumped`).   Through the map to both, elimination over the lasso candidates rests in accord with the second   at every configuration from step 2 (`Pair.whole_rests`).  Resting in accord with the feature from some step on is what the literature calls identification in the limit (Gold 1967), and `learnable_iff` is an observability condition in that setting: its first direction holds of every extrapolation, while the extrapolation of its second direction is built from the law, the map, the feature and the list, so that direction shows existence, not an extrapolation given nothing.  Readings of this as the map bounding what is learned, as the limit of learning without a coupling of the feature back into what is learned, or as a condition on cognition, belong to the study, not to this module. No ethical predicate, ranking or axiom occurs. | S3+S2+S1+S5+L-prefix+L-built+L-extrap+L-miss+L-feature+L-elim |
| /-- The registered course from `s`: what the registration registers at each step of the orbit. -/ | /-- The course from `s` through the map: the value of `reg` at each step of the orbit. -/ | S1 |
| /-- The learner `pred`, reading the registered record, claims the target at every step from `T` on along the orbit from `s`. -/ | /-- The extrapolation `pred`, from the prefix of the course through the map, proposes the feature's value at every step from `T` on along the orbit from `s`. -/ | S1+L-prefix+L-extrap+L-feature |
| /-- The learner comes to rest on the target along the orbit from `s`. -/ | /-- The extrapolation comes to rest in accord with the feature along the orbit from `s`. -/ | L-extrap+L-feature |

No decision is used. -/ | /-- **A proposal finer than the map is right on at most one.** At a step where the features of two configurations with the same future through the map differ, a value an extrapolation proposes is not right on both. | /-- **Already for two still configurations.** Where the law leaves `s` and `s'` unchanged, the registration does not tell them apart and their targets differ, no learner reading the registration rests on the target at both. -/ | /-- **Already for two still configurations.** Where the law leaves `s` and `s'` unchanged, the map does not tell them apart and their features differ, no extrapolation through the map rests in accord with the feature at both. -/ | S1+L-extrap+L-feature |
| /-- **Where the law leaves every configuration unchanged, a target learned at every configuration goes alike across the registration.** -/ | /-- **Where the law leaves every configuration unchanged, a feature learned at every configuration goes alike across the map's lumping.** -/ | S1+L-feature |

| /-- **What is read off the registration is learned by the learner given nothing.** In a world a list holds, where the target is read off what is registered, the learner over the lasso hypotheses on the registered values, its claims read by `g`, rests on the target from `Becoming.Identification`'s bound on (`held_byElimination_rests`). It is given neither the configurations, nor how many there are, nor any value of the target. -/ | /-- **What is read off the map is learned by an extrapolation given nothing.** In a world a list holds, where the feature is read off the map, elimination over the lasso candidates on the values through the map, its proposals read by `g`, rests in accord with the feature from `Becoming.Identification`'s bound on (`held_byElimination_rests`). ## `lean:Parting`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| # Parting: what a record fixes where the course branches  `Becoming.Identification` states what a bounded record fixes under a determined law: the registered course of a world of `N` configurations is fixed by its record before `N + N - 1`, and no earlier step serves every such world. A process of the spine fixes only which steps are admissible, and from one configuration its runs can go different ways. This module states, with no premise beyond what each law names, what a record fixes along the runs of any process.  * **At a parting no learner is right on both.** Two runs part at a step where they register the   same before it and differ at it (`PartAt`). Their records before the step are one record, so   no learner, whatever it is given, the world included, is right at that step on both   (`not_right_on_both`). A learner right at every step from `T` on, on every run from a   configuration, leaves no two of those runs parting at any step from `T` on   (`not_parting_of_right`). With the registered values decided, no two runs from a   configuration part exactly where all of them register one course (`unparted_iff`). | # Parting: what a prefix fixes where the course branches  `Becoming.Identification` states what a bounded prefix fixes under a determined law: the course through a map of a world of `N` configurations is fixed by its prefix before `N + N - 1`, and no earlier step serves every such world. A process of the spine fixes only which steps are admissible, and from one configuration its runs can go different ways. This module states, with no premise beyond what each law names, what a prefix fixes along the runs of any process.  * **At a parting no extrapolation is right on both.** Two runs part at a step where their   courses through the map are the same before it and differ at it (`PartAt`). Their prefixes   before the step are one prefix, so no extrapolation, whatever it is given, the process   included, is right at that step on both (`not_right_on_both`). An extrapolation right at every   step from `T` on, on every run from a configuration, leaves no two of those runs parting at   any step from `T` on (`not_parting_of_right`). With the values through the map decided, no two   runs from a configuration part exactly where all of them have one course through the map   (`unparted_iff`). | S1+L-prefix+L-extrap |
|   from which no two runs part, held by a list of `N` configurations, the registered course has at   most `N` states (`course_statesBelow`), and the learner by the least bound of   `Becoming.Identification`, given nothing, is right at every step from `N + N - 1` on   (`leastBound_right`). No learner is right at step `N + N - 2` on every such run for `N ≥ 2` |   from which no two runs part, held by a list of `N` configurations, the course through the map   has at most `N` states (`course_statesBelow`), and the extrapolation by the least bound of   `Becoming.Identification`, given nothing, is right at every step from `N + N - 1` on   (`leastBound_right`). No extrapolation is right at step `N + N - 2` on every such run for `N ≥ 2` | S1+L-extrap |

The learner by the least bound is right at every step from   `2(T + N) - 1` on (`late_right`). * **Where no two runs part, a learner given the world is right.** Take a process with listed   moves and configurations, decided steps and decided registered values. The learner given the   world (`fromProcess`) reads from the record the configurations a run from `s` can be at   (`candidates`). The   extrapolation built from the process, its listed moves and configurations and the start   (`fromProcess`) reads off the prefix the configurations a run from `s` can be at   (`candidates`). So some learner is right   there exactly where none part (`some_right_iff`): with `not_right_on_both`, at each step a   parting is exactly what neither the record nor the world settles. |   It proposes that configuration's value through the map. So with a bound on configurations a prefix fixes the course through the map exactly as far as the runs do not part, from `N + N - 1` on; where they part, neither the prefix nor the law fixes | S1+L-prefix+L-extrap |

## `lean:LearnableRuns`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| # Learnable runs: what a learner reading a run's registration can learn  `Becoming.Learnable` states what a learner reading a registration can learn along the orbit of a determined law. A determined law is a process of the spine (`Law.process`), and every run of it is its orbit from where the run begins (`Branching.law_run_state`); configurations of a determined law are alike in the frame that registers `reg` exactly where their registered futures are the same (`Law.alike_iff_sameFuture`). This module states the laws of `Becoming.Learnable` about the runs of the law and the likeness of the frame, and proves them through those laws.  * **A run's registered record is its orbit's.** The record a learner reads along a run is the   record along the orbit from the run's first configuration (`prefix_eq`), so a learner rests on   a run exactly where it rests on that orbit (`restsFrom_iff`, `rests_iff`). * **Alike in the frame, learned alike.** Two runs beginning at configurations alike in the frame   that registers `reg` give every learner the same record at every step (`prefixes_same`); a   learner resting on both has targets that agree from some step on (`agree_of_rests_both`), and   where their targets differ at arbitrarily late steps no learner rests on both   (`not_rests_on_both`). * **What is read off the registration is learned along every run a list holds**   (`rests_through`), by the learner over the lasso hypotheses given nothing. * **Exactly.** In a world a list holds, some learner rests on the target along every run of the   law exactly where runs beginning alike in the frame have targets that agree from some step on   (`learnable_iff`); where the law leaves every configuration unchanged, exactly where the target   goes alike across the registration (`still_learnable_iff_lumped`). | # Learnable runs: what can be learned through a map along a run  `Becoming.Learnable` states what an extrapolation through a map can learn along the orbit of a determined law. A determined law is a process of the spine (`Law.process`), and every run of it is its orbit from where the run begins (`Branching.law_run_state`); configurations of a determined law are alike in the frame by `reg` (`Law.frameBy`) exactly where their futures through the map are the same (`Law.alike_iff_sameFuture`). This module states the laws of `Becoming.Learnable` about the runs of the law and the likeness of the frame, and proves them through those laws.  * **A run's prefix is its orbit's.** The prefix through the map along a run is the prefix along   the orbit from the run's first configuration (`prefix_eq`), so an extrapolation rests in accord   on a run exactly where it rests in accord on that orbit (`restsFrom_iff`, `rests_iff`). * **Alike in the frame, learned alike.** Two runs beginning at configurations alike in the frame   by `reg` give every extrapolation the same prefix at every step (`prefixes_same`); an   extrapolation resting on both has features that agree from some step on   (`agree_of_rests_both`), and where their features differ at arbitrarily late steps no   extrapolation rests on both (`not_rests_on_both`). * **What is read off the map is learned along every run a list holds** (`rests_through`), by   elimination over the lasso candidates given nothing. * **Exactly.** In a world a list holds, some extrapolation rests in accord with the feature along   every run of the law exactly where runs beginning alike in the frame have features that agree   from some step on (`learnable_iff`); where the law leaves every configuration unchanged,   exactly where the feature goes alike across the map's lumping (`still_learnable_iff_lumped`). | S1+L-prefix+L-extrap+L-feature+L-elim |
| /-- The registered course of a run: what the registration registers at each of its steps (`Parting.course` on a run of the law's process). -/ | /-- The course of a run through the map: the value of `reg` at each of its steps (`Parting.course` on a run of the law's process). -/ | S1 |
| /-- The learner `pred`, reading the registered record of the run, claims the target at every step from `T` on. -/ | /-- The extrapolation `pred`, from the prefix of the run's course through the map, proposes the feature's value at every step from `T` on. -/ | S1+L-prefix+L-extrap+L-feature |
| /-- The learner comes to rest on the target along the run. -/ | /-- The extrapolation comes to rest in accord with the feature along the run. -/ | L-extrap+L-feature |
| /-- **A run's registered record is the record along the orbit from where it begins.** -/ | /-- **A run's prefix through the map is the prefix along the orbit from where it begins.** -/ | S1+L-prefix |
| /-- **A learner rests on a run from `T` exactly where it rests from `T` on the orbit from where the run begins.** -/ | /-- **An extrapolation rests in accord on a run from `T` exactly where it rests in accord from `T` on the orbit from where the run begins.** -/ | L-extrap |
| /-- Runs beginning alike in the frame that registers `reg` have the same registered future along the runs. -/ | /-- Runs beginning alike in the frame by `reg` have the same future through the map along the runs. -/ | S1 |
| /-- **Runs beginning alike in the frame give every learner the same record.** -/ | /-- **Runs beginning alike in the frame give every extrapolation the same prefix.** -/ | L-prefix+L-extrap |
| /-- **A learner resting on two runs beginning alike in the frame has targets that agree from some step on.** -/ | /-- **An extrapolation resting on two runs beginning alike in the frame has features that agree from some step on.** -/ | L-extrap+L-feature |
| /-- **Where the targets of two runs beginning alike in the frame differ at arbitrarily late steps, no learner rests on both.** -/ | /-- **Where the features of two runs beginning alike in the frame differ at arbitrarily late steps, no extrapolation rests on both.** -/ | L-extrap+L-feature |
| /-- **What is read off the registration is learned along every run a list holds**, by the learner over the lasso hypotheses on the registered values, given nothing, from `Becoming.Identification`'s bound on. -/ | /-- **What is read off the map is learned along every run a list holds**, by elimination over the lasso candidates on the values through the map, given nothing, from `Becoming.Identification`'s bound on. -/ | S1+L-extrap+L-elim |
| /-- **Exactly.** In a world a list holds, some learner rests on the target along every run of the law exactly where runs beginning alike in the frame that registers `reg` have targets that agree from some step on. -/ | /-- **Exactly.** In a world a list holds, some extrapolation rests in accord with the feature along every run of the law exactly where runs beginning alike in the frame by `reg` have features that agree from some step on. -/ | L-extrap+L-feature |
| /-- **Where the law leaves every configuration unchanged, a target is learnable along every run exactly where it goes alike across the registration.** -/ | /-- **Where the law leaves every configuration unchanged, a feature is learnable along every run exactly where it goes alike across the map's lumping.** -/ | S1+L-feature |

## `lean:Unrolling`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
|   reading of the first direction is given the world, the law and the feature (`Learnable.fromWorld`): |   reading of the first direction is built from the list, the law and the feature   (`Learnable.fromWorld`): | L-built |
| /-- **The reading given the world**: the feature of the configuration read for a future, as many steps on as the list is long; `v₀` where no configuration of the list is read. -/ | /-- **The reading built from the list** (with the law, the map and the feature): the feature of the configuration read for a future, as many steps on as the list is long; `v₀` where no configuration of the list is read. -/ | L-built |
