## `lean:ExactRecordRuns`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| # Exact readings of a run's registered record  `KnowledgeCorrespondence` states, for histories, an evidence projection and a two-valued target, when a reading of the evidence answers the target soundly (never wrongly) or exactly (always rightly) on a domain of histories, and when the evidence resolves the target (histories with one evidence have one target). Here the histories are the runs of a process, the evidence at step `t` is the record a registration `reg` leaves along the run before `t` (`recordBefore`), and the target is a two-valued reading `val` of the run's configuration at `t` (`valueAt`). A reading of the record is then a learner at one step: for a determined law, a learner claims the target along every run from `T` on exactly where it reads the record exactly at every step from `T` on (`exact_iff_restsFrom`, through `LearnableRuns.RestsFrom`). This module states the periphery's general laws of these readings of runs and proves them through those laws. | # Exact readings of a run's prefix through a map  `KnowledgeCorrespondence` states, for histories, an evidence projection and a two-valued target, when a reading of the evidence answers the target soundly (never wrongly) or exactly (always rightly) on a domain of histories, and when the evidence resolves the target (histories with one evidence have one target). Here the histories are the runs of a process, the evidence at step `t` is the prefix before `t` of the run's course through a map `reg` (`recordBefore`), and the target is a two-valued feature `val` of the run's configuration at `t` (`valueAt`). A reading of the prefix is then an extrapolation at one step: for a determined law, an extrapolation proposes the feature's value along every run from `T` on exactly where it reads the prefix exactly at every step from `T` on (`exact_iff_restsFrom`, through `LearnableRuns.RestsFrom`). This module states the periphery's general laws of these readings of runs and proves them through those laws. | S1+L-prefix+L-extrap+L-feature |
| * **A sound reading abstains where the record leaves the value open**: where two runs of the   domain have one record before `t` and different values at `t`, a sound reading gives nothing on   that record (`sound_abstains`). * **Soundness holds on every smaller domain of runs** (`sound_within`), and **resolution survives   reading a second registration beside the first** (`finer_resolves`). * **A faithfully kept record keeps exactness** (`kept_exact`): reading the record through what a   retention keeps is exact where the retention is read back faithfully on the domain. | * **A sound reading abstains where the prefix leaves the value open**: where two runs of the   domain have one prefix before `t` and different values at `t`, a sound reading gives nothing on   that prefix (`sound_abstains`). * **Soundness holds on every smaller domain of runs** (`sound_within`), and **resolution survives   reading through a second map beside the first** (`finer_resolves`). * **A faithfully kept prefix keeps exactness** (`kept_exact`): reading the prefix through what a   retention keeps is exact where the retention is read back faithfully on the domain. | S1+L-prefix |
|   domain with one record before `t` have one value at `t` (`exact_resolves`). |   domain with one prefix before `t` have one value at `t` (`exact_resolves`). | L-prefix |
| /-- The record the registration `reg` leaves along a run before step `t`, latest first. -/ | /-- The prefix before step `t` of a run's course through the map `reg`, latest first. -/ | S1+L-prefix |
| /-- **A reading of the record exact on a domain of runs is sound there.** -/ | /-- **A reading of the prefix exact on a domain of runs is sound there.** -/ | L-prefix |
| /-- **An exact reading of the record needs resolution**: runs of the domain with one record before `t` have one value at `t`. -/ | /-- **An exact reading of the prefix needs resolution**: runs of the domain with one prefix before `t` have one value at `t`. -/ | L-prefix |
| /-- **A sound reading abstains where the record leaves the value open**: where two runs of the domain have one record before `t` and different values at `t`, a sound reading gives nothing on that record. -/ | /-- **A sound reading abstains where the prefix leaves the value open**: where two runs of the domain have one prefix before `t` and different values at `t`, a sound reading gives nothing on that prefix. -/ | L-prefix |
| /-- **Resolution survives reading a second registration beside the first.** -/ | /-- **Resolution survives reading through a second map beside the first.** -/ | S1 |
| /-- **A faithfully kept record keeps exactness**: reading the record through what a retention keeps is exact where the retention is read back faithfully on the records of the domain. -/ | /-- **A faithfully kept prefix keeps exactness**: reading the prefix through what a retention keeps is exact where the retention is read back faithfully on the prefixes of the domain. -/ | L-prefix |
| /-- **Abstaining from every record is sound** on every domain of runs. -/ | /-- **Abstaining from every prefix is sound** on every domain of runs. -/ | L-prefix |
| /-- **A learner claims the target along every run of a determined law from `T` on exactly where, at every step from `T` on, it reads the record exactly on all runs.** -/ | /-- **An extrapolation proposes the feature's value along every run of a determined law from `T` on exactly where, at every step from `T` on, it reads the prefix exactly on all runs.** -/ | L-prefix+L-extrap+L-feature |

## `lean:LearningRuns`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| # Learning along runs: what a run's registered record fixes and what a learner reaching it does  `Becoming.Learning` states, of any course of registered values, what a record fixes, what a learner by refutation does on it, what the course answering a learner is, and what training through data selects; `Becoming.Learnable` states what a learner reading a registration learns along a determined law's orbit. A course read along a run of any process is its registered course (`Parting.course`), every course is the registered course of a run of free branching (`Parting.freeRun`), and every run of a determined law is its orbit (`Branching.law_run_state`, `LearnableRuns`). This module states those laws of runs.  * **No record fixes the next value.** At a step where two runs of any process part (they register   alike before it and apart at it, `Parting.PartAt`), a claim made from the record, the values   decided, is refuted on one of them (`missed_on_one_of_part`). Every run's record is shared by   two runs of free branching that repeat from that step on and register two given values there,   and no claim from the record is right on both (`prefix_fixes_no_next`). * **The answering law.** The course answering every claim of a learner is the registered course   of a run of a determined law that keeps its record (`answerReg`, `answer_course`); no list holds   its configurations (`answer_open`); with two values told apart every claim made along it is   refuted (`answer_misses`); and the learner over every merging of classes of hypotheses never   comes to rest along it (`merged_unrested`). * **Learning by refutation along a run.** The learner is through the run's record   (`byElimination_through_prefix`); every hypothesis it passed was refuted at an earlier step of the run   (`missed_before`); over merged classes it holds no hypothesis beyond the place of one that   holds along the run (`merged_bound`); where it is right from some step on along a run, the   run's registered course repeats from some step (`lasso_of_right`). * **Induction along a run held by a list.** On a run of a determined law whose configurations a   list holds, the learner over the lasso hypotheses is refuted fewer than `tri l.length` times,   claims from `l.length` on and is not refuted at arbitrarily late steps   (`held_byElimination_bounded`); that it rests is unrefuted (`held_rest_unrefuted`). Along the run of   negation from `false` it rests from step `3` (`alternate_rests`); negation never rests   (`negation_never_rests`). * **Generalization from a run.** Within the targets that go alike across a registration, their   values at the configurations a run passed before `t` fix the value at a configuration exactly   where it registers like one of them (`determines_iff_lumped_alike`); the value carried over   is right exactly where the world takes one value at the two (`carried_right_iff`), as it does   for a world going alike across the registration (`world_carried_right`). * **Training on runs' records.** Read the registered courses of a process's runs as the class   (`Courses`) and the moments before `t` as the data. A loss computed from the record selects   alike runs registering alike before `t` (`loss_through_prefix`); within a class free beyond the   record, a selection through it leaves the value at a later moment as free as the class does   (`unselected_beyond_prefix`); and a fit to the record is right at a moment on every run exactly   where the record determines it there (`fit_right_iff_runs`). Where runs branch freely, the   record fixes no later value (`free_prefix_fixes_nothing`). * **Alike in the frame, learned alike.** On runs of a determined law beginning alike in the frame   that registers `reg`, a claim at a step where the targets differ is right on at most one   (`claim_not_right_on_both`); with the targets decided it is refuted on one, the records equal at   every step (`missed_on_one`). Along the runs of the law leaving two coordinates unchanged, no   learner reading the first rests on the second at both `(true, false)` and `(true, true)`   (`pair_first_not_rests`), none rests on it along every run (`pair_not_learnable`), and reading   both, the learner over the lasso hypotheses rests on the second from step `2`   (`pair_whole_rests`). | # Learning along runs: what a run's prefix fixes and what an extrapolation along it does  `Becoming.Learning` states, of any course of values, what a prefix fixes, what extrapolation by elimination does on it, what the diagonal course of an extrapolation is, and what training through data selects; `Becoming.Learnable` states what an extrapolation through a map learns along a determined law's orbit. A course read along a run of any process is its course through a map (`Parting.course`), every course is the course of a run of free branching through the identity (`Parting.freeRun`), and every run of a determined law is its orbit (`Branching.law_run_state`, `LearnableRuns`). This module states those laws of runs.  * **No prefix fixes the next value.** At a step where two runs of any process part (alike   through the map before it and apart at it, `Parting.PartAt`), a value proposed from the prefix,   the values decided, misses on one of them (`missed_on_one_of_part`). Every run's prefix is   shared by two runs of free branching that repeat from that step on and take two given values   there, and no value proposed from the prefix is right on both (`prefix_fixes_no_next`). * **The diagonal law.** The course on which every value an extrapolation proposes misses is the   course through a map of a run of a determined law that keeps the whole prefix (`answerReg`,   `answer_course`); no list holds its configurations (`answer_open`); with two values told apart   every value proposed along it misses (`answer_misses`); and elimination over every merging of   classes of candidates never comes to rest along it (`merged_unrested`). * **Elimination along a run.** The extrapolation is through the run's prefix   (`byElimination_through_prefix`); every candidate it passed missed at an earlier step of the run   (`missed_before`); over merged classes its current candidate is never beyond the place of one   that accords along the run (`merged_bound`); where it is right from some step on along a run,   the run's course through the map repeats from some step (`lasso_of_right`). * **Induction along a run held by a list.** On a run of a determined law whose configurations a   list holds, elimination over the lasso candidates misses fewer than `tri l.length` times,   proposes from `l.length` on and does not miss at arbitrarily late steps   (`held_byElimination_bounded`); that it rests is unrefuted (`held_rest_unrefuted`). Along the run of   negation from `false` it rests from step `3` (`alternate_rests`); negation never rests   (`negation_never_rests`). * **Generalization from a run.** Within the features that go alike across a map's lumping, their   values at the configurations a run passed before `t` fix the value at a configuration exactly   where it is lumped with one of them (`determines_iff_lumped_alike`); the value carried over   is right exactly where the world takes one value at the two (`carried_right_iff`), as it does   for a world going alike across the lumping (`world_carried_right`). * **Training on runs' prefixes.** Read the courses through the map of a process's runs as the   class (`Courses`) and the moments before `t` as the data. A loss computed from the prefix   selects alike runs alike through the map before `t` (`loss_through_prefix`); within a class free   beyond the prefix, a selection through it leaves the value at a later moment as free as the class   does (`unselected_beyond_prefix`); and a fit to the prefix is right at a moment on every run   exactly where the prefix determines it there (`fit_right_iff_runs`). Where runs branch freely,   the prefix fixes no later value (`free_prefix_fixes_nothing`). * **Alike in the frame, learned alike.** On runs of a determined law beginning alike in the frame   by `reg` (`Law.frameBy`), a value proposed at a step where the features differ is right on at   most one (`claim_not_right_on_both`); with the features decided it misses on one, the prefixes   equal at every step (`missed_on_one`). Along the runs of the law leaving two coordinates   unchanged, no extrapolation through the map to the first rests in accord with the second at   both `(true, false)` and `(true, true)` (`pair_first_not_rests`), none rests in accord with it   along every run (`pair_not_learnable`), and through the map to both, elimination over the lasso   candidates rests in accord with the second from step `2` (`pair_whole_rests`). | S3+S1+L-prefix+L-extrap+L-miss+L-feature+L-elim |
| /-! ## Refutation read through equal records -/ | /-! ## Misses read through equal prefixes -/ | L-prefix+L-miss |
| /-- A refutation carries to an equal claim and an equal value. -/ | /-- A miss carries to an equal proposal and an equal value. -/ | L-extrap+L-miss |
| /-- A refutation at a step carries to a course with the same record and value there. -/ | /-- A miss at a step carries to a course with the same prefix and value there. -/ | L-prefix+L-miss |
| /-- Held from `t` at the value of `o'`, a course agreeing with `o'` before `t` is refuted at `t` only where `o'` is. -/ | /-- Held from `t` at the value of `o'`, a course agreeing with `o'` before `t` is missed at `t` only where `o'` is. -/ | L-miss |
| /-- **At a parting, a claim from the record is refuted on one of the two runs.** With the registered values decided, where two runs register alike before `t` and apart at `t`, a claim made at `t` from the record is refuted on one of them. -/ | /-- **At a parting, a value proposed from the prefix misses on one of the two runs.** With the values through the map decided, where two runs are alike through the map before `t` and apart at `t`, a value proposed at `t` from the prefix misses on one of them. -/ | S3+S1+L-prefix+L-extrap+L-miss |
| /-- **No record of a run fixes the next value, not even among finite worlds.** Given two values, the record of every run before `t` is the record of two runs of free branching that repeat from `t` on, one registering each value at `t`; a claim made from the record is not right on both. -/ | /-- **No prefix of a run fixes the next value, not even among finite worlds.** Given two values, the prefix of every run before `t` is the prefix of two runs of free branching that repeat from `t` on, one taking each value at `t`; a value proposed from the prefix is not right on both. -/ | L-prefix+L-extrap |
| /-- **The learner by refutation is through the run's record.** On two runs registering alike before `t`, it holds the same hypothesis and makes the same claim at `t`. -/ | /-- **The extrapolation by elimination is through the run's prefix.** On two runs alike through the map before `t`, its current candidate and its proposal at `t` are the same. -/ | S3+S1+L-prefix+L-extrap+L-elim |
| /-- **Every hypothesis the learner passed along a run was refuted at an earlier step of the run.** -/ | /-- **Every candidate elimination passed along a run missed at an earlier step of the run.** -/ | L-extrap+L-miss+L-elim |
| /-- **Learning over classes along a run.** Where hypothesis `j` of class `c` is refuted at no step of the run's registered course, the learner over the merged classes holds no hypothesis beyond place `tri (c + j) + j`. -/ | /-- **Learning over classes along a run.** Where candidate `j` of class `c` misses at no step of the run's course through the map, the current candidate of elimination over the merged classes is never beyond place `tri (c + j) + j`. -/ | S1+L-extrap+L-miss+L-elim |
| /-- **Where the learner over the lasso hypotheses is right from some step on along a run, the run's registered course repeats from some step.** -/ | /-- **Where elimination over the lasso candidates is right from some step on along a run, the run's course through the map repeats from some step.** -/ | S1+L-extrap+L-elim |
| /-- **Generalization from a run.** Within the targets that go alike across the registration, the values at the configurations a run passed before `t` fix the value at `s` exactly where `s` registers like one of them, given two values. -/ | /-- **Generalization from a run.** Within the features that go alike across the map's lumping, the values at the configurations a run passed before `t` fix the value at `s` exactly where `s` is lumped with one of them, given two values. -/ | S1+L-feature |
| two.** A target going alike across the registration and fitting the world `f` at the configurations a run passed before `t` takes at `s` its value at a passed configuration `d` registering like `s`; it is right at `s` exactly where `f` takes one value at `d` and `s`. -/ | two.** A feature going alike across the lumping and fitting the world `f` at the configurations a run passed before `t` takes at `s` its value at a passed configuration `d` lumped with `s`; it is right at `s` exactly where `f` takes one value at `d` and `s`. -/ | S1+L-feature |
| /-- **A world going alike across the registration is learned from a run at every configuration registering like one the run passed.** -/ | /-- **A world going alike across the lumping is learned from a run at every configuration lumped with one the run passed.** -/ | S1 |
| /-! ## Training on the records of runs -/ | /-! ## Training on the prefixes of runs -/ | L-prefix |
| /-- The registered courses of the runs of `P`: the class a learner reading `reg` along the runs can meet. -/ | /-- The courses through `reg` of the runs of `P`: the class an extrapolation through `reg` along the runs can meet. -/ | L-extrap |
| /-- The registered course of every run is one of them. -/ | /-- The course through the map of every run is one of them. -/ | S1 |

| /-- **Training through the record leaves the later value as free as the runs leave it.** Where the registered courses of `P` are free at `x` beyond the record before `t`, for every run whose course a selection through the record selects and every run, a selected course of a run agrees with the first on the record and takes the second's value at `x`. -/ | /-- **Training through the prefix leaves the later value as free as the runs leave it.** Where the courses through the map of `P` are free at `x` beyond the prefix before `t`, for every run whose course a selection through the prefix selects and every run, a selected course of a run agrees with the first on the prefix and takes the second's value at `x`. -/ | S1+L-prefix |
| /-- **A fit to the record is right at a moment on every run exactly where the record determines it.** Every two runs registering alike before `t` register alike at `x` exactly where, within the registered courses of `P`, the record before `t` determines the value at `x`. -/ | /-- **A fit to the prefix is right at a moment on every run exactly where the prefix determines it.** Every two runs alike through the map before `t` are alike at `x` exactly where, within the courses through the map of `P`, the prefix before `t` determines the value at `x`. -/ | S3+S1+L-prefix |
| /-- **Where runs branch freely, the record fixes no later value.** Given two values, within the registered courses of the runs of free branching the record before `t` fixes the value at no moment from `t` on. -/ | /-- **Where runs branch freely, the prefix fixes no later value.** Given two values, within the courses of the runs of free branching the prefix before `t` fixes the value at no moment from `t` on. -/ | L-prefix |
| /-! ## The answering law -/ | /-! ## The diagonal law -/ | TEXT |
| /-- The registration of the law that answers `pred`: the answer to the claim made from the record the law keeps. -/ | /-- The map read along the diagonal law of `pred`: the value set against the proposal made from the prefix the law keeps. -/ | S1+L-prefix+L-extrap |
| /-- **The answering course is the registered course of a run of the answering law.** Along every run of `answerStep` from the empty record, what `answerReg` registers is the diagonal course. -/ | /-- **The diagonal course is the course through a map of a run of the diagonal law.** Along every run of `answerStep` from the empty prefix, the course through `answerReg` is the diagonal course. -/ | S1+L-prefix |
| /-- **The answering world is open.** No list holds the configurations of a run of the answering law from the empty record. -/ | /-- **The diagonal world is open.** No list holds the configurations of a run of the diagonal law from the empty prefix. -/ | L-prefix |
| /-- **With two values told apart, every claim made along the answering run is refuted.** -/ | /-- **With two values told apart, every value proposed along the diagonal run misses.** -/ | L-extrap+L-miss |
| /-- **The learner over every merging never rests along its answering run.** However classes of hypotheses are chosen and merged, the learner over them is right from no step on along the run of the law that answers it. -/ | /-- **Elimination over every merging never rests along its diagonal run.** However classes of candidates are chosen and merged, elimination over them is right from no step on along the run of its diagonal law. -/ | L-extrap+L-elim |
| /-- **Induction along a run held by a list: the refutations are bounded.** On a run of a determined law whose configurations a list `l` holds, the learner over the lasso hypotheses, reading the registration and knowing neither the configurations nor how many there are, is refuted fewer than `tri l.length` times, claims at every step from `l.length` on, and is not refuted at arbitrarily late steps. -/ | /-- **Induction along a run held by a list: the misses are bounded.** On a run of a determined law whose configurations a list `l` holds, elimination over the lasso candidates, through the map and given neither the configurations nor their number, misses fewer than `tri l.length` times, proposes at every step from `l.length` on, and does not miss at arbitrarily late steps. -/ | S1+L-extrap+L-miss+L-elim |
| /-- **Induction along a run held by a list: that the learner rests is unrefuted.** -/ | /-- **Induction along a run held by a list: that elimination rests is unrefuted.** -/ | L-extrap |
| /-- **A claim finer than the registration is right on at most one of two runs beginning alike.** On runs of a determined law beginning alike in the frame that registers `reg`, at a step where the targets differ, a learner's claim is not right on both. -/ | /-- **A proposal finer than the map is right on at most one of two runs beginning alike.** On runs of a determined law beginning alike in the frame by `reg`, at a step where the features differ, a value an extrapolation proposes is not right on both. -/ | S1+L-extrap+L-feature |
| /-- **With the targets decided, the claim is refuted on one of two runs beginning alike, and their records are equal at every step.** -/ | /-- **With the features decided, the proposal misses on one of two runs beginning alike, and their prefixes are equal at every step.** -/ | L-prefix+L-extrap+L-miss+L-feature |
| /-- **Two refutations, then rest.** Along every run of negation from `false`, the learner over the lasso hypotheses reading the configuration claims each value right from step `3` on. -/ | /-- **Two misses, then rest.** Along every run of negation from `false`, elimination over the lasso candidates through the identity proposes each value right from step `3` on. -/ | L-extrap+L-miss+L-elim |
| /-- **Registering the first coordinate, no learner rests on the second along the runs from both `(true, false)` and `(true, true)`** of the law leaving each configuration unchanged. -/ | /-- **Through the map to the first coordinate, no extrapolation rests in accord with the second along the runs from both `(true, false)` and `(true, true)`** of the law leaving each configuration unchanged. -/ | S1+L-extrap |
| /-- **Registering the first coordinate, no learner rests on the second along every run.** -/ | /-- **Through the map to the first coordinate, no extrapolation rests in accord with the second along every run.** -/ | S1+L-extrap |
| /-- **Registering both, the learner over the lasso hypotheses rests on the second along every run from step `2`.** -/ | /-- **Through the map to both, elimination over the lasso candidates rests in accord with the second along every run from step `2`.** -/ | S1+L-extrap+L-elim |

## `lean:Learning`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| undissolved". This module states, with no premise beyond what each law names, when learning from what registers succeeds, what a model of structure and of change carries beyond what registered, what a selection through the data can reach, and what the next level up is. A course is a sequence of registered values, such as the registration of a determined law's orbit; a learner is any claim made from the record before a step. | undissolved". This module states, with no premise beyond what each law names, when learning through a map comes into accord with what follows, what a model of structure and of change carries beyond the data, what a selection through the data can reach, and what the next level up is. A course is a sequence of values, such as a determined law's orbit read through a map; an extrapolation (`pred`) is any map from the prefix before a step to a proposed value or to silence. | S1+L-prefix+L-extrap |
| * **No record fixes the next value.** Given two values, two courses share any record and   differ at its next step, so a claim made from the record is not right on both   (`no_prefix_fixes_next`), and, values decided, is refuted on one of them   (`claim_missed_on_one`); both are courses of worlds held by a list | * **No prefix fixes the next value.** Given two values, two courses share any prefix and   differ at its next step, so a value proposed from the prefix is not right on both   (`no_prefix_fixes_next`), and, values decided, misses on one of them   (`claim_missed_on_one`); both are courses of worlds held by a list | L-prefix+L-extrap+L-miss |
| * **No learner rests on every course.** For every learner a course answers each of its claims   against it (`diagonal`, `diagonal_misses`), so on that course it rests at no step   (`diagonal_unrested`). The answering course is the registration of a law that keeps its   record, a world no list holds (`diagonal_open`). * **Learning by refutation.** Holding hypotheses in order and moving to the next exactly where   the one held is refuted (`indexOn`, `byElimination`), the learner never passes a hypothesis that   holds, so it is refuted at most that hypothesis's place (`index_le_of_accords`) and not at   arbitrarily late steps (`misses_not_recurring`); every hypothesis passed was refuted on   the record at a recorded step (`missed_before`). The learner is through the record: one record, one   hypothesis held and one claim (`byElimination_through_prefix`). | * **No extrapolation rests on every course.** For every extrapolation, on its diagonal course   each value it proposes misses at its step (`diagonal`, `diagonal_misses`), so on that course it   rests at no step (`diagonal_unrested`). The diagonal course is a law's orbit, read at its   latest value, of a law that keeps the whole prefix, a world no list holds (`diagonal_open`). * **Extrapolation by elimination.** Taking candidates in order and moving to the next exactly   where the current one misses (`indexOn`, `byElimination`), the extrapolation never passes a   candidate that accords, so it misses at most that candidate's place (`index_le_of_accords`)   and not at arbitrarily late steps (`misses_not_recurring`); every candidate passed missed at   a step of the prefix (`missed_before`). The extrapolation is through the prefix: one prefix,   one current candidate and one proposed value (`byElimination_through_prefix`). | L-prefix+L-extrap+L-miss+L-elim |
|   configurations along an orbit are held by a list `l`, every registration of the orbit repeats   from some step `i` with some period `q + 1`, `i + q + 1 ≤ l.length` (`lasso_of_held`, from   `revisits_within`); every such course is the registration of a world held by a list   (`lasso_realized`). * **Induction in a world held by a list.** Over the lasso hypotheses in diagonal order   (`lassos`, `pairAt`, `pairAt_place`), a learner that reads only a registration it can tell   apart, and knows neither the configurations nor how many there are, is refuted fewer than   `tri l.length` times, claims at every step from `l.length` on and is not refuted at   arbitrarily late steps (`held_byElimination_bounded`, `lasso_byElimination_bounded`); that it comes to rest   is unrefuted, a double negation (`held_rest_unrefuted`, `rest_unrefuted`). It comes to rest   only on lasso courses (`lasso_of_rest`). The step at which it rests is not stated here. Its   record never shows that it has come to rest: given two values, every claim it makes is refuted,   at that step, on a course of a world held by a list that shares its record   (`no_prefix_fixes_next_finite`). Its answering course is no lasso (`diagonal_not_lasso`).   In `Alternate` it is refuted twice and holds from the third step on, at the bound   `tri 1 + 1 = 2`. * **The meta level.** Classes of hypotheses merged along the pairs (`merged`) are learned by   the same refutation, with the bound composed (`merged_bound`); the learner over every merging   is unrested on its answering course (`merged_unrested`). |   configurations along an orbit are held by a list `l`, the orbit read through any map repeats   from some step `i` with some period `q + 1`, `i + q + 1 ≤ l.length` (`lasso_of_held`, from   `revisits_within`); every such course is the course, through a map, of a world held by a list   (`lasso_realized`). * **Induction in a world held by a list.** Over the lasso candidates in diagonal order   (`lassos`, `pairAt`, `pairAt_place`), the extrapolation by elimination, which proposes only   from the prefix through a map with decided values and is given neither the   configurations nor their number, misses fewer than `tri l.length` times, proposes at every   step from `l.length` on and does not miss at arbitrarily late steps   (`held_byElimination_bounded`, `lasso_byElimination_bounded`); that it comes to rest   is unrefuted, a double negation (`held_rest_unrefuted`, `rest_unrefuted`). It comes to rest   only on lasso courses (`lasso_of_rest`). The step at which it rests is not stated here. Its   prefix never shows that it has come to rest: given two values, every value it proposes   misses, at that step, on a course of a world held by a list that shares its prefix   (`no_prefix_fixes_next_finite`). Its diagonal course is no lasso (`diagonal_not_lasso`).   In `Alternate` it misses twice and accords from the third step on, at the bound   `tri 1 + 1 = 2`. * **The meta level.** Classes of candidates merged along the pairs (`merged`) are learned by   the same elimination, with the bound composed (`merged_bound`); the extrapolation over every   merging is unrested on its diagonal course (`merged_unrested`). | S1+L-prefix+L-extrap+L-miss+L-elim |

| **Classical sources.** Learning by refutation over an enumeration of hypotheses is Gold's identification in the limit by enumeration (Gold 1967, "Language identification in the limit"; Putnam 1965 for trial-and-error predicates): the learner changes its claim finitely often and is right from some step on, with no step at which its record shows that it is. The diagonal course answering every learner is the diagonal argument of Putnam (1963) and Gold (1967) that no learner identifies every course; the reading of induction as convergence without certainty follows Kelly (1996). What is the spine's own is the form: every learner and course is stated on the same registrations as the rest of the core, no decision is used beyond what each theorem names, and the rest of the learner by refutation is stated only as unrefuted (`rest_unrefuted`, `held_rest_unrefuted`, a double negation), its step being given in `Becoming.Identification`. | **Classical sources.** Extrapolation by elimination over an enumeration of candidates is what the literature calls Gold's identification in the limit by enumeration, a learner refuting hypotheses (Gold 1967, "Language identification in the limit"; Putnam 1965 for trial-and-error predicates): the extrapolation changes its proposal finitely often and is right from some step on, with no step at which its prefix shows that it is. The diagonal course on which every extrapolation misses is the diagonal argument of Putnam (1963) and Gold (1967) that no learner identifies every course; the reading of induction as convergence without certainty follows Kelly (1996). What is the spine's own is the form: every extrapolation and course is stated through the same maps as the rest of the core, no decision is used beyond what each theorem names, and the rest of the extrapolation by elimination is stated only as unrefuted (`rest_unrefuted`, `held_rest_unrefuted`, a double negation), its step being given in `Becoming.Identification`. | S1+L-prefix+L-extrap+L-miss+L-elim |
| What is not derived: which hypotheses, classes or lumpings an actual formation holds or can reach; that its registration tells values apart; that its world is held by a list; that an actual network's architecture and updates impose a given lumping; the correspondence of any of these to an actual learner. No ethical predicate, ranking or axiom occurs. | What is not derived: which candidates, classes or lumpings an actual formation goes through or can reach; that the values of its map are decided; that its world is held by a list; that an actual network's architecture and updates impose a given lumping; the correspondence of any of these to an actual formation that learns. No ethical predicate, ranking or axiom occurs. | S1+L-extrap+L-elim |

| /-- **Every claim is answered.** Where `other` moves every value, every claim `pred` makes on the diagonal course is refuted at its step. It holds of every `pred`: of every learner, whatever it holds and however it revises. -/ | /-- **Every proposed value misses on the diagonal course.** Where `other` moves every value, every value `pred` proposes on its diagonal course misses at its step. It holds of every `pred`: of every extrapolation, whatever candidates it goes through and however it revises. -/ | L-extrap+L-miss+L-elim |
| /-- **No learner comes to rest on every course.** On the diagonal course of `pred`, at no step from which on `pred` claims and is right. -/ | /-- **No extrapolation comes to rest on every course.** On the diagonal course of `pred` there is no step from which on `pred` proposes a value and is right. -/ | L-extrap |
| /-- The law keeping the record holds `t` values at step `t`. -/ | /-- The law keeping the prefix holds `t` values at step `t`. -/ | L-prefix |
| /-- **The answering world is open.** No list holds the configurations of the law whose registration is the diagonal course: it keeps its record, so it never returns (`unlisted_of_new` of `Becoming.Recurrence`). -/ | /-- **The diagonal world is open.** No list holds the configurations of the law whose orbit, read at its latest value, is the diagonal course: it keeps the whole prefix, so it never returns (`unlisted_of_new` of `Becoming.Recurrence`). -/ | L-prefix |
| /-! ## Learning by refutation -/ | /-! ## Extrapolation by elimination -/ | L-extrap+L-elim |
| /-- Learning by refutation over hypotheses `H`: from a record, the index of the hypothesis held, starting at `0` and moving to the next exactly where the one held is refuted. The index is the number of refutations so far. -/ | /-- Elimination over candidates `H`: from a prefix, the index of the current candidate, starting at `0` and moving to the next exactly where the current one misses. The index is the number of misses so far. -/ | L-prefix+L-miss+L-elim |
| /-- The learner by refutation: the claim of the hypothesis it holds. -/ | /-- The extrapolation by elimination: the value proposed by the current candidate. -/ | L-extrap+L-elim |
| /-- **The learner never passes a hypothesis that holds.** Where hypothesis `j` is refuted at no step of `o`, the learner holds no hypothesis beyond `j`: it is refuted at most `j` times. -/ | /-- **The extrapolation by elimination never passes a candidate that accords.** Where candidate `j` misses at no step of `o`, the current candidate is never beyond `j`: it misses at most `j` times. -/ | L-extrap+L-miss+L-elim |
| /-- **Every hypothesis passed was refuted on the record.** Each hypothesis before the one held at `t` was held at some earlier step and refuted there. -/ | /-- **Every candidate passed missed at a step of the prefix.** Each candidate before the current one at `t` was current at some earlier step and missed there. -/ | L-prefix+L-miss+L-elim |
| /-- Refutations at arbitrarily late steps raise the index past every bound. -/ | /-- Misses at arbitrarily late steps raise the index past every bound. -/ | L-miss |
| /-- **Refutations are not unbounded where a hypothesis holds.** Where some hypothesis is refuted at no step of `o`, the learner is not refuted at arbitrarily late steps. -/ | /-- **Misses do not recur where a candidate accords.** Where some candidate misses at no step of `o`, the extrapolation by elimination does not miss at arbitrarily late steps. -/ | L-extrap+L-miss+L-elim |
| /-- **The learner is through the record.** On two courses with the same record before `t`, the learner holds the same hypothesis and makes the same claim at `t`. -/ | /-- **The extrapolation is through the prefix.** On two courses with the same prefix before `t`, the current candidate and the proposed value at `t` are the same. -/ | L-prefix+L-extrap+L-elim |
| along an orbit are held by a list `l`, every registration of the orbit repeats, from some step `i` on, with some period `q + 1`, where `i + q + 1 ≤ l.length` (from `revisits_within`). -/ | along an orbit are held by a list `l`, the orbit read through any map repeats, from some step `i` on, with some period `q + 1`, where `i + q + 1 ≤ l.length` (from `revisits_within`). -/ | S1 |
| `0, …, i + q` is held by the list of its positions and registers `o` at every step. -/ | `0, …, i + q` is held by the list of its positions and, read through `o`, takes the values of `o` at every step. -/ | TEXT |
| /-- **Not even within finite worlds does a record fix the next value.** The two courses of `no_prefix_fixes_next` are lasso courses, so courses of worlds held by a list (`lasso_realized`): whatever a learner claims next from a record, some finite world with that record refutes it. -/ | /-- **Not even within finite worlds does a prefix fix the next value.** The two courses of `no_prefix_fixes_next` are lasso courses, so courses of worlds held by a list (`lasso_realized`): whatever value an extrapolation proposes next from a prefix, it misses in some finite world with that prefix. -/ | L-prefix+L-extrap+L-miss |
| /-- The lasso hypothesis `h = (i, q)`: at a step `t` with `i + q + 1 ≤ t` it claims the value `q + 1` steps back; before, it claims nothing. -/ | /-- The lasso candidate `h = (i, q)`: at a step `t` with `i + q + 1 ≤ t` it proposes the value `q + 1` steps back; before, it stays silent. -/ | L-extrap+L-elim |
| /-- **A lasso hypothesis holds of its course.** -/ | /-- **A lasso candidate accords with its course.** -/ | L-miss+L-elim |
| /-! ## The lasso hypotheses in order -/ | /-! ## The lasso candidates in order -/ | L-elim |
| /-- The lasso hypotheses in the order of their pairs. -/ | /-- The lasso candidates in the order of their pairs. -/ | L-elim |
| /-- **On a lasso course the learner's refutations are bounded.** On a course repeating from step `i` with period `q + 1`, the learner by refutation over the lasso hypotheses, which knows neither `i` nor `q`: holds no hypothesis beyond place `tri (i + q) + q`, so it is refuted at most that many times; claims at every step from `i + q + 1` on; and is not refuted at arbitrarily late steps. -/ | /-- **On a lasso course the misses of elimination are bounded.** On a course repeating from step `i` with period `q + 1`, the extrapolation by elimination over the lasso candidates, which is given neither `i` nor `q`: its current candidate is never beyond place `tri (i + q) + q`, so it misses at most that many times; it proposes at every step from `i + q + 1` on; and it does not miss at arbitrarily late steps. -/ | L-extrap+L-miss+L-elim |
| /-- **On a lasso course, that the learner comes to rest is unrefuted.** It is not refuted that from some step on it claims at every step and is right (a double negation); the step is not stated here. The learner's record never shows that it rests: given two values, a claim made from a record is not right on both of two courses that share the record and differ at its next step (`no_prefix_fixes_next`). -/ | /-- **On a lasso course, that elimination comes to rest is unrefuted.** It is not refuted that from some step on it proposes at every step and is right (a double negation); the step is not stated here. Its prefix never shows that it rests: given two values, a value proposed from a prefix is not right on both of two courses that share the prefix and differ at its next step (`no_prefix_fixes_next`). -/ | L-prefix+L-extrap |
| /-- **The learner comes to rest only on lasso courses.** Where from step `T` on it claims at every step and is right, the course repeats from some step on. -/ | /-- **Elimination over the lasso candidates comes to rest only on lasso courses.** Where from step `T` on it proposes at every step and is right, the course repeats from some step on. -/ | L-extrap+L-elim |
| /-- **Induction in a world held by a list: the refutations are bounded.** Under a determined law whose configurations along an orbit are held by a list `l`, the learner by refutation over the lasso hypotheses, reading only a registration it can tell apart and knowing neither the configurations nor how many there are: is refuted fewer than `tri l.length` times; claims at every step from `l.length` on; and is not refuted at arbitrarily late steps. -/ | /-- **Induction in a world held by a list: the misses are bounded.** Under a determined law whose configurations along an orbit are held by a list `l`, the extrapolation by elimination over the lasso candidates, proposing from the orbit's prefix through a map `reg` with decided values and given neither the configurations nor their number: misses fewer than `tri l.length` times; proposes at every step from `l.length` on; and does not miss at arbitrarily late steps. -/ | S1+L-extrap+L-miss+L-elim |
| /-- **Induction in a world held by a list: that the learner comes to rest is unrefuted.** Under a determined law whose configurations along an orbit are held by a list, it is not refuted that from some step on the learner over the lasso hypotheses claims at every step and is right (a double negation). -/ | /-- **Induction in a world held by a list: that elimination comes to rest is unrefuted.** Under a determined law whose configurations along an orbit are held by a list, it is not refuted that from some step on the extrapolation by elimination over the lasso candidates proposes at every step and is right (a double negation). -/ | L-extrap+L-elim |
| /-- **The answering world is no lasso.** The diagonal course of the learner over the lasso hypotheses repeats from no step: it is the course of no world held by a list. -/ | /-- **The diagonal world is no lasso.** The diagonal course of elimination over the lasso candidates repeats from no step: it is the course of no world held by a list. -/ | L-extrap+L-elim |
| /-- Classes of hypotheses merged into one enumeration along the pairs. -/ | /-- Classes of candidates merged into one enumeration along the pairs. -/ | L-elim |
| /-- **Learning over classes is learning by refutation.** Where hypothesis `j` of class `c` holds, the learner by refutation over the merged enumeration holds no hypothesis beyond place `tri (c + j) + j`: choosing a class is learning with the same law, one enumeration wider. -/ | /-- **Learning over classes is elimination again.** Where candidate `j` of class `c` accords, the current candidate of elimination over the merged enumeration is never beyond place `tri (c + j) + j`: choosing a class is learning with the same law, one enumeration wider. -/ | L-extrap+L-miss+L-elim |
| /-- **The learner over every merging is unrested on its diagonal course.** However the classes are chosen and merged, the learner over them is refuted at every claim on its diagonal course, which no list holds (`diagonal_misses`, `diagonal_open`). Read as no top level: learning at the next level up is again learning, and again incomplete. -/ | /-- **Elimination over every merging is unrested on its diagonal course.** However the classes are chosen and merged, every value elimination over them proposes on its diagonal course misses, and no list holds that world (`diagonal_misses`, `diagonal_open`). Read as no top level: learning at the next level up is again learning, and again incomplete. -/ | L-extrap+L-miss |
| from `s` exactly where `s₀` and `s` go alike under `π` by `a`. The record does not fix this | from `s` exactly where `s₀` and `s` go alike under `π` by `a`. The prefix does not fix this | L-prefix |
| /-- P64's selection through a formation's registration is a selection through registering alike. -/ | /-- P64's selection through a formation's couplings is a selection through the classes of histories alike through them. -/ | S3+S2 |
| /-- The learner is refuted twice, at steps `1` and `2`, and holds the hypothesis `(0, 1)` from step `3` on. -/ | /-- Elimination misses twice, at steps `1` and `2`, and its current candidate is `(0, 1)` from step `3` on. -/ | L-extrap+L-miss+L-elim |
| /-- **Two refutations, then rest.** From step `3` on the learner holds `(0, 1)`, the place `tri 1 + 1 = 2` of `lasso_byElimination_bounded`, and claims each value right. -/ | /-- **Two misses, then rest.** From step `3` on the current candidate is `(0, 1)`, the place `tri 1 + 1 = 2` of `lasso_byElimination_bounded`, and each value it proposes is right. -/ | L-extrap+L-miss+L-elim |
| of the class fitting the world `f` on the data claims at `x` the value of an observed input lumped with `x`; it is right exactly where `f` takes one value at the two. -/ | of the class fitting the world `f` on the data takes at `x` the value of an observed input lumped with `x`; it is right exactly where `f` takes one value at the two. -/ | L-extrap |
| * **Selection through a model of the options.** A selection or valuation through a model of the   options (`Relabel.Equivariant`, `Relabel.EquivariantValue` of `Becoming.Selection`) is a   selection through the model's lumping (`selects_through_lumping`, `values_lumped`). It is right   about what matters only where what matters goes alike across that lumping   (`selects_right_through`, `values_right_lumped`): where what matters separates two options the   model lumps, none is right (`selects_not_right_of_parted`, `values_not_right_of_parted`); where   what matters is read off the model, reading it through the model is right   (`selects_right_of_reads`, `values_right_of_reads`). | * **Selection through a map of the alternatives.** A selection or valuation through a map of the   alternatives (`Relabel.Equivariant`, `Relabel.EquivariantValue` of `Becoming.Selection`) is a   selection through the map's lumping (`selects_through_lumping`, `values_lumped`). It is right   about what matters only where what matters goes alike across that lumping   (`selects_right_through`, `values_right_lumped`): where what matters separates two   alternatives the map lumps, none is right (`selects_not_right_of_parted`,   `values_not_right_of_parted`); where what matters is read off the map, reading it through the   map is right (`selects_right_of_reads`, `values_right_of_reads`). | ALT |
| /-! ## Selection through a model of the options  A selector tells options apart through its model of them, an entry for each option (`Becoming.Selection`, namespace `Relabel`); the model lumps together the options it gives one entry. A selection through the model is a selection through that lumping, and it is right about what matters only where what matters goes alike across the lumping. -/ | /-! ## Selection through a map of the alternatives  A selector tells alternatives apart through its map of them, an entry for each alternative (`Becoming.Selection`, namespace `Relabel`); the map lumps together the alternatives it gives one entry. A selection through the map is a selection through that lumping, and it is right about what matters only where what matters goes alike across the lumping. -/ | ALT |
| /-- **A selection through a model is a selection through its lumping.** Options the model `r` lumps together are selected alike. -/ | /-- **A selection through a map is a selection through its lumping.** Alternatives the map `r` lumps together are selected alike. -/ | ALT |
| /-- **A selection through a model is right about what matters only where what matters goes alike across the model's lumping.** If a selection through `r` selects exactly the options satisfying `p`, then `p` holds alike of options `r` lumps together. -/ | /-- **A selection through a map is right about what matters only where what matters goes alike across the map's lumping.** If a selection through `r` selects exactly the alternatives satisfying `p`, then `p` holds alike of alternatives `r` lumps together. -/ | ALT |
| /-- **What a model lumps, no selection through it can separate.** Where `p` holds of one of two options the model `r` lumps together and not of the other, no selection through `r` selects exactly the options satisfying `p`: that takes a finer model. -/ | /-- **What a map lumps, no selection through it can separate.** Where `p` holds of one of two alternatives the map `r` lumps together and not of the other, no selection through `r` selects exactly the alternatives satisfying `p`: that takes a finer map. -/ | ALT |
| /-- **A model reading what matters selects it.** Where `p` is read off the model `r` through `q`, the selection reading every model through `q` is a selection through the model, and it selects exactly the options satisfying `p`. -/ | /-- **A map reading what matters selects it.** Where `p` is read off the map `r` through `q`, the selection reading every map through `q` is a selection through the map, and it selects exactly the alternatives satisfying `p`. -/ | ALT |
| /-- A valuation through a model gives every option the value `f` that matters only where `f` goes alike across the model's lumping. -/ | /-- A valuation through a map gives every alternative the value `f` that matters only where `f` goes alike across the map's lumping. -/ | ALT |
| /-- Where `f` parts two options the model `r` lumps together, no valuation through `r` gives every option its value under `f`. -/ | /-- Where `f` parts two alternatives the map `r` lumps together, no valuation through `r` gives every alternative its value under `f`. -/ | ALT |
| /-- Where `f` is read off the model `r` through `h`, the valuation reading every model through `h` is a valuation through the model, and it gives every option its value under `f`. -/ | /-- Where `f` is read off the map `r` through `h`, the valuation reading every map through `h` is a valuation through the map, and it gives every alternative its value under `f`. -/ | ALT |

## `lean:SelectionRuns`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| # Selection along runs: a selection through a run's registered course  `Becoming.Selection` states what a selection through a model of the options can separate: an entry per option, a selection through the model exchanging what it selects when two options' entries are exchanged (`Relabel.Equivariant`, `Relabel.EquivariantValue`, `Relabel.EquivariantChoice`). `Becoming.Learning` states that such a selection is right about what matters only where what matters goes alike across the model's lumping. This module reads the options as the moments of a run of any process and the model as what a registration registers at each of them: the run's registered course (`Parting.course`). The laws are then laws of the run: of which of its moments any selection through what registers along it can tell apart.  * **A selection through the registered course separates exactly what registers apart.** Two   moments of a run are selected alike by every selection through its registered course exactly   when the registration registers them alike (`selected_alike_iff`), and valued alike by every   valuation through it exactly then (`valued_alike_iff`). A moment is singled out by some   selection of at most one moment exactly when no other moment registers alike   (`singled_iff_untied`); a choice of one moment chooses only such a moment (`choice_untied`). * **Where a run registers one value throughout, nothing is singled out.** On such a run, as on   a run at rest, every valuation through the course values all moments alike   (`values_alike_of_constant`) and every choice of one moment through it withholds   (`choice_withholds_of_constant`). The moments a valuation gives one value are selected alike   where they register alike (`value_level_alike`). * **Right about a target only where the target goes alike across the registration.** A   selection or valuation through the registered course is through what registers alike along   the run (`selects_through_coupling`, `values_lumped`). Where a target on configurations   separates two moments that register alike, no selection through the course selects exactly   the moments meeting it, and no valuation through it gives each moment its value   (`selects_not_right_of_parted`, `values_not_right_of_parted`); where the target is read off   the registration, reading it through the course is right (`selects_right_of_reads`,   `values_right_of_reads`). | # Selection along runs: a selection through a run's course through a map  `Becoming.Selection` states what a selection through a map of the alternatives can separate: an entry per alternative, a selection through the map exchanging what it selects when two alternatives' entries are exchanged (`Relabel.Equivariant`, `Relabel.EquivariantValue`, `Relabel.EquivariantChoice`). `Becoming.Learning` states that such a selection is right about what matters only where what matters goes alike across the map's lumping. This module reads the alternatives as the moments of a run of any process and the map of them as the values of a map `reg` at each of them: the run's course through the map (`Parting.course`). The laws are then laws of the run: of which of its moments any selection through its course through the map can tell apart.  * **A selection through the course separates exactly what the map separates.** Two moments of a   run are selected alike by every selection through its course through the map exactly when the   map takes one value at them (`selected_alike_iff`), and valued alike by every valuation   through it exactly then (`valued_alike_iff`). A moment is singled out by some selection of at   most one moment exactly when no other moment takes its value (`singled_iff_untied`); a choice   of one moment chooses only such a moment (`choice_untied`). * **Where a run takes one value throughout, nothing is singled out.** On such a run, as on   a run at rest, every valuation through the course values all moments alike   (`values_alike_of_constant`) and every choice of one moment through it withholds   (`choice_withholds_of_constant`). The moments a valuation gives one value are selected alike   where they take one value through the map (`value_level_alike`). * **Right about a feature only where the feature goes alike across the map's lumping.** A   selection or valuation through the course is through the lumping the map makes along the run   (`selects_through_coupling`, `values_lumped`). Where a feature on configurations separates two   moments the map lumps, no selection through the course selects exactly the moments meeting it,   and no valuation through it gives each moment its value (`selects_not_right_of_parted`,   `values_not_right_of_parted`); where the feature is read off the map, reading it through the   course is right (`selects_right_of_reads`, `values_right_of_reads`). | S1+L-feature+ALT |
| /-- **Two moments of a run are selected alike by every selection through its registered course exactly when the registration registers them alike.** -/ | /-- **Two moments of a run are selected alike by every selection through its course through the map exactly when the map takes one value at them.** -/ | S1 |
| /-- **Two moments of a run are valued alike by every valuation through its registered course exactly when the registration registers them alike.** -/ | /-- **Two moments of a run are valued alike by every valuation through its course through the map exactly when the map takes one value at them.** -/ | S1 |
| /-- **A moment of a run is singled out exactly where it registers apart from every other.** Some selection through the registered course, selecting at most one moment at each course, selects `k` exactly when no other moment of the run registers as `k` does. -/ | /-- **A moment of a run is singled out exactly where the map sets it apart from every other.** Some selection through the course, selecting at most one moment at each course, selects `k` exactly when no other moment of the run takes `k`'s value through the map. -/ | S1 |
| /-- **A choice of one moment through the registered course chooses a moment registering apart from every other.** -/ | /-- **A choice of one moment through the course chooses a moment the map sets apart from every other.** -/ | S1 |
| /-- **Along a run registering one value throughout, a choice of one moment through the registered course withholds.** So it does along a run at rest. -/ | /-- **Along a run taking one value throughout, a choice of one moment through the course withholds.** So it does along a run at rest. -/ | TEXT |
| /-- **Along a run registering one value throughout, every valuation through the registered course values all moments alike.** -/ | /-- **Along a run taking one value throughout, every valuation through the course values all moments alike.** -/ | TEXT |
| /-- **The moments a valuation through the registered course gives one value are selected alike where they register alike.** -/ | /-- **The moments a valuation through the course gives one value are selected alike where they take one value through the map.** -/ | S1 |
| /-- **A selection through the registered course is through what registers alike along the run.** -/ | /-- **A selection through the course is through the lumping the map makes along the run.** -/ | S1 |
| /-- **A valuation through the registered course goes alike across what registers alike along the run.** -/ | /-- **A valuation through the course goes alike across the lumping the map makes along the run.** -/ | S1 |
| /-- **Where a target separates two moments that register alike, no selection through the registered course selects exactly the moments meeting it.** That takes a finer registration. -/ | /-- **Where a feature separates two moments the map lumps, no selection through the course selects exactly the moments meeting it.** That takes a finer map. -/ | S1+L-feature |

## `lean:Selection`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| # Selection: a selection through registration reaches exactly what registers | # Selection: a selection through a formation's couplings reaches exactly what couples in | S2 |
| go: "Great, let's finish all of them". This module states, with no premise beyond what each law names, what a selection decided by a formation's registration can tell apart. A selection is any claim about level histories, read as which histories are kept, rewarded or leave more descendants. It is through a selector's registration up to a step when the histories the selector registers alike up to that step are selected alike (`Web.SelectsThrough`); a score is through it when they score alike (`Web.ScoresThrough`). That a selection is through registration is supplied, never derived.  * **A selection reaches exactly what registers.** Two histories are selected alike by every   selection through `f`'s registration up to `t` exactly when `f` registers them alike up to `t`   (`Web.selects_alike_iff`). The belief of `Becoming.Testing` is such a selection   (`Web.class_selects`), so where `f` registers two histories apart, a selection through its   registration holds at one and fails at the other (`Web.separates`); registering alike is an   equivalence (`Web.AlikeThroughUpTo.trans`). A selection through registration is a claim its   selector tests completely, refuted exactly where it fails (`Web.SelectsThrough.excludes_iff`).   Admission and the levels of observed conditions are selected and scored through registration   (`Web.admits_through`, `Web.observed_scores`); a selection through registration stays so at   later steps, under more observation, under negation and under conjunction   (`Web.SelectsThrough.later`, `Web.SelectsThrough.widen`, `Web.SelectsThrough.not`,   `Web.SelectsThrough.and`), and a score's thresholds are selections   (`Web.ScoresThrough.atLeast`). For scores the same law holds where registering alike with the   first history is decidable, a hypothesis named at that point (`Web.scores_alike_iff`). * **Groups.** A selection through a group's pooled registration is one that selects alike the   histories every member registers alike (`Web.SelectsThroughAll`); the same law holds of it   (`Web.selects_alike_all_iff`, `Web.separates_all`). A member's selection is a group selection   (`Web.SelectsThrough.all`), and group selections are closed under conjunction   (`Web.SelectsThroughAll.and`). * **Blind beyond the registration closure.** With the rest of the exchange fixed, every   selection and every score through the registration of a formation within `f`'s registration   closure selects the levels with and without a part beyond the closure alike   (`Flow.unselected_beyond_coupling`, `Flow.unscored_beyond_coupling`); so does every   selection through the pooled registration of a group within the closure   (`Flow.unselected_beyond_coupling_all`). Where the members respond, under the hypotheses of   `Flow.alike_within_coupling`, it selects the two flows alike   (`Flow.unselected_beyond_coupling_responsive`). * **The gap.** In `Clearing` the dweller lies within the clearer's reach closure and beyond its   registration closure, and the clearer does not register the habitat   (`Clearing.dweller_within_reach`). Every selection through the clearer's registration selects   the cleared and the spared world alike at every step, while the dweller ends in the one and   not the other; that the dweller ends is selected through the clearer's registration at no step   (`Clearing.unselected`). The dweller's admission at the third step is a selection through the   dweller's registration and separates the two worlds (`Clearing.selected_by_dweller`); the   admission of both at the third step is a selection through the pooled registration of the   clearer and the dweller that separates them and is through neither alone   (`Clearing.selected_pooled`). * **Selection favours reaching beyond.** In `Diversion` a taker gauges a store. In the diverting   flow it takes one unit from a source a dweller depends on and gives it to the store; in the   undiverted flow nothing is exchanged. The selection that the store stands at least one at the   second step is through the taker's registration, holds in the diverting flow and fails in the   undiverted one, while the diverting flow reaches the dweller, beyond the taker's registration   closure, and ends it (`Diversion.favours`). Every selection through the taker's registration   selects the diverting flow alike with a supplying flow, which raises the store without drawing   on the source and reaches nothing beyond the taker (`Diversion.blind_to_source`). | go: "Great, let's finish all of them". This module states, with no premise beyond what each law names, what a selection decided by what couples into a formation can tell apart. A selection is any claim about level histories, read as which histories are kept, rewarded or leave more descendants. It is through a selector's couplings up to a step when the histories alike through them up to that step are selected alike (`Web.SelectsThrough`); a score is through them when they score alike (`Web.ScoresThrough`). That a selection is through a selector's couplings is supplied, never derived.  * **A selection reaches exactly what couples in.** Two histories are selected alike by every   selection through `f`'s couplings up to `t` exactly when they are alike through `f`'s couplings   up to `t` (`Web.selects_alike_iff`). The class alike through `f` of `Becoming.Testing` is such   a selection (`Web.class_selects`), so where two histories are apart through `f`'s couplings, a   selection through them holds at one and fails at the other (`Web.separates`); alike through the   couplings is an equivalence (`Web.AlikeThroughUpTo.trans`). A selection through a selector's   couplings is a claim the selector tests completely, excluded exactly where it fails   (`Web.SelectsThrough.excludes_iff`). Admission and the levels of conditions coupled in by level   are selected and scored through the couplings (`Web.admits_through`, `Web.observed_scores`); a   selection through the couplings stays so at later steps, under more coupling by level, under   negation and under conjunction (`Web.SelectsThrough.later`, `Web.SelectsThrough.widen`,   `Web.SelectsThrough.not`, `Web.SelectsThrough.and`), and a score's thresholds are selections   (`Web.ScoresThrough.atLeast`). For scores the same law holds where being alike with the   first history is decidable, a hypothesis named at that point (`Web.scores_alike_iff`). * **Groups.** A selection through a group's pooled couplings is one that selects alike the   histories alike through every member's couplings (`Web.SelectsThroughAll`); the same law holds   of it (`Web.selects_alike_all_iff`, `Web.separates_all`). A member's selection is a group   selection (`Web.SelectsThrough.all`), and group selections are closed under conjunction   (`Web.SelectsThroughAll.and`). * **Blind beyond the coupling closure.** With the rest of the exchange fixed, every   selection and every score through the couplings of a formation within `f`'s coupling   closure selects the levels with and without a part beyond the closure alike   (`Flow.unselected_beyond_coupling`, `Flow.unscored_beyond_coupling`); so does every   selection through the pooled couplings of a group within the closure   (`Flow.unselected_beyond_coupling_all`). Where the members respond, under the hypotheses of   `Flow.alike_within_coupling`, it selects the two flows alike   (`Flow.unselected_beyond_coupling_responsive`). * **The gap.** In `Clearing` the dweller lies within the clearer's reach closure and beyond its   coupling closure, and the habitat does not couple into the clearer   (`Clearing.dweller_within_reach`). Every selection through the clearer's couplings selects   the cleared and the spared world alike at every step, while the dweller ends in the one and   not the other; that the dweller ends is selected through the clearer's couplings at no step   (`Clearing.unselected`). The dweller's admission at the third step is a selection through the   dweller's couplings and separates the two worlds (`Clearing.selected_by_dweller`); the   admission of both at the third step is a selection through the pooled couplings of the   clearer and the dweller that separates them and is through neither alone   (`Clearing.selected_pooled`). * **Selection favours reaching beyond.** In `Diversion` a taker gauges a store. In the diverting   flow it takes one unit from a source a dweller depends on and gives it to the store; in the   undiverted flow nothing is exchanged. The selection that the store stands at least one at the   second step is through the taker's couplings, holds in the diverting flow and fails in the   undiverted one, while the diverting flow reaches the dweller, beyond the taker's coupling   closure, and ends it (`Diversion.favours`). Every selection through the taker's couplings   selects the diverting flow alike with a supplying flow, which raises the store without drawing   on the source and reaches nothing beyond the taker (`Diversion.blind_to_source`). | S4+S3+S2+S5 |
| * **Through a model of the options.** A selector tells options apart only through its model of   them: an entry for each option, of any kind; options given one entry are tied, lumped together   by the model. A selection is through the model when exchanging two options' entries exchanges   what it selects (`Relabel.Equivariant`). Two options are selected alike by every such selection   exactly when the model ties them (`Relabel.selected_alike_iff`), as a selection through   registration reaches exactly what registers. One selecting at most one option singles out   exactly the options whose entry no other option shares (`Relabel.singled_iff_untied`, the   selection `Relabel.Alone`), and at a full tie of two or more options it selects none   (`Relabel.Equivariant.none_of_full_tie`). A valuation through the model, such as a weight on   each option, gives tied options equal values (`Relabel.EquivariantValue.tied`,   `Relabel.valued_alike_iff`), and a choice of one option chooses no tied option and withholds at   a full tie (`Relabel.EquivariantChoice.untied`, `Relabel.EquivariantChoice.withholds_of_full_tie`).   Exchanging two options tells them apart: their equality is decided, a condition each statement   names. The model is a lumping (`Lumped` of `Becoming.Learning`), and a selection through it is | * **Through a map of the alternatives.** A selector tells alternatives apart only through its map   of them: an entry for each alternative, of any kind; alternatives given one entry are tied,   lumped together by the map. A selection factors through the map when exchanging two   alternatives' entries exchanges what it selects (`Relabel.Equivariant`). Two alternatives are   selected alike by every such selection exactly when the map ties them   (`Relabel.selected_alike_iff`), as a selection through a formation's couplings reaches exactly   what couples in. One selecting at most one alternative singles out exactly the alternatives   whose entry no other alternative shares (`Relabel.singled_iff_untied`, the selection   `Relabel.Alone`), and at a full tie of two or more alternatives it selects none   (`Relabel.Equivariant.none_of_full_tie`). A valuation through the map, such as a weight on   each alternative, gives tied alternatives equal values (`Relabel.EquivariantValue.tied`,   `Relabel.valued_alike_iff`), and a choice of one alternative chooses no tied alternative and   withholds at a full tie (`Relabel.EquivariantChoice.untied`,   `Relabel.EquivariantChoice.withholds_of_full_tie`). Exchanging two alternatives tells them   apart: their equality is decided, a condition each statement names. The map is a lumping   (`Lumped` of `Becoming.Learning`), and a selection through it is | S2+S1+ALT |
| - Any selection decided by what a selector registers (keeping, rewarding, reproducing,   training) reaches exactly what that selector registers. It is blind to whatever a candidate's   part does beyond the selector's registration closure, however far the candidate's reach   closure extends. The gap is the reach closure less the registration closure. - The gap closes only through relations: dependence, observation, or another's registration   pooled, as the dweller's is in `Clearing`. Pooling with formations within the closure does not   close it (`Flow.unselected_beyond_coupling_all`). - Where reaching beyond raises what registers, selection favours it (`Diversion`), and cannot   tell it from raising what registers without reaching beyond. With `share_rises` the share of   what reaches beyond rises among descendants while the world is open; enclosed, the selection   stops with the descent (`Flow.descent_exhausted` of `Becoming.Capture`). - Read for AI training, a reward process is a selector: training reaches only what the reward   registers. The AI connects this with Ren's phrase from an earlier paper, "capability outruns   correction": what is selected through a registration can reach beyond it, and correction by   that registration does not reach there.  What is not derived: who selects, what the selector registers, that a selection is through registration at all (supplied as `Web.SelectsThrough`), that offspring follow the selection, that reaching beyond is chosen, that a selection is through a given model, and how an option it singles out is found (a search over listed options). The law for scores in the converse direction takes the decidability of registering alike as a hypothesis. No ethical predicate, ranking or axiom occurs.  **Classical sources.** A selection through a model of the options that commutes with relabelling (`Relabel.Equivariant`) is neutral in the sense of May (1952): it treats the options alike under every permutation that the model does not tell apart, so that at a full tie it selects none, as May's neutrality forces a tie between alternatives that are interchanged. | - Any selection decided by what couples into a selector (keeping, rewarding, reproducing,   training) reaches exactly what couples into that selector. It is blind to whatever a   candidate's part does beyond the selector's coupling closure, however far the candidate's   reach closure extends. The gap is the reach closure less the coupling closure. - The gap closes only through relations: dependence, coupling by level, or another's couplings   pooled, as the dweller's are in `Clearing`. Pooling with formations within the closure does not   close it (`Flow.unselected_beyond_coupling_all`). - Where reaching beyond raises what couples in, selection favours it (`Diversion`), and cannot   tell it from raising what couples in without reaching beyond. With `share_rises` the share of   what reaches beyond rises among descendants while the world is open; enclosed, the selection   stops with the descent (`Flow.descent_exhausted` of `Becoming.Capture`). - Read for AI training, a reward process is a selector: training reaches only what couples into   the reward. The AI connects this with Ren's phrase from an earlier paper, "capability outruns   correction": what is selected through a selector's couplings can reach beyond them, and   correction through those couplings does not reach there.  What is not derived: who selects, what couples into the selector, that a selection is through its couplings at all (supplied as `Web.SelectsThrough`), that offspring follow the selection, that reaching beyond is chosen, that a selection factors through a given map, and how an alternative it singles out is found (a search over listed alternatives). The law for scores in the converse direction takes the decidability of being alike through the couplings as a hypothesis. No ethical predicate, ranking or axiom occurs.  **Classical sources.** A selection through a map of the alternatives that commutes with relabelling (`Relabel.Equivariant`) is neutral in the sense of May (1952): it treats the alternatives alike under every permutation that the map does not tell apart, so that at a full tie it selects none, as May's neutrality forces a tie between alternatives that are interchanged. | S4+S3+S2+S1+ALT |

| /-- **A selection reaches exactly what registers.** Two histories are selected alike by every selection through `g`'s registration up to `t` exactly when `g` registers them alike up to `t`. -/ | /-- **A selection reaches exactly what couples in.** Two histories are selected alike by every selection through `g`'s couplings up to `t` exactly when they are alike through `g`'s couplings up to `t`. -/ | S3+S2 |
| /-- **What registers apart is separated.** Where `g` does not register two histories alike up to `t`, a selection through its registration, its belief, holds at the first and fails at the second. -/ | /-- **What is apart through the couplings is separated.** Where two histories are not alike through `g`'s couplings up to `t`, a selection through them, the class alike through `g`, holds at the first and fails at the second. -/ | S3+S2+S5 |
| /-- **A selection through registration is tested completely.** Its selector refutes it exactly where it fails. -/ | /-- **A selection through a selector's couplings is tested completely.** It is excluded at its selector exactly where it fails. -/ | S2+S5 |

| /-- A score's threshold is a selection through the same registration. -/ | /-- A score's threshold is a selection through the same couplings. -/ | S2 |
