| /-! ## Selection through a model of the options  A selector tells options apart only through its model of them: an entry per option, of any kind, the options given one entry being tied, lumped together by the model (`Lumped` of `Becoming.Learning`). A selection among them is through the model when exchanging two options' entries exchanges what it selects (`Relabel.Equivariant`); a valuation of the options, such as a weight on each, is through the model when it exchanges their values (`Relabel.EquivariantValue`); a choice of at most one option is when it exchanges the choice (`Relabel.EquivariantChoice`). As a selection through registration reaches exactly what registers (`Web.selects_alike_iff`), a selection through a model separates exactly what the model separates. -/ | /-! ## Selection through a map of the alternatives  A selector tells alternatives apart only through its map of them: an entry per alternative, of any kind, the alternatives given one entry being tied, lumped together by the map (`Lumped` of `Becoming.Learning`). A selection among them factors through the map when exchanging two alternatives' entries exchanges what it selects (`Relabel.Equivariant`); a valuation of the alternatives, such as a weight on each, factors through the map when it exchanges their values (`Relabel.EquivariantValue`); a choice of at most one alternative does when it exchanges the choice (`Relabel.EquivariantChoice`). As a selection through a formation's couplings reaches exactly what couples in (`Web.selects_alike_iff`), a selection through a map separates exactly what the map separates. -/ | S2+S1+ALT |

| # Testing: what registers in a formation, and what it can test | # Testing: what couples into a formation, and what it can test | S2 |
| module states, with no premise beyond balance on the conditions registered and the representation, what a formation can register, what a claim needs in order to be tested by it, and where a difference stays unregistered. No significance, ranking or axiom occurs. The responsive form of reach blindness, the exact threshold laws, refutation relative to a model, pooled testing, the bridge to | module states, with no premise beyond balance on the conditions coupled into a formation and the representation, what can couple into a formation, what a claim needs in order to be tested by it, and where a difference does not pass. No significance, ranking or axiom occurs. The responsive form of reach blindness, the exact threshold laws, exclusion relative to a model, pooled testing, the bridge to | S2+S5 |
| * **Registration.** A formation registers its support, whether the levels admit it, and the   levels of the conditions it observes (`obs`). Under two level histories it registers alike at a   step when the levels admit it alike and its observed conditions stand alike   (`Web.AlikeThrough`), and alike up to a step when it does so at every step up to it   (`Web.AlikeThroughUpTo`). A condition it depends on and does not observe registers only   whether it meets the need (`Web.alike_of_threshold`); an observed one registers its level   (`Web.observed_level_couples`). Observing more distinguishes more   (`Web.AlikeThroughUpTo.of_obs`). The base observation is support only (`thresholdOnly`). In the   one-kind reading a formation's own standing then registers through support where it bears on   itself (`Web.SelfBearing`); a gauge of its own standing (`ownStanding`) is an observation   premise, as a gauge of a stock is. * **Refutation.** A claim about level histories is refuted by a formation at a step when no   history meeting it is registered alike up to that step (`Web.Excludes`), and relative to a model,   a class of admissible histories, when no admissible history meeting it is (`Web.ExcludesWithin`):   the refutation of the model and the claim together (`Web.excludesWithin_iff_conjunction`). A   claim is refuted only through a difference in what registers (`Web.Excludes.not_of_alike`); what   holds is never refuted, and within a model only where the model admits what holds   (`Web.Excludes.not_meets`, `Web.ExcludesWithin.not_meets`); a refutation stands at every later   step (`Web.Excludes.later`); observing more refutes more (`Web.Excludes.widen`). Without a model,   no finite record refutes a claim about what follows it (`Web.tail_unexcluded`). With a model of   the exchange, a formation observing a stock refutes at the first step every false claim that it   comes to nothing (`Flow.excludes_tail_within`); a wrong model refutes what holds   (`Stationary.model_error`), and registration refutes the model (`Stationary.model_excluded`). A   formation's belief is the histories it registers alike with the actual one   (`Web.ClassThrough`, `Web.class_excludes_iff`); a judgment from it is a common response of   `Becoming.Inquiry`, kept as later steps and more observation narrow the belief   (`Web.class_judge_iff`, `Web.class_judge_later`, `Web.class_judge_widen`). * **Reach blindness.** A formation registers a condition when it depends on it or observes it   (`Web.CoupledInto`). The registration closure is the closure under exchanging with a condition   registered (`Flow.WithinCoupling`). It holds the influence closure of `Becoming.Feedback`,   is that closure where the formation registers support only, and grows with observation and   with exchange (`Flow.withinCoupling_of_withinInfluence`,   `Flow.withinCoupling_thresholdOnly`, `Flow.withinCoupling_mono_obs`,   `Flow.withinCoupling_mono_flow`). Let two flows with the same formations present balance on   each condition the members of a region register (`Flow.BalancesOn`), let the region be closed   in both under being affected through what is registered, let the levels start alike on what its   members register, and let each member's exchange on what members register follow its own   registration. Then every member registers alike at every step, whatever is done beyond the   region or on what no member registers (`Flow.alike_within_closed`,   `Flow.alike_within_coupling`). With the rest of the exchange fixed this is P42's form for   every observation (`Flow.uncoupled_beyond_coupling`): no claim met by the levels without   a part beyond the closure is refuted within it, a refutation there rests on a model of the   part's exchange, and a judgment there holds with and without the part   (`Flow.unexcluded_beyond_coupling`, `Flow.beyond_needs_model`, `Flow.class_judge_beyond`). * **Testing is relational.** In `Clearing` the clearer registers alike at every step whether or   not its clearing ends the dweller (`Clearing.alike_for_clearer`, an instance of   `alike_within_closed`). In the spared world the claim that the dweller is ended is refuted by   the dweller at the third step and by the clearer at no step (`Clearing.tested_by_dweller`); a   claim neither refutes, the two together refute (`Clearing.pooled`); with a model fixing the   start and the exchange, the clearer refutes what it does not register, and its registration   does no work (`Clearing.model_idles_coupling`). * **Threshold blindness.** Let two flows balance on each condition `g` registers, the exchange   there following `g`'s registration (`Flow.ExchangeFollows`). While `g` registers alike, every   difference on a condition it registers stays as it was at the start (`Flow.difference_kept`).   Where the second web starts at least as high on what `g` registers and alike on what it   observes, `g` registers alike at every step before which the first web supported it   (`Flow.alike_while_supported`), and a difference changes only after a step at which the second   web admitted `g` and the first did not support it (`Flow.difference_changes_after_divergence`).   For one stock `d` higher that `g` depends on and does not observe: it stands `d` higher, and   `g` registers alike, at every step before which the first web supported `g` at every step   (`Flow.subthreshold_alike`, `Flow.subthreshold_alike_always`). Both webs failing together, the difference can stay   for ever (`JointFailure.difference_kept`). `SupportDrawn` is a witness in which the difference   changes (`SupportDrawn.difference_waits`). Observing the stock lifts this: the formation of   `Gauged` registers the two webs apart at the first step and draws on the higher stock while   supported in both (`Gauged.gauge_changes_difference`). Where others respond to the stock itself   the hypothesis fails, and the higher stock can end `g` first (`Attracting.higher_breaks_first`).   On a prey that bears on itself, a bite registers through support only where it brings the prey   below its need, and a gauge of itself registers every bite (`SelfPrey.threshold_on_oneself`). | * **Coupling into a formation.** Into a formation couple its support, whether the levels admit   it, and the levels of the conditions coupled into it by level (`obs`). Under two level   histories they are alike through its couplings at a step when the levels admit it alike and the   conditions coupled into it by level stand alike (`Web.AlikeThrough`), and alike up to a step   when they are so at every step up to it (`Web.AlikeThroughUpTo`). A condition coupled into it   by threshold only passes only whether it meets the need (`Web.alike_of_threshold`); one coupled   by level passes its level (`Web.observed_level_couples`). More coupling by level distinguishes   more (`Web.AlikeThroughUpTo.of_obs`). The base coupling is by threshold only   (`thresholdOnly`). In the one-kind reading a formation's own standing then couples into it   through support where it bears on itself (`Web.SelfBearing`); a gauge of its own standing   (`ownStanding`) is a supplied coupling by level, as a gauge of a stock is. * **Exclusion.** A claim about level histories is excluded at a formation at a step when no   history meeting it is alike through the formation's couplings up to that step (`Web.Excludes`),   and relative to a model, a class of admissible histories, when no admissible history meeting it   is (`Web.ExcludesWithin`): the exclusion of the model and the claim together   (`Web.excludesWithin_iff_conjunction`). A claim is excluded only through a difference that   couples into the formation (`Web.Excludes.not_of_alike`); what holds is never excluded, and   within a model only where the model admits what holds (`Web.Excludes.not_meets`,   `Web.ExcludesWithin.not_meets`); an exclusion stands at every later step (`Web.Excludes.later`);   more coupling by level excludes more (`Web.Excludes.widen`). Without a model, no finite prefix   of levels excludes a claim about what follows it (`Web.tail_unexcluded`). With a model of the   exchange, a formation into which a stock couples by level excludes at the first step every   false claim that the stock comes to nothing (`Flow.excludes_tail_within`); a wrong model   excludes what holds (`Stationary.model_error`), and what couples in excludes the model   (`Stationary.model_excluded`). The class alike through a formation's couplings is the   histories alike with the actual one through them (`Web.ClassThrough`,   `Web.class_excludes_iff`); a judgment from it is a common response of `Becoming.Inquiry`,   kept as later steps and more coupling by level narrow the class (`Web.class_judge_iff`,   `Web.class_judge_later`, `Web.class_judge_widen`). * **Reach blindness.** A condition couples into a formation when the formation depends on it or   it is coupled into the formation by level (`Web.CoupledInto`). The coupling closure is the   closure under exchanging with a condition coupled in (`Flow.WithinCoupling`). It holds the   influence closure of `Becoming.Feedback`, is that closure where coupling is by threshold only,   and grows with coupling by level and with exchange (`Flow.withinCoupling_of_withinInfluence`,   `Flow.withinCoupling_thresholdOnly`, `Flow.withinCoupling_mono_obs`,   `Flow.withinCoupling_mono_flow`). Let two flows with the same formations present balance on   each condition coupled into a member of a region (`Flow.BalancesOn`), let the region be closed   in both under being affected through what couples in, let the levels start alike on what couples   into its members, and let each member's exchange on what couples into members follow what   couples into it. Then every member is alike through its couplings at every step, whatever is   done beyond the region or on what couples into no member (`Flow.alike_within_closed`,   `Flow.alike_within_coupling`). With the rest of the exchange fixed this is P42's form for   every coupling by level (`Flow.uncoupled_beyond_coupling`): no claim met by the levels without   a part beyond the closure is excluded within it, an exclusion there rests on a model of the   part's exchange, and a judgment there holds with and without the part   (`Flow.unexcluded_beyond_coupling`, `Flow.beyond_needs_model`, `Flow.class_judge_beyond`). * **Testing is relational.** In `Clearing` the clearer is alike through its couplings at every   step whether or not its clearing ends the dweller (`Clearing.alike_for_clearer`, an instance of   `alike_within_closed`). In the spared world the claim that the dweller is ended is excluded at   the dweller at the third step and at the clearer at no step (`Clearing.tested_by_dweller`); a   claim excluded at neither is excluded at the two together (`Clearing.pooled`); with a model   fixing the start and the exchange, the clearer excludes what does not couple into it, and its   couplings do no work (`Clearing.model_idles_coupling`). * **Threshold blindness.** Let two flows balance on each condition coupled into `g`, the exchange   there following what couples into `g` (`Flow.ExchangeFollows`). While the flows are alike   through `g`'s couplings, every difference on a condition coupled into `g` stays as it was at the   start (`Flow.difference_kept`). Where the second web starts at least as high on what couples   into `g` and alike on what is coupled into it by level, the flows are alike through `g`'s   couplings at every step before which the first web supported it (`Flow.alike_while_supported`),   and a difference changes only after a step at which the second web admitted `g` and the first   did not support it (`Flow.difference_changes_after_divergence`). For one stock `d` higher that   couples into `g` by threshold only: it stands `d` higher, and the flows are alike through `g`'s   couplings, at every step before which the first web supported `g` at every step   (`Flow.subthreshold_alike`, `Flow.subthreshold_alike_always`). Both webs failing together, the   difference can stay for ever (`JointFailure.difference_kept`). `SupportDrawn` is a witness in   which the difference changes (`SupportDrawn.difference_waits`). Coupling the stock by level   lifts this: the two webs are apart through the couplings of `Gauged`'s formation at the first   step, and it draws on the higher stock while supported in both   (`Gauged.gauge_changes_difference`). Where others respond to the stock itself the hypothesis   fails, and the higher stock can end `g` first (`Attracting.higher_breaks_first`). On a prey that   bears on itself, a bite passes through support only where it brings the prey below its need, and   a gauge of itself passes every bite (`SelfPrey.threshold_on_oneself`). | S4+S3+S2+S5+L-prefix |
|   exchange follows registration (`subthreshold_alike`); beyond the horizon, where the continuation   is open (`Becoming.Horizon`); and beyond the registration closure (`alike_within_closed`). They   are not the only ones: support registers only net exchange (`Flow.Balances.agrees_iff_even`),   and one joint bit across the conditions required. - A runaway is a loop that runs inside one of them. This is a heuristic, not a statement: no loop   or gain is defined, and where others respond to a stock its loss can register sooner   (`Attracting`). - Measurement registers a level below the threshold (`Web.observed_level_couples`); a model of   the exchange crosses the horizon relative to itself and answers to registration   (`Flow.excludes_tail_within`, `Stationary`); new observation and new exchange widen the closure   (`Flow.withinCoupling_mono_obs`, `Flow.withinCoupling_mono_flow`); others' registration   tests what one cannot (`Clearing.tested_by_dweller`, `Clearing.pooled`), and reaches one only   through a relation that is itself an exchange or an observation. - Falsifiability is the condition of being corrected by the unfolding: a claim and its rivals   must differ in what can register (`Web.Excludes.not_of_alike`). It is relational and moves with   what is registered. Without a model no finite record tests a claim about what follows it; with   one, the test is of the model and the claim together. - Read with Ren's 心 and 理, 理 is a model answerable to registration: it narrows the histories   admitted, so that the same registration decides more; it can mislead, and registration corrects   it.  What is not derived: - that a formation observes anything beyond its support, gauges its own standing, forms a model,   or wants to avoid a runaway; - what a registration is about: registering alike bounds what can be told apart, and says nothing   of what a difference signifies; - what an act does: a claim about it compares two histories, and is tested only relative to a   model of the history without the act (`Flow.beyond_needs_model`,   `Clearing.model_idles_coupling`); varying one's own act and registering the difference is   open; |   exchange follows what couples in (`subthreshold_alike`); beyond the horizon, where the   continuation is open (`Becoming.Horizon`); and beyond the coupling closure   (`alike_within_closed`). They are not the only ones: support passes only net exchange   (`Flow.Balances.agrees_iff_even`), and one joint bit across the conditions required. - A runaway is a loop that runs inside one of them. This is a heuristic, not a statement: no loop   or gain is defined, and where others respond to a stock its loss can couple in sooner   (`Attracting`). - Measurement couples a level into a formation below the threshold   (`Web.observed_level_couples`); a model of the exchange crosses the horizon relative to itself   and answers to what couples in (`Flow.excludes_tail_within`, `Stationary`); new coupling by   level and new exchange widen the closure (`Flow.withinCoupling_mono_obs`,   `Flow.withinCoupling_mono_flow`); what couples into others tests what does not couple into one   (`Clearing.tested_by_dweller`, `Clearing.pooled`), and reaches one only through a relation that   is itself an exchange or a coupling by level. - Falsifiability is the condition of being corrected by the unfolding: a claim and its rivals   must differ in what can couple in (`Web.Excludes.not_of_alike`). It is relational and moves with   what couples in. Without a model no finite prefix tests a claim about what follows it; with   one, the test is of the model and the claim together. - Read with Ren's 心 and 理, 理 is a model answerable to what couples in: it narrows the histories   admitted, so that the same couplings decide more; it can mislead, and what couples in corrects   it.  What is not derived: - that anything couples into a formation by level beyond its support, that it gauges its own   standing, forms a model, or wants to avoid a runaway; - what a coupling is about: being alike through the couplings bounds what can be told apart, and   says nothing of what a difference signifies; - what an act does: a claim about it compares two histories, and is tested only relative to a   model of the history without the act (`Flow.beyond_needs_model`,   `Clearing.model_idles_coupling`); varying one's own act and the difference coupling back is   open; | S4+S3+S2+L-prefix |

| /-- No level is observed: a formation registers its support only. In the one-kind reading its own standing then couples in through support where it bears on itself (`Web.SelfBearing`). -/ | S2 |

| /-- The record `l` up to `t`, then the level `k` on `c`. -/ | /-- The level history `l` up to `t`, then the level `k` on `c`. -/ | TEXT |

| /-- `g` registers alike at every step up to `t`. -/ | /-- Alike through `g`'s couplings at every step up to `t`. -/ | S3+S2 |
| /-- A claim about level histories is refuted by `g` at `t` under `l` when no history meeting the claim is registered alike by `g` up to `t`. | /-- `g`'s belief at `t` under `l`: the histories it registers alike with `l` up to `t`. -/ | /-- The class alike through `g` at `t` under `l`: the histories alike with `l` through `g`'s couplings up to `t`. -/ | S3+S2+S5 |
| /-- Every member of a group registers alike up to `t`. -/ | /-- Alike through the couplings of every member of a group up to `t`. -/ | S3+S2 |

| /-- Every history registers alike with itself. -/ | /-- Every history is alike with itself through `g`'s couplings. -/ | S2 |
| /-- Registering alike is symmetric. -/ | /-- Alike through the couplings is symmetric. -/ | S3+S2 |
| /-- Registering alike up to a step is symmetric. -/ | /-- Alike through the couplings up to a step is symmetric. -/ | S3+S2 |

| /-- **What registers of one condition: its threshold.** With every other condition `g` registers alike at `s`, a condition `g` depends on and does not observe registers only whether it meets the need: meeting it under both histories or under neither, `g` registers alike, whatever the two levels are. -/ | /-- **What passes of one condition: its threshold.** With every other condition coupled into `g` alike at `s`, a condition coupled into `g` by threshold only passes only whether it meets the need: meeting it under both histories or under neither, they are alike through `g`'s couplings, whatever the two levels are. -/ | S3+S2 |
| /-- **What registers of one condition: its level, where observed.** Any difference in an observed condition at `s` is registered. -/ | /-- **What passes of one condition: its level, where coupled by level.** Any difference at `s` in a condition coupled into `g` by level makes the histories not alike through `g`'s couplings. -/ | S3+S2 |
| /-- A claim is refuted only through a difference in what registers: where a history meeting it is registered alike up to a step, it is not refuted there. -/ | /-- A claim is excluded only through a difference that couples in: where a history meeting it is alike through `g`'s couplings up to a step, it is not excluded there. -/ | S3+S2+S5 |
| /-- What holds is never refuted. -/ | /-- What holds is never excluded. -/ | S5 |
| /-- A refutation stands: refuted at a step, refuted at every later step. -/ | /-- An exclusion stands: excluded at a step, excluded at every later step. -/ | S5 |
| /-- Observing more refutes more. -/ | /-- More coupling by level excludes more. -/ | S2+S5 |
| /-- A claim met by a history that agrees with the record up to `t` is not refuted at `t`. -/ | /-- A claim met by a history that agrees with `l` up to `t` is not excluded at `t`. -/ | S5 |
| /-- **Without a model, a finite record leaves what follows open.** For every record up to `t`, neither the claim that a condition comes to nothing nor the claim that it stays above nothing after `t` is refuted at `t`, by any formation under any observation. -/ | /-- **Without a model, a finite prefix leaves what follows open.** For every level history up to `t`, neither the claim that a condition comes to nothing nor the claim that it stays above nothing after `t` is excluded at `t`, at any formation under any coupling by level. -/ | S2+S5+L-prefix |
| /-- Refutation is refutation relative to the model admitting every history. -/ | /-- Exclusion is exclusion relative to the model admitting every history. -/ | S5 |
| /-- Refutation within a model is refutation of the model and the claim together. -/ | /-- Exclusion within a model is exclusion of the model and the claim together. -/ | S5 |
| /-- A narrower model refutes more. -/ | /-- A narrower model excludes more. -/ | S5 |
| /-- Within a model admitting what holds, what holds is never refuted. -/ | /-- Within a model admitting what holds, what holds is never excluded. -/ | S5 |
| /-- A group refutes what any member refutes. -/ | /-- What is excluded at any member is excluded at the group. -/ | S5 |
| /-- Refutation is a claim disjoint from the belief. -/ | /-- A claim is excluded exactly where it is disjoint from the class alike through `g`. -/ | S5 |

| /-- Later steps narrow the belief and keep every common response. -/ | /-- Later steps narrow the class and keep every common response. -/ | S5 |
| /-- More observation narrows the belief and keeps every common response. -/ | /-- More coupling by level narrows the class and keeps every common response. -/ | S2+S5 |
| /-- Webs agreeing on what `g` requires and needs register its support through admission by their levels. -/ | /-- Webs agreeing on what `g` requires and needs pass its support through admission by their levels. -/ | TEXT |
| /-- Where `W'` stands at least as high on what `g` registers and alike on what it observes, registering alike at a step is exactly: wherever the levels of `W'` admit `g`, `W` supports it. -/ | /-- Where `W'` stands at least as high on what couples into `g` and alike on what couples into it by level, being alike through `g`'s couplings at a step is exactly: wherever the levels of `W'` admit `g`, `W` supports it. -/ | S3+S2 |
| /-! ## Reach blindness: nothing beyond the registration closure registers -/ | /-! ## Reach blindness: nothing beyond the coupling closure couples in -/ | S4+S2 |
| /-- `g` is affected through what it registers by `h`: `h` exchanges with a condition `g` registers. -/ | /-- `g` is affected by `h` through what couples into `g`: `h` exchanges with a condition coupled into `g`. -/ | S2 |
| /-- `g` lies within `f`'s registration closure: the closure under being affected through what is registered. -/ | /-- `g` lies within `f`'s coupling closure: the closure under being affected through what couples in. -/ | S4+S2 |
| /-- Affected through what is registered, in either of two flows. -/ | /-- Affected through what couples in, in either of two flows. -/ | S2 |

A hypothesis on the whole exchange around `g`: it excludes others responding to what does not couple into `g`. -/ | S3+S2 |

| /-- The registration closure holds the influence closure. -/ | /-- The coupling closure holds the influence closure. -/ | S4+S2 |
| /-- Registering support only, the registration closure is the influence closure. -/ | /-- Where coupling is by threshold only, the coupling closure is the influence closure. -/ | S4+S2 |
| /-- The registration closure grows with observation. -/ | /-- The coupling closure grows with coupling by level. -/ | S4+S2 |
| /-- The registration closure grows with exchange. -/ | /-- The coupling closure grows with exchange. -/ | S4+S2 |
| /-- Nothing beyond a formation's registration closure exchanges with a condition registered within it. -/ | /-- Nothing beyond a formation's coupling closure exchanges with a condition coupled into a formation within it. -/ | S4+S2 |

Then every member registers alike at every step, whatever differs beyond the region, in what members do with conditions no member registers, or in levels no member registers. -/ | /-- **Reach blindness where the members respond.** Take two flows with the same formations present, balancing on each condition coupled into a member, and a region closed in both under being affected through what couples in. | nothing the rest registers bounds how near the break is. -/ | nothing coupled into the rest bounds how near the break is. -/ | S2 |

| Where the second web starts at least as high on what g registers and alike on what it observes, g registers alike at every step | Where the second web starts at least as high on what couples into g and alike on what couples into it by level, the webs are alike through g's couplings at every step | S3 |
| Where the starts differ only in one stock g depends on and does not observe | Where the starts differ only in one stock coupled into g by threshold only | S2 |
| every other condition g registers stands alike and g has registered alike | every other condition coupled into g stands alike and the webs have been alike through g's couplings | S3 |
| in Gauged a formation observing its stock | in Gauged a formation into which its stock couples by level | S2 |
| the exchange following its registration constraining nothing, while through support only the exchange does not follow its registration | the exchange following its couplings constraining nothing, while with only support coupled in the exchange does not follow its couplings | S2 |
| the exchange not following its registration (Attracting | the exchange not following its couplings (Attracting | S2 |
| a bite of one registers alike through support at every step and through a gauge of oneself at the second, and a bite of two registers through support at the second step | a bite of one leaves the webs alike through support at every step and shows through a gauge of oneself at the second, and a bite of two shows through support at the second step | S3 |
| 设两个流在形成 g 所识（依存或观察）的每个条件上逐步平衡，并且 g 周围的交换随其识：凡 g 直到某步识得相同，两个流跨过该步在 g 所识的每个条件上 | 设两个流在耦合入形成 g（经阈或经水平）的每个条件上逐步平衡，并且 g 周围的交换随其耦合：凡两个流直到某步经 g 的耦合无别，它们跨过该步在耦合入 g 的每个条件上 | S3 |
| 那么 g 识得相同期间，它所识条件上的每个差别 | 那么经 g 的耦合无别期间，耦合入 g 之条件上的每个差别 | S3 |
| 第二个网在 g 所识的条件上起始不低、在其观察的条件上相同时，在第一个网此前每步都支持 g 的每一步，g 都识得相同 | 第二个网在耦合入 g 的条件上起始不低、在经水平耦合入 g 的条件上相同时，在第一个网此前每步都支持 g 的每一步，两网都经 g 的耦合无别 | S3 |
| 起始只在 g 依存而不观察的一个储量上不同 | 起始只在只经阈耦合入 g 的一个储量上不同 | S2 |
| g 所识的其余条件相同，g 此前每步都识得相同 | 耦合入 g 的其余条件相同，两网此前每步都经 g 的耦合无别 | S3 |
| Gauged 中观察储量的形成 | Gauged 中储量经水平耦合入的形成 | S2 |
| 交换随其识而不约束任何东西，只识支持时交换不随其识 | 交换随其耦合而不约束任何东西，只有支持耦合入时交换不随其耦合 | S2 |
| 交换不随其识（Attracting | 交换不随其耦合（Attracting | S2 |
| 咬一个单位经支持每步都识得相同，量度自身在第二步识之，咬两个单位经支持在第二步就被识 | 咬一个单位时两网经支持每步无别、经量度自身在第二步显出，咬两个单位时经支持在第二步就显出 | S3 |
| 平衡只在被识的条件上逐步假设 | 平衡只在耦合入的条件上逐步假设 | S2 |
| Balance is assumed only on the conditions registered, at each step | Balance is assumed only on the conditions coupled in, at each step | S2 |
| 交换随识（Flow.ExchangeFollows）是给出的假设，不由动力学推出：它是关于 g 周围全部交换的，排除他者对 g 所不识者作出回应 | 交换随耦合入者（Flow.ExchangeFollows）是给出的假设，不由动力学推出：它是关于 g 周围全部交换的，排除他者对不耦合入 g 者作出回应 | S2 |
| The exchange following registration (Flow.ExchangeFollows) is a supplied hypothesis, not derived from the dynamics: it concerns the whole exchange around g and excludes others responding to what g does not register | The exchange following what couples in (Flow.ExchangeFollows) is a supplied hypothesis, not derived from the dynamics: it concerns the whole exchange around g and excludes others responding to what does not couple into g | S2 |
| 识：形成识它的支持（水平是否接纳它）与它所观察之条件的水平（obs，参数，基础是 supportOnly）。活动由表示给出，不算作被识 | 耦合：耦合入形成的，是它的支持（水平是否接纳它），经阈，与经水平耦合入它之条件的水平（obs，参数，基础是 supportOnly）。活动由表示给出，不算作耦合入者 | S2 |
| Registration: a formation registers its support (whether the levels admit it) and the levels of the conditions it observes (obs, a parameter, with supportOnly the base). Activity is supplied by the representation and is not counted as registered | Coupling: into a formation couple its support (whether the levels admit it), by threshold, and the levels of the conditions coupled into it by level (obs, a parameter, with supportOnly the base). Activity is supplied by the representation and is not counted as coupled in | S2 |
| （阈下在交换随识之处，视界之外在延续开放之处，识闭包之外在成员只经自己之识回应之处） | （阈下在交换随耦合入者之处，视界之外在延续开放之处，耦合闭包之外在成员只经自己的耦合回应之处） | S4 |
| (below a threshold where the exchange follows registration, beyond the horizon where the continuation is open, beyond the registration closure where the members respond only through their own registration) | (below a threshold where the exchange follows what couples in, beyond the horizon where the continuation is open, beyond the coupling closure where the members respond only through their own couplings) | S4 |
| 理 读作对识负责的模型。所以记录中的读法 | 理 读作对识负责的模型（其后依 Ren 读作对结构与变化之规律的建模）。所以记录中的读法 | S2 |
| and 理 is read as a model answerable to registration. So the readings | and 理 is read as a model answerable to registration (later, following Ren, as the modeling of the laws of structure and change). So the readings | S2 |
| 第 4 问（形成自身的立着是否算作被识）由 AI 探索后决定：基础只识支持（supportOnly），一类读法中自身的立着经承担自身（Web.SelfBearing）作为阈值被识 | 第 4 问（形成自身的立着是否算作被识）由 AI 探索后决定：基础只有支持耦合入（supportOnly），一类读法中自身的立着经承担自身（Web.SelfBearing）经阈耦合入 | S2 |
| the base registers support only (supportOnly); in the one-kind reading own standing registers through self-bearing (Web.SelfBearing) as a threshold | only support couples into the base (supportOnly); in the one-kind reading own standing couples in by threshold through self-bearing (Web.SelfBearing) | S2 |
| Ren 定为“识”（“for registers, use 识”），取差别在形成的状态中显出之义（见整合文档 §{SEC}.2）。 | Ren 定为“识”（“for registers, use 识”），取差别在形成的状态中显出之义（见整合文档 §{SEC}.2）。2026-10-18 的命名决定之后，“registers”改作“耦合入”，识保留给一个形成经其耦合所成之归并的历程。 | S2 |
| a difference showing in the formation's state (§70.2 of the integration note). | a difference showing in the formation's state (§70.2 of the integration note). Under the naming decision of 18 October 2026 “registers” became “couples into”, and 识 is kept for a formation's course through the lumping its couplings make. | S2 |
| 一个形成识什么，两个水平历史何时对它识得相同。 | 什么耦合入一个形成，两个水平历史何时经其耦合无别。 | S2 |
| What a formation registers, and when two level histories register alike for it. | What couples into a formation, and when two level histories are alike through its couplings. | S2 |
| 交换随识的假设与阈下差别的规律。 | 交换随耦合入者的假设与阈下差别的规律。 | S2 |
| The hypothesis of the exchange following registration, and the laws | The hypothesis of the exchange following what couples in, and the laws | S2 |
| 第二个网的水平在第一个网支持 g 处接纳 g，所以 g 识得相同。 | 第二个网的水平在第一个网支持 g 处接纳 g，所以两网经 g 的支持无别。 | S3 |
| The second web's levels admit g wherever the first web supports it, so g registers alike. | The second web's levels admit g wherever the first web supports it, so the webs are alike through g's support. | S3 |
| 被识的条件。 | 耦合入的条件。 | S2 |
| The conditions registered. | The conditions coupled in. | S2 |
| 这里交换随识时，储量上的差别在支持分岔之前不被识。 | 这里交换随耦合入者时，储量上的差别在支持分岔之前不显出。 | S2 |
| here, where the exchange follows registration, a difference in a stock registers only once support diverges. | here, where the exchange follows what couples in, a difference in a stock shows only once support diverges. | S2 |
| 这里支持在分岔之前不识储量的差别。 | 这里储量的差别在分岔之前不经支持显出。 | S2 |
| here support registers a difference in a stock only once it diverges. | here a difference in a stock shows through support only once support diverges. | S2 |
| 这里是阈值处的识。 | 这里是经阈的耦合。 | S2 |
| here registration at a threshold. | here coupling by threshold. | S2 |
| 没有已识的量约束到断点的距离 | 没有耦合入的量约束到断点的距离 | S2 |
| no registered quantity bounds the distance to it | no quantity that couples in bounds the distance to it | S2 |
| 他者回应储量本身时，交换不随识 | 他者回应储量本身时，交换不随耦合入者 | S2 |
| Where others respond to the stock itself the exchange does not follow registration | Where others respond to the stock itself the exchange does not follow what couples in | S2 |
| 未加限定的读法“损失直到断裂才被识”对此不成立。 | 未加限定的读法“损失直到断裂才显出”对此不成立。 | S2 |

## `record:testing-within-registration`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| 识之内的检验 | 耦合之内的检验 | S2 (@title/zh) |
| Testing within registration | Testing within the couplings | S2 (@title/en) |
| 主张只经所识的差别被否证，相对于模型的否证是对模型与主张一起的否证。成员只经自己之识回应时，识闭包之外的在其中不被识；检验是关系的。 | 主张只经耦合入者的差别被排除，相对于模型的排除是对模型与主张一起的排除。成员只经自己的耦合回应时，耦合闭包之外的不在其中显出；检验是关系的。 | S5 (@statement/zh) |
| A claim is refuted only through a difference in what registers, and refutation relative to a model refutes the model and the claim together. Where members respond only through their own registration, nothing beyond the registration closure registers within it; testing is relational. | A claim is excluded only through a difference in what couples in, and exclusion relative to a model excludes the model and the claim together. Where members respond only through their own couplings, nothing beyond the coupling closure shows within it; testing is relational. | S5 (@statement/en) |
| 形成识它的支持（水平是否接纳它）与它所观察之条件的水平；基础只识支持。在两个水平历史之下，在某步水平同样接纳它、所观察的条件同样立着，就是识得相同；直到某步每步如此，就是直到该步识得相同。 | 耦合入形成的，是它的支持（水平是否接纳它），经阈，与经水平耦合入它之条件的水平；基础只有支持耦合入。在两个水平历史之下，在某步水平同样接纳它、经水平耦合入的条件同样立着，就是在该步经其耦合无别；直到某步每步如此，就是直到该步经其耦合无别。 | S3 (@laws/0/zh) |
| A formation registers its support (whether the levels admit it) and the levels of the conditions it observes; the base registers support only. Under two level histories it registers alike at a step when the levels admit it alike and its observed conditions stand alike, and alike up to a step when it does so at every step up to it. | Into a formation couple its support (whether the levels admit it), by threshold, and the levels of the conditions coupled into it by level; into the base only support couples. Two level histories are alike through its couplings at a step when the levels admit it alike and its conditions coupled in by level stand alike, and alike through them up to a step when they are so at every step up to it. | S3 (@laws/0/en) |
| 其余所识条件相同时，依存而不观察的条件只以是否满足所需被识；观察的条件以其水平被识；观察得更多分辨得更多。 | 其余耦合入的条件相同时，只经阈耦合入的条件只传递它是否满足所需；经水平耦合入的条件传递其水平；经水平耦合入的越多，分辨得越多。 | S2 (@laws/1/zh) |
| With every other registered condition alike, a condition depended on and not observed registers only whether it meets the need; an observed one registers its level; observing more distinguishes more. | With every other condition coupled in alike, a condition coupled in by threshold only passes only whether it meets the need; one coupled in by level passes its level; the more couples in by level, the more is told apart. | S2 (@laws/1/en) |
| 关于水平历史的主张在某步被否证，是指满足它的历史没有一个直到该步与实际识得相同：主张只经所识的差别被否证，成立的永不被否证，否证此后一直成立，观察得更多否证得更多。 | 关于水平历史的主张在某步被排除，是指满足它的历史没有一个直到该步经形成的耦合与实际无别：主张只经耦合入者的差别被排除，成立的永不被排除，排除此后一直成立，经水平耦合入的越多，排除得越多。 | S5 (@laws/2/zh) |
| A claim about level histories is refuted at a step when no history meeting it registers alike with the actual one up to that step: a claim is refuted only through a difference in what registers, what holds is never refuted, a refutation stands, and observing more refutes more. | A claim about level histories is excluded at a step when no history meeting it is alike with the actual one through the formation's couplings up to that step: a claim is excluded only through a difference in what couples in, what holds is never excluded, an exclusion stands, and the more couples in by level, the more is excluded. | S5 (@laws/2/en) |
| 没有模型时，对任何有限的记录，“某条件将归于无”与“它此后一直在无之上”都不被否证。 | 没有模型时，对任何有限的前段，“某条件将归于无”与“它此后一直在无之上”都不被排除。 | L-prefix (@laws/3/zh) |
| Without a model, for any finite record, neither the claim that a condition comes to nothing nor the claim that it stays above nothing afterwards is refuted. | Without a model, for any finite prefix, neither the claim that a condition comes to nothing nor the claim that it stays above nothing afterwards is excluded. | L-prefix (@laws/3/en) |
| 相对于模型（可容许历史的类）的否证恰是对模型与主张一起的否证；以流的平衡水平历史为模型、实际历史是其中之一时，观察储量的形成在第一步否证一切“它将归于无”的假主张；错的模型否证成立的，识否证模型。 | 相对于模型（可容许历史的类）的排除恰是对模型与主张一起的排除；以流的平衡水平历史为模型、实际历史是其中之一时，储量经水平耦合入的形成在第一步排除一切“它将归于无”的假主张；错的模型排除成立的，耦合入者排除模型。 | S5 (@laws/4/zh) |
| Refutation relative to a model, a class of admissible histories, is exactly refutation of the model and the claim together; relative to a flow's balanced level histories, the actual one among them, a formation observing a stock refutes at the first step every false claim that it comes to nothing; a wrong model refutes what holds, and registration refutes the model. | Exclusion relative to a model, a class of admissible histories, is exactly exclusion of the model and the claim together; relative to a flow's balanced level histories, the actual one among them, a formation into which a stock couples by level excludes at the first step every false claim that it comes to nothing; a wrong model excludes what holds, and what couples in excludes the model. | S5 (@laws/4/en) |
| 形成的信念是与实际识得相同的历史；否证是主张与信念不交；由识作出的判断恰是 Becoming.Inquiry 中判断过程在信念上的共同回应，更晚的步与更多的观察都保持它。 | 经形成之耦合无别之类，是经其耦合与实际无别的历史；排除是主张与这个类不交；经其耦合作出的判断恰是 Becoming.Inquiry 中判断过程在这个类上的共同回应，更晚的步与更多的耦合使类收窄，都保持它。 | S5 (@laws/5/zh) |
| A formation's belief is the histories registering alike with the actual one; refutation is a claim disjoint from the belief; a judgment from registration is exactly a common response of the judging process of Becoming.Inquiry over the belief, kept as later steps and more observation narrow it. | The class alike through a formation's couplings is the histories alike with the actual one through them; exclusion is a claim disjoint from that class; a judgment made through the couplings is exactly a common response of the judging process of Becoming.Inquiry over the class, kept as later steps and more coupling narrow it. | S5 (@laws/5/en) |
| 形成依存于或观察一个条件就识它；识闭包是对经所识者受影响的闭包，含影响闭包，只识支持时就是它，随观察与交换增大。 | 形成依存于一个条件，或该条件经水平耦合入它，该条件就耦合入它；耦合闭包是对经耦合入者受影响的闭包，含影响闭包，只有支持耦合入时就是它，随经水平的耦合与交换增大。 | S4 (@laws/6/zh) |
| A formation registers a condition when it depends on it or observes it; the registration closure is the closure under being affected through what is registered, holding the influence closure, equal to it registering support only, and growing with observation and exchange. | A condition couples into a formation when the formation depends on it or it couples in by level; the coupling closure is the closure under being affected through what couples in, holding the influence closure, equal to it where only support couples in, and growing with coupling by level and with exchange. | S4 (@laws/6/en) |
| 两个流相同的形成在场、在一个区域的成员所识的每个条件上逐步平衡，区域在两个流中都对经所识者受影响封闭，水平起始在成员所识的条件上相同，每个成员在自己识得相同之处对成员所识的条件同样取用与给予时，每个成员每步都识得相同。 | 两个流相同的形成在场、在耦合入一个区域之成员的每个条件上逐步平衡，区域在两个流中都对经耦合入者受影响封闭，水平起始在耦合入成员的条件上相同，每个成员在两个流经其耦合无别之处对耦合入成员的条件同样取用与给予时，两个流每步都经每个成员的耦合无别。 | S3 (@laws/7/zh) |
| For two flows with the same formations present, balancing at each step on every condition a member of a region registers, the region closed in both under being affected through what is registered, the levels starting alike on what members register, and each member taking from and giving to what members register alike wherever it has registered alike, every member registers alike at every step. | For two flows with the same formations present, balancing at each step on every condition coupled into a member of a region, the region closed in both under being affected through what couples in, the levels starting alike on what couples into members, and each member taking from and giving to what couples into members alike wherever the flows have been alike through its couplings, the flows are alike through every member's couplings at every step. | S3 (@laws/7/en) |
| 其余交换固定、在识闭包内所识的条件上逐步平衡时：成员都在识闭包之外的部分，闭包之内每步有与无它都识得相同；无部分的水平所满足的主张在闭包之内不被否证；相对于模型的否证靠模型对部分之交换的排除；闭包之内的判断在有与无部分时都可容许。 | 其余交换固定、在耦合入闭包内者的条件上逐步平衡时：成员都在耦合闭包之外的部分，闭包之内每步有与无它都经其耦合无别；无部分的水平所满足的主张在闭包之内不被排除；相对于模型的排除靠模型对部分之交换的排除；闭包之内的判断在有与无部分时都可容许。 | S4 (@laws/8/zh) |
| With the rest of the exchange fixed and balance at each step on what is registered within the closure, a part whose members all lie beyond the registration closure is registered alike with and without it at every step within it; no claim met by the levels without it is refuted there; a refutation there relative to a model rests on the model excluding its exchange; a judgment there is admissible with and without it. | With the rest of the exchange fixed and balance at each step on what couples in within the closure, the worlds with and without a part whose members all lie beyond the coupling closure are alike through the couplings within it at every step; no claim met by the levels without it is excluded there; an exclusion there relative to a model rests on the model excluding its exchange; a judgment there is admissible with and without it. | S4 (@laws/8/en) |
| Clearing 中清除者每步都识得相同，无论清除是否终结栖居者；在不清除的世界中，“栖居者已被终结”被清除者在任何一步都不否证，被栖居者在第三步否证；“第三步栖居者或清除者不被接纳”，两者各自都不否证，合起来否证。 | Clearing 中两个世界每步都经清除者的耦合无别，无论清除是否终结栖居者；在不清除的世界中，“栖居者已被终结”被清除者在任何一步都不排除，被栖居者在第三步排除；“第三步栖居者或清除者不被接纳”，两者各自都不排除，合起来排除。 | S5 (@laws/9/zh) |
| In Clearing the clearer registers alike at every step whether or not its clearing ends the dweller; with the habitat spared, the claim that the dweller is ended is refuted by the clearer at no step and by the dweller at the third step; the claim that the dweller or the clearer is not admitted at the third step is refuted by neither alone and by the two together. | In Clearing the worlds are alike through the clearer's couplings at every step whether or not its clearing ends the dweller; with the habitat spared, the claim that the dweller is ended is excluded by the clearer at no step and by the dweller at the third step; the claim that the dweller or the clearer is not admitted at the third step is excluded by neither alone and by the two together. | S5 (@laws/9/en) |
| 以固定起始与交换的模型，清除者在第一步否证它不识的。 | 以固定起始与交换的模型，清除者在第一步排除不耦合入它的。 | S5 (@laws/10/zh) |
| With a model fixing the start and the exchange, the clearer refutes at the first step what it does not register. | With a model fixing the start and the exchange, the clearer excludes at the first step what does not couple into it. | S5 (@laws/10/en) |
| Prey 中不依存于自身的猎物，捕食者在其影响闭包之外、量度自身时的识闭包之内：猎物经支持每步把捕食者的部分识得相同，量度自身则在被咬之后识其取用；这靠被省略的关系。 | Prey 中不依存于自身的猎物，捕食者在其影响闭包之外、量度自身时的耦合闭包之内：有与无捕食者的部分，两个世界经猎物的支持每步无别，量度自身则在被咬之后传递其取用；这靠被省略的关系。 | S4 (@laws/11/zh) |
| In Prey, the prey not depending on itself, the predator lies beyond its influence closure and within its registration closure under a gauge of itself: the prey registers the predator's part alike through support at every step, and the gauge registers the taking after the bite; this rests on omitted relations. | In Prey, the prey not depending on itself, the predator lies beyond its influence closure and within its coupling closure under a gauge of itself: the worlds with and without the predator's part are alike through the prey's support at every step, and the gauge passes the taking after the bite; this rests on omitted relations. | S4 (@laws/11/en) |
| 识与否证的定义不设前提；模型是给出的可容许历史类。 | 耦合与排除的定义不设前提；模型是给出的可容许历史类。 | S5 (@hypotheses/0/zh) |
| The definitions of registration and refutation assume nothing; a model is a supplied class of admissible histories. | The definitions of coupling and exclusion assume nothing; a model is a supplied class of admissible histories. | S5 (@hypotheses/0/en) |
| 平衡只在被识的条件上逐步假设 | 平衡只在耦合入的条件上逐步假设 | S2 |
| Balance is assumed only on the conditions registered, at each step | Balance is assumed only on the conditions coupled in, at each step | S2 |
| 另设区域在两个流中封闭、起始相同与成员只经自己之识回应 | 另设区域在两个流中封闭、起始相同与成员只经自己的耦合回应 | S2 |
| and the members responding only through their own registration, all supplied | and the members responding only through their own couplings, all supplied | S2 |
| 识：支持与所观察条件的水平（obs，参数）；活动由表示给出，不算作被识。否证是对一个形成、在一步、在一个水平历史之下的；主张是关于水平历史的任何命题。 | 耦合：支持与经水平耦合入之条件的水平（obs，参数）；活动由表示给出，不算作耦合入者。排除是对一个形成、在一步、在一个水平历史之下的；主张是关于水平历史的任何命题。 | S5 (@hypotheses/3/zh) |
| Registration: support and the levels of the conditions observed (obs, a parameter); activity is supplied by the representation and is not counted as registered. Refutation is by one formation, at a step, under one level history; a claim is any proposition about level histories. | Coupling: support and the levels of the conditions coupled in by level (obs, a parameter); activity is supplied by the representation and is not counted as coupled in. Exclusion is by one formation, at a step, under one level history; a claim is any proposition about level histories. | S5 (@hypotheses/3/en) |
| 形成自身的立着是否算作被识（Ren 的第 4 问）由 AI 探索后决定：基础只识支持（supportOnly）；一类读法中自身的立着经承担自身（Web.SelfBearing）作为阈值被识；量度自身（ownStanding）是观察前提。理由见整合文档。 | 形成自身的立着是否算作耦合入者（Ren 的第 4 问，当时问的是“是否被识”）由 AI 探索后决定：基础只有支持耦合入（supportOnly）；一类读法中自身的立着经承担自身（Web.SelfBearing）经阈耦合入；量度自身（ownStanding）是经水平耦合的前提。理由见整合文档。 | S2 (@hypotheses/4/zh) |
| Whether a formation's own standing counts as registered (Ren's question 4) was decided by the AI after exploring the options: the base registers support only (supportOnly); in the one-kind reading own standing registers through self-bearing (Web.SelfBearing) as a threshold; a gauge of oneself (ownStanding) is an observation premise. The reasons are in the integration note. | Whether a formation's own standing counts as coupled in (Ren's question 4, asked then as whether it is registered) was decided by the AI after exploring the options: only support couples into the base (supportOnly); in the one-kind reading own standing couples in by threshold through self-bearing (Web.SelfBearing); a gauge of oneself (ownStanding) is a premise of coupling by level. The reasons are in the integration note. | S2 (@hypotheses/4/en) |
| 反馈的三种不见各只在其范围内成立：阈下在交换随识之处，视界之外在延续开放之处，识闭包之外在成员只经自己之识回应之处。 | 反馈的三种不见各只在其范围内成立：阈下在交换随耦合入者之处，视界之外在延续开放之处，耦合闭包之外在成员只经自己的耦合回应之处。 | S4 (@does_not_show/0/zh) |
| Each feedback blindness holds only in its scope: below threshold where exchange follows registration, beyond the horizon where continuation is open, beyond the registration closure where members respond only through their own registration. | Each feedback blindness holds only in its scope: below threshold where exchange follows what couples in, beyond the horizon where continuation is open, beyond the coupling closure where members respond only through their own couplings. | S4 (@does_not_show/0/en) |
| 这些规律不推出形成观察支持之外的任何东西、形成模型、想要检验或知道其识闭包。 | 这些规律不推出有支持之外的东西经水平耦合入形成，也不推出形成形成模型、想要检验或知道其耦合闭包。 | S4 (@does_not_show/1/zh) |
| The laws do not derive that a formation observes anything beyond its support, forms a model, wants to test or knows its registration closure. | The laws do not derive that anything beyond its support couples into a formation by level, that it forms a model, wants to test or knows its coupling closure. | S4 (@does_not_show/1/en) |
| 识得相同界定能分辨的，不说一个差别关于什么。 | 经耦合无别界定能分辨的，不说一个差别关于什么。 | S3 (@does_not_show/2/zh) |
| Registering alike bounds what can be told apart and says nothing of what a difference is about. | Being alike through the couplings bounds what can be told apart and says nothing of what a difference is about. | S3 (@does_not_show/2/en) |
| 变动自己的动作并识差别（实验）在这里没有陈述 | 变动自己的动作而差别经耦合显出（实验）在这里没有陈述 | S2 |
| varying one's own act and registering the difference (experiment) is not stated here | varying one's own act and the difference showing through the couplings (experiment) is not stated here | S2 |
| 相对于模型的否证可以出错：Stationary 中模型说没有取用与给予，相对于它，量度储量的形成在第一步否证“储量将归于无” | 相对于模型的排除可以出错：Stationary 中模型说没有取用与给予，相对于它，量度储量的形成在第一步排除“储量将归于无” | S5 |
| Refutation relative to a model can err: in Stationary the model says nothing takes or gives, and relative to it a formation gauging the stock refutes at the first step | Exclusion relative to a model can err: in Stationary the model says nothing takes or gives, and relative to it a formation gauging the stock excludes at the first step | S5 |
| 识闭包可以严格大于影响闭包：Prey 中捕食者在猎物的影响闭包之外，量度自身时在其识闭包之内（Prey.couples_taking）；所识的差别对猎物不起作用 | 耦合闭包可以严格大于影响闭包：Prey 中捕食者在猎物的影响闭包之外，量度自身时在其耦合闭包之内（Prey.couples_taking）；耦合入的差别对猎物不起作用 | S4 |
| The registration closure can be strictly larger than the influence closure: in Prey the predator lies beyond the prey's influence closure and, under a gauge of itself, within its registration closure (Prey.couples_taking); the registered difference does nothing | The coupling closure can be strictly larger than the influence closure: in Prey the predator lies beyond the prey's influence closure and, under a gauge of itself, within its coupling closure (Prey.couples_taking); the difference coupled in does nothing | S4 |
| 多步 Scope 的桥：感知只经识的策略在识得相同的流上有同样的所见范围；模型从哪里来（见 projection-carrying-a-law 与 generalization-through-lumping）；由动力学给出回应，而不是另给它；识关于什么（校准）；识不分辨值时（阈下）的学习。 | 多步 Scope 的桥：只经耦合感知的策略在经其耦合无别的流上有同样的所见范围；模型从哪里来（见 projection-carrying-a-law 与 generalization-through-lumping）；由动力学给出回应，而不是另给它；耦合入者关于什么（校准）；耦合入者不分辨值时（阈下）的学习。 | S3 (@next_delta/zh) |
| The multi-step Scope bridge: a policy sensing only through registration has the same visible scope on flows registered alike; where a model comes from (see projection-carrying-a-law, generalization-through-lumping); responses given by the dynamics, not supplied; calibration; learning below threshold. | The multi-step Scope bridge: a policy sensing only through its couplings has the same visible scope on flows alike through them; where a model comes from (see projection-carrying-a-law, generalization-through-lumping); responses given by the dynamics, not supplied; calibration; learning below threshold. | S3 (@next_delta/en) |
| 理 原由 AI 读作对识负责的模型，现依 Ren 2026-10-12 的读法，为对结构与变化之规律的建模；模型仍只经识被检验 | 理 原由 AI 读作对识负责的模型，现依 Ren 2026-10-12 的读法，为对结构与变化之规律的建模；模型仍只经耦合入者被检验 | S2 |
| a model is still tested only through registration (generalization-through-lumping) | a model is still tested only through what couples in (generalization-through-lumping) | S2 |
| 加入观察，并以成员有回应的形式 Flow.alike_within_closed 为其根据；只识支持时 Flow.uncoupled_beyond_coupling 就是它 | 加入经水平的耦合，并以成员有回应的形式 Flow.alike_within_closed 为其根据；只有支持耦合入时 Flow.uncoupled_beyond_coupling 就是它 | S2 |
| adding observation, and grounding it in the form where the members respond, Flow.alike_within_closed; registering support only, Flow.uncoupled_beyond_coupling is it | adding coupling by level, and grounding it in the form where the members respond, Flow.alike_within_closed; where only support couples in, Flow.uncoupled_beyond_coupling is it | S2 |

| There registration at a threshold and the laws of differences below it where the exchange follows registration; here registration, refutation, reach and the relational character of testing. | There coupling by threshold and the laws of differences below it where the exchange follows what couples in; here coupling, exclusion, reach and the relational character of testing. | S5 |

| here without a model no finite record refutes a claim about what follows it, while relative to a model one can. | here without a model no finite prefix excludes a claim about what follows it, while relative to a model one can. | L-prefix |
