## `record:wisdom-root-part-one`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| 识、理、检验、学习与推广、经识的选择与训练、够、失控、判断与元层次的已检查规律及形成路线，对每个学习者与保留相同内容的每个表示成立（核心中不用公理）：这是目标 wisdom-root 的形式支撑。 | 耦合与识（一个形成经其耦合所成之归并的历程）、理、检验、学习与推广、经耦合的选择与训练、够、失控、判断与元层次的已检查规律及形成路线，对每个外推与保留相同内容的每个表示成立（核心中不用公理）：这是目标 wisdom-root 的形式支撑。 | S2 (@statement/zh) |
| The checked laws of registration, 理, testing, learning and generalization, selection and training, enough, runaway, judgment and the meta level, and the routes of formation hold of every learner and representation keeping the same content, axiom-free in the core: the formal support of wisdom-root. | The checked laws of coupling and 识, 理, testing, learning and generalization, selection and training, enough, runaway, judgment and the meta level, and the routes of formation hold of every extrapolation and representation with the same content, axiom-free in the core: wisdom-root's formal support. | S2 (@statement/en) |
| Each law below is stated for every formation, learner, lumping | Each law below is stated for every formation, extrapolation, lumping | L-extrap |
| 下列每条规律都对每个形成、每个学习者、每个归并 | 下列每条规律都对每个形成、每个外推、每个归并 | L-extrap |
| Registration: a claim is refuted only through a registered difference, and no record fixes the next value. | Coupling: a claim is excluded only through a difference in what couples in, and no prefix fixes the next value. | S5 (@laws/0/en) |
| 识：主张只经所识的差别被否证，无记录固定下一个。 | 耦合：主张只经耦合入者的差别被排除，无前段固定下一个值。 | S5 (@laws/0/zh) |
| Testing: refutation relative to a model refutes the model and the claim together; the record fixes an act's outcome only where taken; replication is return; one occasion stands for its class under the model. | Testing: exclusion relative to a model excludes the model and the claim together; the record fixes an act's outcome only where taken; replication is return; one occasion stands for its class under the model. | S5 (@laws/2/en) |
| 检验：相对于模型的否证是对模型与主张一起的否证，记录只在取过之处固定动作的结果，重复是返回，一次场合代表经模型相同的类。 | 检验：相对于模型的排除是对模型与主张一起的排除，记录只在取过之处固定动作的结果，重复是返回，一次场合代表经模型相同的类。 | S5 (@laws/2/zh) |
| Learning: in a world held by a list, learning by refutation is refuted a bounded number of times, its coming to rest is unrefuted and its record never shows that it has come to rest; every learner has an open course refuting each of its claims. | Learning: in a world held by a list, extrapolation by elimination misses a bounded number of times, its coming to rest is unrefuted and its prefix never shows that it has come to rest; every extrapolation has an open course on which each value it proposes misses. | L-elim (@laws/3/en) |
| 学习：被列表所持的世界中按否证学习的否证有界、静止不能被否证、其记录从不表明它已静止；每个学习者都有否证它每个主张的开放的序列。 | 学习：被列表所持的世界中逐除外推的不合有界、静止不能被否证、其前段从不表明它已静止；每个外推都有一个开放的历程，使它提出的每个值都不合。 | L-elim (@laws/3/zh) |
| 明 (others' registration): what the clearer cannot test the dweller can, and together they test what neither can alone; selection through registration is blind beyond the registration closure, and pooled registration selects what it misses. | 明 (others' couplings): what the clearer cannot test the dweller can, and together they test what neither can alone; selection through the couplings is blind beyond the coupling closure, and pooled couplings select what it misses. | S4 (@laws/4/en) |
| 明（他者之识）：清除者不能检验的栖居者能，合起来能检验各自都不能的；经识的选择在识闭包之外不见，合起来的识选出它。 | 明（他者的耦合）：清除者不能检验的栖居者能，合起来能检验各自都不能的；经耦合的选择在耦合闭包之外不见，合起来的耦合选出它。 | S4 (@laws/4/zh) |
| Runaway: before the break, how far it is does not register, while observing the stock registers it. | Runaway: before the break, how far it is does not couple in, while coupling the stock in by level passes it. | S2 (@laws/6/en) |
| 失控：断裂之前断裂还有多远不被识，观察储量则识之。 | 失控：断裂之前断裂还有多远不耦合入，储量经水平耦合入则传递它。 | S2 (@laws/6/zh) |
| Proxies: selection and training go only through the selector's registration or the data; values beyond the data stay as free as the class leaves them; reaching beyond the registration closure to raise what registers is selected. | Proxies: selection and training go only through the selector's couplings or the data; values beyond the data stay as free as the class leaves them; reaching beyond the coupling closure to raise what couples in is selected. | S4 (@laws/7/en) |
| 代理：选择与训练只经选择者之识或数据，数据之外的值与类同样自由，伸出识闭包之外而抬高所识者被选中。 | 代理：选择与训练只经选择者的耦合或数据，数据之外的值与类同样自由，伸出耦合闭包之外而抬高耦合入者被选中。 | S4 (@laws/7/zh) |
| Judgment: a judgment from registration is exactly a common response of the judging process over the belief. | Judgment: a judgment made through the couplings is exactly a common response of the judging process over the class alike through them. | S5 (@laws/8/en) |
| 判断：由识作出的判断恰是判断过程在信念上的共同回应。 | 判断：经耦合作出的判断恰是判断过程在经其耦合无别之类上的共同回应。 | S5 (@laws/8/zh) |
| The formal routes of formation: learning by refutation forms a model from the record; the acquisition chain forms an interpretation of every expression from three encounters; Hebbian development and the route of updating are laws on the joint configuration; the responsive route learns where the task fails. | The formal routes of formation: extrapolation by elimination forms a model from the prefix; the acquisition chain forms an interpretation of every expression from three encounters; Hebbian development and the route of updating are laws on the joint configuration; the responsive route learns where the task fails. | L-elim (@laws/10/en) |
| 形成的形式路线：按否证学习从记录形成模型，习得链从三次接触形成对全部表达式的解释，Hebb 式发展与更新路线是联合配置上的律，回应路线在失败处学习。 | 形成的形式路线：逐除外推从前段形成模型，习得链从三次接触形成对全部表达式的解释，Hebb 式发展与更新路线是联合配置上的律，回应路线在失败处学习。 | L-elim (@laws/10/zh) |
| 把规律对到智慧的各面（识、理、检验、学习、明、够、失控、代理、判断、元）是 AI 的读法，理依 Ren，其余依 Ren 的交托采用；“明”在这里只指看见自身所识之外要他者之识，不取其全部古典含义；“对每个表示成立”指每个学习者都受这些规律约束，不指有智慧者显式持有一个模型。 | 把规律对到智慧的各面（耦合与识、理、检验、学习、明、够、失控、代理、判断、元）是 AI 的读法，理依 Ren，其余依 Ren 的交托采用；“明”在这里只指看见耦合入自身者之外要他者的耦合，不取其全部古典含义；“对每个表示成立”指每个外推都受这些规律约束，不指有智慧者显式持有一个模型。 | S2 (@hypotheses/1/zh) |
| Matching the laws to aspects of wisdom (registration, 理, testing, learning, 明, enough, runaway, proxies, judgment, meta level) is the AI's reading, adopted on Ren's delegation (理 is Ren's); 明 here means only that seeing beyond one's own registration needs another's, not its whole classical sense; “holding of every representation” means every learner is bound by these laws, not that the wise hold an explicit model. | Matching the laws to aspects of wisdom (coupling and 识, 理, testing, learning, 明, enough, runaway, proxies, judgment, meta level) is the AI's reading, adopted on Ren's delegation (理 is Ren's); 明 here means only that seeing beyond what couples into oneself needs another's couplings, not its whole classical sense; “holding of every representation” means every extrapolation is bound by these laws, not that the wise hold an explicit model. | S2 (@hypotheses/1/en) |

| The laws of selection through registration and of proxies. | The laws of selection through the couplings and of proxies. | S2 (@dependencies/6/detail/en) |
| 经识的选择恰及选择者所识；在识闭包之外不见；合起来的识选出缺口中的终结。 | 经耦合的选择恰及耦合入选择者的；在耦合闭包之外不见；合起来的耦合选出缺口中的终结。 | S4 (@dependencies/6/interface/output/zh) |
| Selection through registration reaches exactly what the selector registers; blind beyond the registration closure; pooled registration selects the ending in the gap. | Selection through the couplings reaches exactly what couples into the selector; blind beyond the coupling closure; pooled couplings select the ending in the gap. | S4 (@dependencies/6/interface/output/en) |
| 有界识别说明为何无普遍完成的学习者不等于实际学习永不收束；用于必要性范围核对。 | 有界辨识说明为何没有普遍完成的外推不等于实际学习永不收束；用于必要性范围核对。 | L-extrap (@dependencies/8/detail/zh) |
| Bounded identification distinguishes the absence of a universally completed learner from perpetual non-convergence in actual learning; used to check necessity scope. | Bounded identification distinguishes the absence of a universally completed extrapolation from perpetual non-convergence in actual learning; used to check necessity scope. | L-extrap (@dependencies/8/detail/en) |
| Bounded stable correctness: where the orbit is held by a list of length N, the learner by refutation claims and is right at every step from a step computed from N, which the learner does not have. | Bounded stable correctness: where the orbit is held by a list of length N, extrapolation by elimination proposes and is right at every step from a step computed from N, which the extrapolation does not have. | L-elim (@progress/2/en) |
| 有界稳定正确：轨道被长 N 的列表所持时，按否证学习者从由 N 算出的一步起每步主张且正确；这一步是学习者所没有的 N 的函数。 | 有界稳定正确：轨道被长 N 的列表所持时，逐除外推从由 N 算出的一步起每步提出且正确；这一步是外推所没有的 N 的函数。 | L-elim (@progress/2/zh) |
| A refuting course for each learner is not perpetual revision in every actual world: bounded identification excludes a universal perpetual-revision reading. | A course on which each value an extrapolation proposes misses is not perpetual revision in every actual world: bounded identification excludes a universal perpetual-revision reading. | L-miss (@does_not_show/2/en) |
| 每个学习者有一个反驳历程，不是每个实际世界要求永久修订：有界识别排除永久修订的普遍读法。 | 每个外推有一个使它所提出的值都不合的历程，不是每个实际世界要求永久修订：有界辨识排除永久修订的普遍读法。 | L-miss (@does_not_show/2/zh) |

## `record:comparison-where-the-rest-responds`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| 部分之外的形成都经自己之识回应时，两个流的比较在部分的回应所及之内决定：其外的每个形成识得相同、交换相同，差别到不了它；只识支持时，回应所及是所及闭包之并。 | 部分之外的形成都经自己的耦合回应时，两个流的比较在部分的回应所及之内决定：两个流经其外每个形成的耦合无别、它交换相同，差别到不了它；只有支持耦合入时，回应所及是所及闭包之并。 | S3 (@statement/zh) |
| Where every formation outside a part responds through its registration, comparing two flows is decided within the part's responsive reach: beyond it each formation registers and exchanges alike and the difference never arrives; registering support only, that reach is the union of the reach closures. | Where every formation outside a part responds through its couplings, two flows compare within its responsive reach: beyond it they are alike through the couplings, the part exchanges alike, and the difference never arrives; where only support couples in, the reach is the union of reach closures. | S3 (@statement/en) |
| 对“a 在两个流之一中与 b 所识的条件交换”这一关系的闭包之内 | 对“a 在两个流之一中与耦合入 b 的条件交换”这一关系的闭包之内 | S2 |
| when it lies within some member's closure under the relation ‘a exchanges, in one of the two flows, with a condition b registers’ | when it lies within some member's closure under the relation ‘a exchanges, in one of the two flows, with a condition coupled into b’ | S2 |
| 对两个流、两个网、一个观察与部分 | 对两个流、两个网、一个经水平的耦合（obs）与部分 | S2 |
| for two flows, two webs, an observation and a part | for two flows, two webs, a coupling by level (obs) and a part | S2 |
| 恰当某个成员在它经两个流画出的识闭包之内；一个流时，恰当某个成员在它的识闭包之内。 | 恰当某个成员在它经两个流画出的耦合闭包之内；一个流时，恰当某个成员在它的耦合闭包之内。 | S4 |
| exactly when some member lies within its registration closure drawn through both flows; with one flow, exactly when some member lies within its registration closure. | exactly when some member lies within its coupling closure drawn through both flows; with one flow, exactly when some member lies within its coupling closure. | S4 |
| 只识支持、一个流时，一个形成在回应所及之内恰当 | 只经阈耦合、一个流时，一个形成在回应所及之内恰当 | S2 |
| registering support only, with one flow, a formation lies within it exactly when | with coupling by threshold only and one flow, a formation lies within it exactly when | S2 |
| 其余经识回应：部分之外的每个形成，凡在第一个网下对两个网的水平直到第 t 步识得相同，在第 t 步与每个条件的取用与给予在两个流中相同；其余固定（第二个流扣下部分）时无论识什么它都成立。 | 其余经其耦合回应：部分之外的每个形成，凡在第一个网下两个网的水平直到第 t 步经其耦合无别，在第 t 步与每个条件的取用与给予在两个流中相同；其余固定（第二个流扣下部分）时无论什么耦合入它都成立。 | S3 (@laws/3/zh) |
| The rest responds through its registration when each formation outside the part that has registered alike, under the first web, between the levels of the two webs up to step t takes from and gives to every condition alike in the two flows at step t; with the rest fixed (the second flow withholding the part) this holds whatever registers. | The rest responds through its couplings when each formation outside the part, wherever the levels of the two webs have been alike through its couplings, under the first web, up to step t, takes from and gives to every condition alike in the two flows at step t; with the rest fixed (the second flow withholding the part) this holds whatever couples in. | S3 (@laws/3/en) |
| 回应所及之外的形成构成在两个流中都对“被识自”封闭的区域，由闭包的定义直接得到。 | 回应所及之外的形成构成在两个流中都对“经耦合入者受影响”封闭的区域，由闭包的定义直接得到。 | S2 (@laws/4/zh) |
| The formations beyond the responsive reach form a region closed in both flows under being registered from, directly from the definition of the closure. | The formations beyond the responsive reach form a region closed in both flows under being affected through what couples in, directly from the definition of the closure. | S2 (@laws/4/en) |
| 限定：两个流的在场形成每步相同，在回应所及之外者所识的每个条件上逐步平衡且起始相同，其余经识回应时，回应所及之外的每个形成每步识得相同，并与每个条件交换相同（封闭区域内相同的实例）。 | 限定：两个流的在场形成每步相同，在耦合入回应所及之外者的每个条件上逐步平衡且起始相同，其余经其耦合回应时，两个流每步经回应所及之外每个形成的耦合无别，它并与每个条件交换相同（封闭区域内相同的实例）。 | S3 (@laws/5/zh) |
| Confinement: where the two flows have the same formations present at every step, balance at each step on every condition registered by a formation beyond the reach and start alike there, and the rest responds through its registration, every formation beyond the responsive reach registers alike and exchanges alike with every condition at every step (an instance of likeness within a closed region). | Confinement: where the two flows have the same formations present at every step, balance at each step on every condition coupled into a formation beyond the reach and start alike there, and the rest responds through its couplings, the flows are alike through every formation beyond the responsive reach at every step, and it exchanges alike with every condition (an instance of likeness within a closed region). | S3 (@laws/5/en) |
| 两个流每步从这样的形成所识的每个条件取用与向它给予的总量相同 | 两个流每步从耦合入这样的形成的每个条件取用与向它给予的总量相同 | S2 |
| the totals the two flows take from and give to each condition such a formation registers are alike | the totals the two flows take from and give to each condition coupled into such a formation are alike | S2 |
| 起始相同、其余经识回应时，对从 p 覆盖 | 起始相同、其余经其耦合回应时，对从 p 覆盖 | S2 |
| start alike and the rest responds through registration, for any significance | start alike and the rest responds through its couplings, for any significance | S2 |
| 只识支持时前提成立 | 只经阈耦合时前提成立 | S2 |
| the premise holds registering support only | the premise holds with coupling by threshold only | S2 |
| 两个形成各需要并观察自己的条件 | 两个形成各需要自己的条件，且该条件经水平耦合入它 | S2 |
| two formations each require and observe their own condition | two formations each require their own condition, which couples into it by level | S2 |
| 起始相同，第二个经识回应。 | 起始相同，第二个经其耦合回应。 | S2 |
| they start alike and the second responds through its registration. | they start alike and the second responds through its couplings. | S2 |
| 经识回应（Flow.RespondsThroughCoupling）在这些定律中是给出的前提，定律只在回应所及之外用到它；它排除对未被识者的回应、噪声与未表示为条件的外来输入。 | 经其耦合回应（Flow.RespondsThroughCoupling）在这些定律中是给出的前提，定律只在回应所及之外用到它；它排除对不耦合入者的回应、噪声与未表示为条件的外来输入。 | S2 (@hypotheses/0/zh) |
| Responding through registration (Flow.RespondsThroughCoupling) is a supplied premise in these laws, which use it only beyond the responsive reach; it excludes responding to what is not registered, noise, and outside inputs not represented as conditions. | Responding through the couplings (Flow.RespondsThroughCoupling) is a supplied premise in these laws, which use it only beyond the responsive reach; it excludes responding to what does not couple in, noise, and outside inputs not represented as conditions. | S2 (@hypotheses/0/en) |
| 其余遵从一个只读各形成所识的交换律时 | 其余遵从一个只读耦合入各形成者的交换律时 | S2 |
| Where the rest follows a law of exchange reading only what each formation registers, the premise | Where the rest follows a law of exchange reading only what couples into each formation, the premise | S2 |
| 每个读已立者的律经识一切而读，条件有限时经其敏感而读（ExchangeLaw.Nonanticipating.through_sensitive）；取律的敏感为识时，那些回应落在识之内，回应所及随之变宽。 | 每个读已立者的律都经一切之耦合而读，条件有限时经其敏感而读（ExchangeLaw.Nonanticipating.through_sensitive）；取律的敏感为耦合时，那些回应落在耦合之内，回应所及随之变宽。 | S2 (@hypotheses/2/zh) |
| Every law of what has stood reads through registering everything, and with finitely many conditions through its sensitivity (ExchangeLaw.Nonanticipating.through_sensitive); with the law's sensitivity as the registration those responses fall within it and the responsive reach widens. | Every law of what has stood reads through the coupling of everything, and with finitely many conditions through its sensitivity (ExchangeLaw.Nonanticipating.through_sensitive); with the law's sensitivity as the coupling those responses fall within it and the responsive reach widens. | S2 (@hypotheses/2/en) |
| 限定的定律只在回应所及之外者所识的条件上逐步假设平衡 | 限定的定律只在耦合入回应所及之外者的条件上逐步假设平衡 | S2 |
| The confinement laws assume balance at each step only on the conditions registered by formations beyond the responsive reach | The confinement laws assume balance at each step only on the conditions coupled into formations beyond the responsive reach | S2 |
| 形成只经其识回应是给出的前提：在此既不推出它们这样回应，也不推出怎样回应。 | 形成只经其耦合回应是给出的前提：在此既不推出它们这样回应，也不推出怎样回应。 | S2 (@does_not_show/0/zh) |

| which is only the exchange of formations beyond the reach on what they register. | which is only the exchange of formations beyond the reach on what couples into them. | S2 |
| 前提可以不成立：Attracting 中取用者什么都不识（不需要任何条件、只识支持），却在储量较高时多取用（Attracting.higher_breaks_first）；按检视，那里没有部分时经识回应不成立 | 前提可以不成立：Attracting 中没有什么耦合入取用者（它不需要任何条件、只有支持耦合入），却在储量较高时多取用（Attracting.higher_breaks_first）；按检视，那里没有部分时经其耦合回应不成立 | S2 |
| The premise can fail: in Attracting the taker registers nothing (it requires nothing and registers support only), yet takes more where the stock is higher (Attracting.higher_breaks_first); by inspection, with no part, responding through registration fails there | The premise can fail: in Attracting nothing couples into the taker (it requires nothing, and only support couples in), yet it takes more where the stock is higher (Attracting.higher_breaks_first); by inspection, with no part, responding through the couplings fails there | S2 |
| 回答本章开放项“其余交换随之调整的反事实”中其余经识回应的情形。读法（AI 的，依 Ren 的交托采用）：一个动作有两种反事实，其余固定时只计部分直接到达的，其余经识回应时计部分对经所识者影响的闭包；一个动作对与之相互作用的形成造成什么，在第二种之内决定；升级回路是每一方都经识回应、差别回到部分的情形，它不在其余固定的比较中出现；只识支持时回应所及是所及闭包之并 | 回答本章开放项“其余交换随之调整的反事实”中其余经其耦合回应的情形。读法（AI 的，依 Ren 的交托采用）：一个动作有两种反事实，其余固定时只计部分直接到达的，其余经其耦合回应时计部分对经耦合入者影响的闭包；一个动作对与之相互作用的形成造成什么，在第二种之内决定；升级回路是每一方都经其耦合回应、差别回到部分的情形，它不在其余固定的比较中出现；只经阈耦合时回应所及是所及闭包之并 | S2 |
| answering, for the case where the rest responds through its registration, the chapter's open item “the counterfactual in which the rest of the exchange adjusts”. Readings (the AI's, adopted on Ren's delegation): an act has two counterfactuals, with the rest fixed, where only what the part reaches directly counts, and with the rest responding through its registration, where the part's closure under affecting what is registered counts; what an act does to formations that interact with it is decided within the second; an escalation loop is the case where each party responds through its registration and the difference returns to the part, which does not show in the comparison with the rest fixed; registering support only, the responsive reach | answering, for the case where the rest responds through its couplings, the chapter's open item “the counterfactual in which the rest of the exchange adjusts”. Readings (the AI's, adopted on Ren's delegation): an act has two counterfactuals, with the rest fixed, where only what the part reaches directly counts, and with the rest responding through its couplings, where the part's closure under affecting what couples in counts; what an act does to formations that interact with it is decided within the second; an escalation loop is the case where each party responds through its couplings and the difference returns to the part, which does not show in the comparison with the rest fixed; with coupling by threshold only, the responsive reach | S2 |
| 从区域一侧：对经所识者受影响封闭、成员只经自己之识回应的区域，其成员识得相同。 | 从区域一侧：对经耦合入者受影响封闭、成员只经自己的耦合回应的区域，两个流经其成员的耦合无别。 | S3 (@dependencies/0/interface/output/zh) |
| From a region's side: members of a region closed under being affected through what is registered, responding only through their own registration, register alike. | From a region's side: for a region closed under being affected through what couples in, its members responding only through their own couplings, the flows are alike through the members' couplings. | S3 (@dependencies/0/interface/output/en) |
| 从部分一侧：回应所及之外的区域封闭，其成员识得相同、交换相同。 | 从部分一侧：回应所及之外的区域封闭，两个流经其成员的耦合无别、成员交换相同。 | S3 (@dependencies/0/interface/input/zh) |
| From a part's side: the region beyond the responsive reach is closed, and its members register alike and exchange alike. | From a part's side: the region beyond the responsive reach is closed, and the flows are alike through its members' couplings and its members exchange alike. | S3 (@dependencies/0/interface/input/en) |
| 在区域成员所识的条件上逐步平衡；两个流的在场形成相同；起始相同；其余经识回应。 | 在耦合入区域成员的条件上逐步平衡；两个流的在场形成相同；起始相同；其余经其耦合回应。 | S2 (@dependencies/0/interface/conditions/zh) |
| Balance at each step on what the region's members register; the same formations present; a common start; the rest responding through its registration. | Balance at each step on what couples into the region's members; the same formations present; a common start; the rest responding through its couplings. | S2 (@dependencies/0/interface/conditions/en) |
| 回应所及是对经所识者影响的闭包；只识支持、一个流时是所及闭包之并；所及闭包是影响闭包之逆。 | 回应所及是对经耦合入者影响的闭包；只经阈耦合、一个流时是所及闭包之并；所及闭包是影响闭包之逆。 | S2 (@dependencies/3/interface/input/zh) |
| The responsive reach is the closure under affecting what is registered; registering support only, with one flow, it is the union of reach closures; the reach closure is the converse of the influence closure. | The responsive reach is the closure under affecting what couples in; with coupling by threshold only and one flow, it is the union of reach closures; the reach closure is the converse of the influence closure. | S2 (@dependencies/3/interface/input/en) |
| 限定只在回应所及之外者所识的条件上逐步假设平衡 | 限定只在耦合入回应所及之外者的条件上逐步假设平衡 | S2 |
| the confinement assumes balance at each step only on what formations beyond the responsive reach register | the confinement assumes balance at each step only on what couples into formations beyond the responsive reach | S2 |
| 经第二个的观察与回应回到第一个自身的支持 | 经耦合入第二个的水平与它的回应回到第一个自身的支持 | S2 |
| through the second's observation and response | through the level coupled into the second and its response | S2 |
| 那里其余固定的假设在这里换为经识回应。 | 那里其余固定的假设在这里换为经其耦合回应。 | S2 |
| the rest held fixed there is replaced here by responding through registration. | the rest held fixed there is replaced here by responding through the couplings. | S2 |

## `record:identification-where-runs-part`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| 凡一个过程的运行所识的是同一历程之处，配置数为 N 时，记录从 2N − 1 起确定它，与确定的律一样。在分开之处，没有学习者，无论被给予什么（世界在内），在两个运行上都对；列出、可判定的过程中，被给予世界的学习者恰在不分开之处都对。 | 凡一个过程的运行经映射是同一历程之处，配置数为 N 时，前段从 2N − 1 起确定它，与确定的律一样。在分开之处，没有外推，无论被给予什么（过程在内），在两个运行上都对；列出、可判定的过程中，依过程所建的外推恰在不分开之处都对。 | L-built (@statement/zh) |
| Wherever the runs of a process register one course, a record fixes it from 2N − 1 on with N configurations, as for determined laws. At a parting no learner, whatever it is given, is right on both runs; for listed, decided processes a learner given the world is right exactly where none part. | Wherever a process's runs have one course through the map, a prefix fixes it from 2N − 1 on with N configurations. At a parting no extrapolation, whatever it is given, is right on both runs; for listed, decided processes the one built from the process is right exactly where none part. | L-built (@statement/en) |
| 两个运行在某步之前所识相同、在该步不同，即在该步分开；两者在该步之前的记录是同一个，所以没有学习者，无论被给予什么，在两者上都对；在从一个配置出发的每个运行上从 T 起都对的学习者，使这些运行在 T 之后不分开。 | 两个运行在某步之前经映射取同一值、在该步取不同值，即在该步分开；两者在该步之前的前段是同一个，所以没有外推，无论被给予什么，在两者上都对；在从一个配置出发的每个运行上从 T 起都对的外推，使这些运行在 T 之后不分开。 | L-prefix (@laws/0/zh) |
| Two runs part at a step where they register the same before it and differ at it; their records before it are one, so no learner, whatever it is given, is right on both; a learner right from T on, on every run from a configuration, leaves no two of those runs parting from T on. | Two runs part at a step where they take one value through the map before it and different values at it; their prefixes before it are one, so no extrapolation, whatever it is given, is right on both; an extrapolation right from T on, on every run from a configuration, leaves no two of those runs parting from T on. | L-prefix (@laws/0/en) |
| 所识的值可判定时，从一个配置出发没有两个运行分开，恰当它们所识的是同一历程。 | 经映射的值可判定时，从一个配置出发没有两个运行分开，恰当它们经映射的是同一历程。 | S1 (@laws/1/zh) |
| With the registered values decided, no two runs from a configuration part exactly where all of them register one course. | With the values through the map decided, no two runs from a configuration part exactly where all of them have one course through the map. | S1 (@laws/1/en) |
| 从不分开的配置出发、被 N 个配置的列表所持的运行，其所识的历程至多有 N 个状态，以最小的界读记录的学习者不被给予什么，从 N + N − 1 起每步都对；N ≥ 2 时没有学习者在每个这样的运行上于 N + N − 2 都对。 | 从不分开的配置出发、被 N 个配置的列表所持的运行，其经映射的历程至多有 N 个状态，按最小的界的外推不被给予什么，从 N + N − 1 起每步都对；N ≥ 2 时没有外推在每个这样的运行上于 N + N − 2 都对。 | L-extrap (@laws/3/zh) |
| On a run from an unparted configuration held by a list of N configurations the registered course has at most N states, and the learner by the least bound, given nothing, is right from N + N − 1 on; for N ≥ 2 no learner is right at N + N − 2 on every such run of every process of N configurations. | On a run from an unparted configuration held by a list of N configurations the course through the map has at most N states, and the extrapolation by the least bound, given nothing, is right from N + N − 1 on; for N ≥ 2 no extrapolation is right at N + N − 2 on every such run of every process of N configurations. | L-extrap (@laws/3/en) |
| 分支不显于所识者也不分开。 | 不经映射显出的分支也不分开。 | S1 |
| a branching that does not show in what is registered parts nowhere either. | a branching that does not show through the map parts nowhere either. | S1 |
| 自由分支下每个历程都是一个运行的历程；每个记录之后，两个有这一记录的运行分开，每个学习者在其中一个上于下一步不对，且有一个运行使它每步都不对。 | 自由分支下每个历程都是一个运行的历程；每个前段之后，两个有这一前段的运行分开，每个外推在其中一个上于下一步不对，且有一个运行使它每步都不对。 | L-prefix (@laws/5/zh) |
| Under free branching every course is a run's course; after every record two runs with that record part, every learner is not right at the next step on one of them, and one run makes it wrong at every step. | Under free branching every course is a run's course; after every prefix two runs with that prefix part, every extrapolation is not right at the next step on one of them, and one run makes it wrong at every step. | L-prefix (@laws/5/en) |
| 其中一个运行所识的历程没有状态数的界。 | 其中一个运行经映射的历程没有状态数的界。 | S1 |
| and one of its runs has a registered course with no bound on its states. | and one of its runs has a course through the map with no bound on its states. | S1 |
| 运行只在 T 步之前分开、此后不分开时，所识的值可判定，从一个运行在 T 所在处出发没有两个运行分开；被 N 个配置所持时其历程至多 T + N 个状态，以最小的界读记录的学习者从 2(T + N) − 1 起每步都对。 | 运行只在 T 步之前分开、此后不分开时，经映射的值可判定，从一个运行在 T 所在处出发没有两个运行分开；被 N 个配置所持时其历程至多 T + N 个状态，按最小的界的外推从 2(T + N) − 1 起每步都对。 | L-extrap (@laws/7/zh) |
| Where runs part only before step T and never after, the registered values decided, no two runs part from where a run is at T; held by N configurations its course has at most T + N states, and the learner by the least bound is right from 2(T + N) − 1 on. | Where runs part only before step T and never after, the values through the map decided, no two runs part from where a run is at T; held by N configurations its course has at most T + N states, and the extrapolation by the least bound is right from 2(T + N) − 1 on. | L-extrap (@laws/7/en) |
| 反方向：步与所识的值可判定时，被给予世界的学习者从记录读出运行可能所在的配置，取第一个由之可走下去者，说它所识的值；它在某步于从一个配置出发的每个运行上都对，恰当没有两个这样的运行在该步分开；所以某个学习者在那里对，恰当没有运行在那里分开。 | 反方向：步与经映射的值可判定时，依过程所建的外推从前段读出运行可能所在的配置，取第一个由之可走下去者，提出它经映射的值；它在某步于从一个配置出发的每个运行上都对，恰当没有两个这样的运行在该步分开；所以某个外推在那里对，恰当没有运行在那里分开。 | L-built (@laws/9/zh) |
| The converse: with decided steps and registered values, the learner given the world reads from the record the configurations a run can be at, takes the first from which a run goes on, and claims what it registers; it is right at a step on every run from a configuration exactly where no two of those runs part there, so some learner is right there exactly where none part. | The converse: with decided steps and values through the map, the extrapolation built from the process reads from the prefix the configurations a run can be at, takes the first from which a run goes on, and proposes its value through the map; it is right at a step on every run from a configuration exactly where no two of those runs part there, so some extrapolation is right there exactly where none part. | L-built (@laws/9/en) |
| unparted_iff、leastBound_right、late_* 与 free_open 要所识的值可判定；world_right 等要 | unparted_iff、leastBound_right、late_* 与 free_open 要经映射的值可判定；world_right_iff 等要 | S1 |
| the late_ laws and free_open need the registered values decided; world_right and its kin need | the late_ laws and free_open need the values through the map decided; world_right_iff and its kin need | S1 |
| 学习者读配置所识的值；动作只在识读它们时被识（例如配置带着它的上一个动作）。 | 外推读配置经映射的值；动作只在映射读它们时显出（例如配置带着它的上一个动作）。 | S1 (@hypotheses/1/zh) |
| The learner reads the values registered at configurations; moves register only where the registration reads them (for instance, where a configuration carries its last move). | The extrapolation reads the values of configurations through the map; moves show only where the map reads them (for instance, where a configuration carries its last move). | S1 (@hypotheses/1/en) |
| 无限或步不可判定的过程中被给予世界的学习者：构造要配置与动作列出、步可判定，那里由之能否走下去一般不可判定。 | 无限或步不可判定的过程中依过程所建的外推：构造要配置与动作列出、步可判定，那里由之能否走下去一般不可判定。 | L-built (@does_not_show/1/zh) |
| The learner given the world for infinite or undecided processes: the construction needs listed moves and configurations and decided steps, and elsewhere whether a run goes on is not decided in general. | The extrapolation built from the process for infinite or undecided processes: the construction needs listed moves and configurations and decided steps, and elsewhere whether a run goes on is not decided in general. | L-built (@does_not_show/1/en) |
| free_parts_after：每个记录之后，自由分支上两个有这一记录的运行分开；free_never_right：对每个学习者，自由分支上的对角运行使它每步都不对；free_unbounded：两个配置上的一个运行，其历程不是任何套索，没有状态数的界。 | free_parts_after：每个前段之后，自由分支上两个有这一前段的运行分开；free_never_right：对每个外推，自由分支上的对角运行使它每步都不对；free_unbounded：两个配置上的一个运行，其历程不是任何套索，没有状态数的界。 | L-prefix (@counterexamples/0/detail/zh) |
| free_parts_after: after every record two runs of free branching with that record part; free_never_right: for every learner, the diagonal run of free branching makes it wrong at every step; free_unbounded: a run on two configurations whose course is no lasso and has no bound on its states. | free_parts_after: after every prefix two runs of free branching with that prefix part; free_never_right: for every extrapolation, the diagonal run of free branching makes it wrong at every step; free_unbounded: a run on two configurations whose course is no lasso and has no bound on its states. | L-prefix (@counterexamples/0/detail/en) |
| 无限或步不可判定的过程中被给予世界的学习者能对之处；2(T + N) − 1 的恰好性。 | 无限或步不可判定的过程中依过程所建的外推能对之处；2(T + N) − 1 的恰好性。 | L-built (@next_delta/zh) |
| Where a learner given the world can be right for infinite or undecided processes; the exactness of 2(T + N) − 1. | Where an extrapolation built from the process can be right for infinite or undecided processes; the exactness of 2(T + N) − 1. | L-built (@next_delta/en) |
| 已知：分开处无学习者两者都对是不可区分论证 | 已知：分开处无外推两者都对是不可区分论证 | L-extrap |
| Known result: no learner right on both at a parting | Known result: no extrapolation right on both at a parting | L-extrap |
| 对所识者成立，到运行不分开为止 | 对经映射所读者成立，到运行不分开为止 | S1 |
| holds for what is registered, as far as runs do not part | holds for what is read through the map, as far as runs do not part | S1 |
| 读法（Ren 同日已取）：分开处记录与所给的律不固定的，由情境固定 | 读法（Ren 同日已取）：分开处前段与所给的律不固定的，由情境固定 | L-prefix |
| at a parting, what the record and the supplied law leave open the situation fixes (weights-count-situations). | at a parting, what the prefix and the supplied law leave open the situation fixes (weights-count-situations). | L-prefix |
| 至多 N 个状态的历程上，以最小的界读记录的学习者从 2N − 1 起都对；2N − 2 不够。 | 至多 N 个状态的历程上，按最小的界的外推从 2N − 1 起都对；2N − 2 不够。 | L-extrap (@dependencies/0/interface/output/zh) |

| 复用 record、record_congr、holdFrom、diagonal、diagonal_refutes、diagonal_not_lasso、learner 与 lassos：自由分支上的对角与开放。 | 复用 prefixOf、prefix_congr、holdFrom、diagonal、diagonal_misses、diagonal_not_lasso、byElimination 与 lassos：自由分支上的对角与开放。 | N5 (@dependencies/1/detail/zh) |
| Reuses record, record_congr, holdFrom, diagonal, diagonal_refutes, diagonal_not_lasso, learner and lassos: the diagonal and openness on free branching. | Reuses prefixOf, prefix_congr, holdFrom, diagonal, diagonal_misses, diagonal_not_lasso, byElimination and lassos: the diagonal and openness on free branching. | N5 (@dependencies/1/detail/en) |

| 运行在两处有同一配置，从那里起所识的历程相同。 | 运行在两处有同一配置，从那里起经映射的历程相同。 | S1 (@dependencies/3/interface/input/zh) |

## `record:runaway-distance-unregistered`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| Runaway and the unregistered distance to the break | Runaway, and the distance to the break that does not couple in | S2 (@title/en) |
| 失控与不被识的断裂距离 | 失控与不耦合入的断裂距离 | S2 (@title/zh) |
| 每个经识、其余固定的策略保持支持就永不动用它不观察的较多储量；断裂之前，断裂还有多远不被识。 | 每个经耦合、其余交换固定的策略保持支持就永不动用只经阈耦合入它的较多储量；断裂之前，断裂还有多远不耦合入。 | S2 (@statement/zh) |
| Every policy through registration, with the rest fixed, that keeps its support leaves any larger unobserved stock unused; before the break, how far off the break is does not register. | Every policy through the couplings, with the rest of the exchange fixed, that keeps its support leaves unused any larger stock coupled into it by threshold only; before the break, how far off the break is does not couple in. | S2 (@statement/en) |
| 形成 g 的策略经其识，是指凡 g 直到某步识得相同，它在所识的每个条件上的取用与给予在两个流中相同；其余固定，是指其他形成在 g 所识的条件上的交换每步相同；两个流在场的形成相同时，两者合起来就是交换随识。 | 形成 g 的策略经其耦合，是指凡两个流直到某步经 g 的耦合无别，它在耦合入 g 的每个条件上的取用与给予在两个流中相同；其余固定，是指其他形成在耦合入 g 的条件上的交换每步相同；两个流在场的形成相同时，两者合起来就是交换随耦合入者。 | S3 (@laws/0/zh) |
| A formation g's policy goes through its registration when, wherever g has registered alike up to a step, its take from and give to every condition it registers are alike in the two flows; the rest is fixed when every other formation's exchange on the conditions g registers is alike at every step; with the same formations present, the two give exchange following registration. | A formation g's policy goes through its couplings when, wherever the two flows have been alike through g's couplings up to a step, its take from and give to every condition coupled into g are alike in the two flows; the rest is fixed when every other formation's exchange on the conditions coupled into g is alike at every step; with the same formations present, the two give exchange following what couples in. | S3 (@laws/0/en) |
| 对每个经识、其余固定的策略，在场的形成相同、在 g 所识的条件上逐步平衡、起始只在 g 依存而不观察的储量上不同且第二个网中高 d 时：第一个网每步都支持 g，储量就每步都高 d，g 每步都识得相同。 | 对每个经耦合、其余固定的策略，在场的形成相同、在耦合入 g 的条件上逐步平衡、起始只在只经阈耦合入 g 的储量上不同且第二个网中高 d 时：第一个网每步都支持 g，储量就每步都高 d，两网每步都经 g 的耦合无别。 | S3 (@laws/1/zh) |
| For every policy through registration with the rest fixed, the same formations present and balance at each step on what g registers, the starts differing only in a stock g depends on and does not observe, d higher in the second web: where the first web supports g at every step, the stock stands d higher and g registers alike at every step. | For every policy through the couplings with the rest fixed, the same formations present and balance at each step on what couples into g, the starts differing only in a stock coupled into g by threshold only, d higher in the second web: where the first web supports g at every step, the stock stands d higher and the webs are alike through g's couplings at every step. | S3 (@laws/1/en) |
| 同样的策略、其余、在场与平衡之下，第二个网起始在 g 所识者上不低、在所观察者上相同，而 g 所识条件上的差别在某步之前已改变时，第一个网并非此前每步都支持 g。 | 同样的策略、其余、在场与平衡之下，第二个网起始在耦合入 g 者上不低、在经水平耦合入 g 者上相同，而耦合入 g 之条件上的差别在某步之前已改变时，第一个网并非此前每步都支持 g。 | S2 (@laws/2/zh) |
| Under the same policy, rest, presence and balance, where the second web starts at least as high on what g registers and alike on what g observes, and a difference on a condition g registers has changed by a step, the first web did not support g at every earlier step. | Under the same policy, rest, presence and balance, where the second web starts at least as high on what couples into g and alike on what couples into g by level, and a difference on a condition coupled into g has changed by a step, the first web did not support g at every earlier step. | S2 (@laws/2/en) |
| 在网支持 g 的每一步，g 不观察的储量的抬高，g 识得相同；观察它时，任何正的抬高每步都被识。 | 在网支持 g 的每一步，只经阈耦合入 g 的储量的抬高，两网经 g 的耦合无别；储量经水平耦合入时，任何正的抬高每步都显出。 | S3 (@laws/4/zh) |
| At every step at which the web supports g, a raise of a stock g does not observe registers alike for g; observing it, every positive raise registers at every step. | At every step at which the web supports g, the web with a stock coupled into g by threshold only raised is alike through g's couplings; with the stock coupled in by level, every positive raise shows at every step. | S3 (@laws/4/en) |
| g 不观察 c、到视界 u 为止只有 c 的所需可能不满足时，c 的某个抬高（证明中以到 u 为止的所需之和）在原网支持 g 的每一步识得相同，并在 u 之前每步都支持 g：断裂之前，断裂还有多远不被识。 | c 只经阈耦合入 g、到视界 u 为止只有 c 的所需可能不满足时，c 的某个抬高（证明中以到 u 为止的所需之和）在原网支持 g 的每一步经 g 的耦合无别，并在 u 之前每步都支持 g：断裂之前，断裂还有多远不耦合入。 | S3 (@laws/5/zh) |
| Where g does not observe c and c is the only requirement of g that may be unmet up to a horizon u, some raise of c (in the proof, by the sum of the needs up to u) registers alike at every step at which the web supports g and supports g at every step up to u: before the break, how far off it is does not register. | Where c couples into g by threshold only and c is the only requirement of g that may be unmet up to a horizon u, some raise of c (in the proof, by the sum of the needs up to u) is alike through g's couplings at every step at which the web supports g and supports g at every step up to u: before the break, how far off it is does not couple in. | S3 (@laws/5/en) |
| 一个抬高在较低的网支持它的每一步识得相同 | 一个抬高在较低的网支持它的每一步经其耦合无别 | S3 |
| and a raise registers alike at every step at which the lower web supports it | and a raise is alike through its couplings at every step at which the lower web supports it | S3 |
| 平衡只在被识的条件上逐步假设（Flow.BalancesOn）；策略经识与其余固定是给出的假设，由它们推出交换随识；他者回应什么是给出的。 | 平衡只在耦合入的条件上逐步假设（Flow.BalancesOn）；策略经耦合与其余固定是给出的假设，由它们推出交换随耦合入者；他者回应什么是给出的。 | S2 (@hypotheses/0/zh) |
| Balance is assumed only on the conditions registered, at each step (Flow.BalancesOn); the policy going through registration and the rest being fixed are supplied hypotheses, from which exchange following registration is derived; what others respond to is supplied. | Balance is assumed only on the conditions coupled in, at each step (Flow.BalancesOn); the policy going through the couplings and the rest being fixed are supplied hypotheses, from which exchange following what couples in is derived; what others respond to is supplied. | S2 (@hypotheses/0/en) |
| 读法（AI 的，依 Ren 的交托采用）：失控是断裂之前自身之识与储量多出任意多的世界相同的回路；它的策略不能用上它所不识的；要避免它而又不放弃所有的，需要量度、模型（理）或他者之识； | 读法（AI 的，依 Ren 的交托采用）：失控是这样的回路：断裂之前，经它自身的耦合，世界与储量多出任意多的世界无别；它的策略不能用上只经阈耦合入它的余量；要避免它而又不放弃所有的，需要量度、模型（理）或另一形成的耦合； | S2 |
| Readings (the AI's, adopted on Ren's delegation): a runaway is a loop whose own registration before the break is that of a world with as much more stock as one likes; its policy cannot use what it does not register; avoiding it without forgoing what is there needs measurement, a model (理) or another formation's registration; | Readings (the AI's, adopted on Ren's delegation): a runaway is a loop through whose own couplings, before the break, the world is alike with a world with as much more stock as one likes; its policy cannot use a margin coupled into it by threshold only; avoiding it without forgoing what is there needs measurement, a model (理) or another formation's couplings; | S2 |
| 增量是策略的层面：经识的策略加其余固定推出交换随识。 | 增量是策略的层面：经耦合的策略加其余交换固定推出交换随耦合入者。 | S2 |
| the delta is the level of policies: a policy through registration with the rest fixed gives exchange following registration. | the delta is the level of policies: a policy through the couplings with the rest of the exchange fixed gives exchange following what couples in. | S2 |
| 平衡；交换随识。 | 平衡；交换随耦合入者。 | S2 (@dependencies/0/interface/conditions/zh) |
| Balance; exchange following registration. | Balance; exchange following what couples in. | S2 (@dependencies/0/interface/conditions/en) |
| 什么被识。 | 什么耦合入。 | S2 (@dependencies/1/interface/output/zh) |
| What registers. | What couples in. | S2 (@dependencies/1/interface/output/en) |
| 抬高不被识；观察时被识。 | 只经阈耦合入时抬高不显出；经水平耦合入时显出。 | S2 (@dependencies/1/interface/input/zh) |
| A raise does not register; observed, it does. | A raise coupled in by threshold only does not show; coupled in by level, it does. | S2 (@dependencies/1/interface/input/en) |
| 平衡只在被识的条件上逐步假设，抬高保持它。 | 平衡只在耦合入的条件上逐步假设，抬高保持它。 | S2 |
| balance is assumed only on the conditions registered, and raising keeps it. | balance is assumed only on the conditions coupled in, and raising keeps it. | S2 |
| 这里储备推迟多久不被识。 | 这里储备推迟多久不耦合入。 | S2 |
| here how long it defers it does not register. | here how long it defers it does not couple in. | S2 |
| 这里经支持之识连断裂的距离也不给。 | 这里经支持的耦合连断裂的距离也不给。 | S2 |
| here registration through support does not even give the distance to the break. | here coupling through support does not even give the distance to the break. | S2 |
| 这里失控的断裂在其前不被识；那里使之失控的回路的形成也不被它所携带的量识，能识它的是识耦合本身者。 | 这里失控的断裂在其前不耦合入；那里使之失控的回路的形成也不在它所携带的量中显出，能承载它的是耦合入耦合本身者。 | S2 |
| Here a runaway's break is not registered before it; there the formation of the loop that runs away is not registered by the amounts it carries either: what registers it is what registers the couplings themselves. | Here a runaway's break does not couple in before it; there the formation of the loop that runs away does not show in the amounts it carries either: what carries it is what the couplings themselves couple into. | S2 |
| 形成只经它所读的、在储量下游的水平识储量 | 储量只经形成所读的、在储量下游的水平耦合入它 | S2 |
| and a formation registers a stock only through a level it reads downstream of it | and a stock couples into a formation only through a level it reads downstream of the stock | S2 |

## `record:the-split-that-makes-sense`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| 确定的律下，保留所识并承载规律的最粗切分有意义；所识的部分单独有意义恰当其余对它的下一值不造成差别。每段所识的历史是一条确定的律，状态有限恰当所识从某一刻起重复。 | 确定的律下，保留映射之值并承载规律的最粗切分有意义；映射之值的一部分单独有意义，恰当其余对它的下一值不造成差别。每段值的历史是一条确定的律，状态有限恰当它从某一刻起重复。 | S1 (@statement/zh) |
| For a determined law, the coarsest split keeping what registers and carrying a law makes sense, and a registered part alone makes sense exactly where the rest makes no difference to its next value. Every history of values is a determined law, finite-state exactly where it is eventually periodic. | S1 (@statement/en) |
| 以 Becoming.Unfolding 的定义：确定的律 next 经识读 o 读作框架 Law.frameBy next o；描述有意义，指它恰把相同的配置等同，相同就是所识的未来相同。 | 以 Becoming.Unfolding 的定义：确定的律 next 经映射 o 读作框架 Law.frameBy next o；描述有意义，指它恰把相同的配置等同，相同就是经映射的未来相同。 | S1 (@laws/0/zh) |
| With Becoming.Unfolding's definitions: a determined law next read through a registration o is the frame Law.frameBy next o; a description makes sense when it identifies exactly the alike configurations, and alike is the same registered future. | With Becoming.Unfolding's definitions: a determined law next read through a map o is the frame Law.frameBy next o; a description makes sense when it identifies exactly the alike configurations, and alike is the same future through the map. | S1 (@laws/0/en) |
| 确定的律下，描述与律同去，恰当它把被等同者带到被等同者。保留所识并同去的切分以互模拟联系配置，不失去所识的任何东西；最粗的这样的切分有意义。 | 确定的律下，描述与律同去，恰当它把被等同者带到被等同者。保留映射之值并同去的切分以互模拟联系配置，不失去映射所取的任何东西；最粗的这样的切分有意义。 | S1 (@laws/1/zh) |
| Under a determined law, a description goes alike with the law exactly where it carries what it identifies into what it identifies. A split keeping the map's value and going alike relates configurations by a bisimulation and loses nothing the map takes, and the coarsest such split makes sense. | S1 (@laws/1/en) |
| 什么都不分的切分有意义，恰当所识的未来处处相同；识得一切时，配置自身有意义。 | 什么都不分的切分有意义，恰当经映射的未来处处相同；映射是恒等时，配置自身有意义。 | S1 (@laws/2/zh) |
| The split lumping everything makes sense exactly where the registered futures are all the same; registering everything, the configuration itself makes sense. | The split lumping everything makes sense exactly where the futures through the map are all the same; where the map is the identity, the configuration itself makes sense. | S1 (@laws/2/en) |
| 现在所识者自身有意义，恰当它固定自己所识的未来，恰当它与律同去，这是一步上的条件。 | 映射现在之值自身有意义，恰当它固定自己经映射的未来，恰当它与律同去，这是一步上的条件。 | S1 (@laws/3/zh) |
| What is registered now makes sense by itself exactly where it fixes its own registered future, exactly where it goes alike with the law, a condition on one step. | The map's present value makes sense by itself exactly where it fixes its own future through the map, exactly where it goes alike with the law, a condition on one step. | S1 (@laws/3/en) |
| 律在配对 (x, c) 上、识第一部分时，第一部分单独有意义恰当其余对它的下一值不造成差别，等价地恰当其余从不造成所识的差别；下一值由随之改变的规则给出（next (x, c) = (F c x, G c x)）时，恰当对每个 x、c、c′ 有 F c x = F c′ x，即 Becoming.Mechanism 的 adaptive_state_law_iff 的条件。 | 律在配对 (x, c) 上、映射取第一部分时，第一部分单独有意义恰当其余对它的下一值不造成差别，等价地恰当其余从不经映射造成差别；下一值由随之改变的规则给出（next (x, c) = (F c x, G c x)）时，恰当对每个 x、c、c′ 有 F c x = F c′ x，即 Becoming.Mechanism 的 adaptive_state_law_iff 的条件。 | S1 (@laws/4/zh) |
| For a law on pairs (x, c) registering the first part, the first part alone makes sense exactly where the rest makes no difference to its next value, equivalently where the rest never makes a registered difference; where the next value is set by a rule changing with it, next (x, c) = (F c x, G c x), exactly where F c x = F c′ x for every x, c and c′, the condition of adaptive_state_law_iff of Becoming.Mechanism. | For a law on pairs (x, c) whose map takes the first part, the first part alone makes sense exactly where the rest makes no difference to its next value, equivalently where the rest never makes a difference through the map; where the next value is set by a rule changing with it, next (x, c) = (F c x, G c x), exactly where F c x = F c′ x for every x, c and c′, the condition of adaptive_state_law_iff of Becoming.Mechanism. | S1 (@laws/4/en) |
| Motion.move (x, v) = (x + v, v) 是 adaptive 过程；只识 x 时这一对有意义，x 单独不承载规律，v 单独失去 x，两者单独都没有意义；不动的律下 x 单独有意义。 | Motion.move (x, v) = (x + v, v) 是 adaptive 过程；映射只取 x 时这一对有意义，x 单独不承载规律，v 单独失去 x，两者单独都没有意义；不动的律下 x 单独有意义。 | S1 (@laws/5/zh) |
| Motion.move (x, v) = (x + v, v) is an adaptive process; registering x, the pair makes sense, x alone carries no law, v alone loses x, and neither alone makes sense; under the still law, x alone makes sense. | Motion.move (x, v) = (x + v, v) is an adaptive process; with a map taking x, the pair makes sense, x alone carries no law, v alone loses x, and neither alone makes sense; under the still law, x alone makes sense. | S1 (@laws/5/en) |
| 一段所识的序列 o 中，时刻 t 与 t′ 是同一状态，指对每个 n 有 o (t + n) = o (t′ + n)。这是等价关系，并作为自身继续；沿轨道读就是所识的未来相同；每个时刻所识都不同时，每个时刻是自己的状态。 | 一段值的序列 o 中，时刻 t 与 t′ 是同一状态，指对每个 n 有 o (t + n) = o (t′ + n)。这是等价关系，并作为自身继续；沿轨道读就是经映射的未来相同；每个时刻的值都不同时，每个时刻是自己的状态。 | S1 (@laws/6/zh) |
| In a registered sequence o, moments t and t′ are one state when o (t + n) = o (t′ + n) for every n. This is an equivalence that goes on as itself, it is the same future through the map read along an orbit, and where every moment takes a different value each moment is its own state. | S1 (@laws/6/en) |
| 每段所识的历史是经识读读出的确定的律：时刻之律下（每个时刻走到下一个），两个时刻相同，恰当它们是同一状态。 | 每段值的历史是经映射读出的确定的律：时刻之律下（每个时刻走到下一个），两个时刻相同，恰当它们是同一状态。 | S1 (@laws/7/zh) |
| Every registered history is a determined law read through a registration: under the law of moments, each going on to the next, two moments are alike exactly where they are one state. | Every history of values is a determined law read through a map: under the law of moments, each going on to the next, two moments are alike exactly where they are one state. | S1 (@laws/7/en) |
| 不加判定的按关系的鸽笼给出：所识的历史状态有限（每个时刻与某个 L 以下的时刻是同一状态），恰当它有两个时刻是同一状态。返回的配置在每个识读之下状态有限。 | 不加判定的按关系的鸽笼给出：值的历史状态有限（每个时刻与某个 L 以下的时刻是同一状态），恰当它有两个时刻是同一状态。返回的配置经每个映射状态有限。 | S1 (@laws/8/zh) |

## `record:counterfactual-is-the-course`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| 经识回应推出：律经识而读，是指部分之外每个形成在第一个网下识得相同到第 t 步的两段历史，律在第 t 步给出相同；这时其余在律的任意两个进程中经识回应。 | 经耦合回应推出：律经耦合而读，是指部分之外每个形成在第一个网下到第 t 步经其耦合无别的两段历史，律在第 t 步给出相同；这时其余在律的任意两个进程中经其耦合回应。 | S2 (@laws/7/zh) |
| Responding through registration follows: the law reads through a registration when, for every formation outside the part, two histories it registers alike under the first web up to step t give alike at step t; then the rest responds through its registration in any two courses of the law. | Responding through the couplings follows: the law reads through the couplings when, for every formation outside the part, two histories alike through its couplings under the first web up to step t give alike at step t; then the rest responds through its couplings in any two courses of the law. | S2 (@laws/7/en) |
| 每个读已立者的律经识一切而读；形成的交换对一个条件敏感，是指某步只改该条件的水平会改变它。 | 每个读已立者的律经一切之耦合而读；形成的交换对一个条件敏感，是指某步只改该条件的水平会改变它。 | S2 (@laws/8/zh) |
| Every law of what has stood reads through registering everything; a formation's exchange is sensitive to a condition when at some step changing that condition's levels alone changes it. | Every law of what has stood reads through the coupling of everything; a formation's exchange is sensitive to a condition when at some step changing that condition's levels alone changes it. | S2 (@laws/8/en) |
| 条件有限（一个列表含每个条件）、条件的相等可判定时，读已立者的律经每个观察其敏感者的识而读，尤其经其敏感本身而读；证明逐个条件替换。 | 条件有限（一个列表含每个条件）、条件的相等可判定时，读已立者的律经每个其敏感者经水平耦合入的耦合而读，尤其经其敏感本身而读；证明逐个条件替换。 | S2 (@laws/9/zh) |
| With finitely many conditions (a list holding every condition) and decidable equality of conditions, a law of what has stood reads through every registration observing what it is sensitive to, so through its sensitivity itself, by changing one condition at a time. | With finitely many conditions (a list holding every condition) and decidable equality of conditions, a law of what has stood reads through every coupling by which what it is sensitive to couples in by level, so through its sensitivity itself, by changing one condition at a time. | S2 (@laws/9/en) |
| 反之，律所经的每个识，连同形成所依存的，都不漏掉它敏感的条件（双重否定）；不判定敏感，只判定两个数是否相等。 | 反之，律所经的每个耦合，连同形成所依存的，都不漏掉它敏感的条件（双重否定）；不判定敏感，只判定两个数是否相等。 | S2 (@laws/10/zh) |
| Conversely, every registration a law reads through, with what the formation depends on, leaves out no condition the exchange is sensitive to (double-negated); no sensitivity is decided, only whether two numbers are equal. | Conversely, every coupling a law reads through, with what the formation depends on, leaves out no condition the exchange is sensitive to (double-negated); no sensitivity is decided, only whether two numbers are equal. | S2 (@laws/10/en) |
| 比较：律的两个平衡的进程在场形成相同、起始相同、律经一个识而读时，对覆盖每个不在经此识画出的回应所及之外者（双重否定）的任何意义，每个保留另一个上有意义者恰如包含它，两者都不增加恰当两者都不超出。 | 比较：律的两个平衡的进程在场形成相同、起始相同、律经一个耦合而读时，对覆盖每个不在经此耦合画出的回应所及之外者（双重否定）的任何意义，每个保留另一个上有意义者恰如包含它，两者都不增加恰当两者都不超出。 | S2 (@laws/11/zh) |
| The comparison: for two balanced courses of the law with the same formations present, starting alike, the law reading through a registration, any significance covering every formation not beyond the responsive reach drawn through that registration (double-negated) makes each retain along the other exactly as it includes it, and neither add exactly where neither exceeds. | The comparison: for two balanced courses of the law with the same formations present, starting alike, the law reading through a coupling, any significance covering every formation not beyond the responsive reach drawn through that coupling (double-negated) makes each retain along the other exactly as it includes it, and neither add exactly where neither exceeds. | S2 (@laws/11/en) |
| 条件有限时识取作律的敏感；第二个流取作另一个动作生成的、可行的进程时亦然，不另给第二个流与回应。 | 条件有限时这个耦合取作律的敏感；第二个流取作另一个动作生成的、可行的进程时亦然，不另给第二个流与回应。 | S2 (@laws/12/zh) |
| With finitely many conditions the registration is the law's sensitivity; likewise with the second flow the feasible course another act generates, with neither the second flow nor the response supplied. | With finitely many conditions the coupling is the law's sensitivity; likewise with the second flow the feasible course another act generates, with neither the second flow nor the response supplied. | S2 (@laws/12/en) |
| 规律 8 与 12 改取律经识而读（ExchangeLaw.Through） | 规律 8 与 12 改取律经耦合而读（ExchangeLaw.Through） | S2 |
| laws 8 and 12 take instead that the law reads through a registration (ExchangeLaw.Through) | laws 8 and 12 take instead that the law reads through a coupling (ExchangeLaw.Through) | S2 |
| 经识回应的推出近于定义；实质内容是反事实由动作固定、生成与可行的等价、敏感是最小的识、因果模型的实例与回路由律推出。 | 经耦合回应的推出近于定义；实质内容是反事实由动作固定、生成与可行的等价、敏感是最小的耦合、因果模型的实例与回路由律推出。 | S2 (@does_not_show/1/zh) |
| Responding through registration follows near-definitionally; the substance is the act fixing the counterfactual, generation as feasibility, sensitivity as least registration, the causal-model instance and the loop from the law. | Responding through the couplings follows near-definitionally; the substance is the act fixing the counterfactual, generation as feasibility, sensitivity as least coupling, the causal-model instance and the loop from the law. | S2 (@does_not_show/1/en) |
| 对未被识者的回应 | 对不耦合入者的回应 | S2 |

## `record:where-a-level-couples`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| A response of an exchange to one level is a coupling of every strength array of the law. For a law reading through registration only registered levels couple; by threshold only a crossing passes, through the conjunction of the needs, and observing is exactly where a level can couple within its need. | An exchange's response to one level is a coupling of every strength array of the law. For a law reading through the couplings only levels coupled in respond; by threshold only a crossing passes, through the needs' conjunction, and coupling by level is exactly where a level couples within its need. | S2 (@statement/en) |
| 交换对一个水平的回应是律的每个强度阵列的耦合。对经所识而读的律，只有所识的水平耦合；经阈只有跨越得过，且经所需之合取，观察恰是水平能在所需之内耦合之处。 | 交换对一个水平的回应是律的每个强度阵列的耦合。对经耦合而读的律，只有耦合入的水平被回应；经阈只有跨越得过，且经所需之合取，经水平耦合恰是水平能在所需之内耦合之处。 | S2 (@statement/zh) |
| Only what couples into a formation responds: for a law reading through registration, a level a formation outside the part neither depends on nor observes is coupled into its exchanges with strength zero. A response thus comes from a registered condition (as a double negation), is read in every schedule covering registration, and is a route of reading and of the law's strengths restricted to that schedule. | Only what couples into a formation responds: for a law reading through the couplings, a level that couples into a formation outside the part neither by threshold nor by level is coupled into its exchanges with strength zero. A response thus comes from a condition that couples in (as a double negation), is read in every schedule covering what couples in, and is a route of reading and of the law's strengths restricted to that schedule. | S2 (@laws/1/en) |
| 只有耦合入形成者才被回应：对一个律，若它对部分之外的每个形成只读该形成所识者，则形成既不依赖也不观察的水平，在每一步以强度零耦合入它与每个条件的交换；所以回应来自它所识的条件（以双重否定），并在覆盖所识的每个时程中被读，是 Becoming.Observation 的所读路线的耦合，也是律的强度限于该时程后的路线。 | 只有耦合入形成者才被回应：对一个律，若它对部分之外的每个形成只读耦合入该形成者，则既不经阈也不经水平耦合入形成的水平，在每一步以强度零耦合入它与每个条件的交换；所以回应来自耦合入的条件（以双重否定），并在覆盖耦合入者的每个时程中被读，是 Becoming.Observation 的所读路线的耦合，也是律的强度限于该时程后的路线。 | S2 (@laws/1/zh) |
| By threshold, only a crossing passes: for a law reading through registration, where a formation outside the part does not observe a condition, a change of that condition's level at one step that leaves the formation's need for it met alike changes nothing the formation exchanges. | By threshold, only a crossing passes: for a law reading through the couplings, where a condition couples into a formation outside the part by threshold only, a change of that condition's level at one step that leaves the formation's need for it met alike changes nothing the formation exchanges. | S2 (@laws/2/en) |
| 经阈只过跨越：对经所识而读的律，部分之外的形成不观察一个条件时，该条件在一步的水平的改变若使形成对它的所需同样得足，则不改变形成所交换的任何东西。 | 经阈只过跨越：对经耦合而读的律，一个条件只经阈耦合入部分之外的形成时，该条件在一步的水平的改变若使形成对它的所需同样得足，则不改变形成所交换的任何东西。 | S2 (@laws/2/zh) |
| By threshold, a crossing passes: for a law reading through registration, a response to a condition the formation does not observe is a crossing of its need (the requirement not refuted, the two levels not meeting it alike); where the formation requires at a step exactly that condition, with a positive need, a law reading through registration responds to a crossing there, at every condition. | By threshold, a crossing passes: for a law reading through the couplings, a response to a condition not coupled in by level is a crossing of its need (the requirement not refuted, the two levels not meeting it alike); where the formation requires at a step exactly that condition, with a positive need, a law reading through the couplings responds to a crossing there, at every condition. | S2 (@laws/3/en) |
| 经阈则跨越得过：对经所识而读的律，对形成不观察之条件的回应是其所需的跨越（所需不被否定，两个水平不同样满足它）；形成在一步恰只需要该条件且所需为正时，有经所识而读的律在那里对跨越回应，于每个条件。 | 经阈则跨越得过：对经耦合而读的律，对不经水平耦合入之条件的回应是其所需的跨越（所需不被否定，两个水平不同样满足它）；形成在一步恰只需要该条件且所需为正时，有经耦合而读的律在那里对跨越回应，于每个条件。 | S2 (@laws/3/zh) |
| Among several conditions, a crossing passes through the conjunction: a response of a law reading through registration to an unobserved condition changes the formation's admission at that step, only where its other requirements there are met; where admission there is decided, some such law responds to a crossing into every later exchange exactly where the condition is required with a positive need. | Among several conditions, a crossing passes through the conjunction: a response of a law reading through the couplings to a condition not coupled in by level changes the formation's admission at that step, only where its other requirements there are met; where admission there is decided, some such law responds to a crossing into every later exchange exactly where the condition is required with a positive need. | S2 (@laws/4/en) |
| 多个条件之间，跨越经合取而过：经所识而读的律对不观察之条件的回应改变形成在该步的容许，且只在它那一步的其他所需已满足之处；该步容许可判定时，恰在该条件被需要且所需为正之处，有这样的律对跨越回应，及于之后每一步的交换。 | 多个条件之间，跨越经合取而过：经耦合而读的律对不经水平耦合入之条件的回应改变形成在该步的容许，且只在它那一步的其他所需已满足之处；该步容许可判定时，恰在该条件被需要且所需为正之处，有这样的律对跨越回应，及于之后每一步的交换。 | S2 (@laws/4/zh) |
| By level, the level passes: where marked formations observe a condition, the law taking its level reads through registration and responds to it within the need at every step. So, for a formation outside the part, it is not refuted that it observes the condition exactly where it is not refuted that some law reading through its registration responds to it by level. | By level, the level passes: where a condition couples into marked formations by level, the law taking its level reads through the couplings and responds to it within the need at every step. So, for a formation outside the part, it is not refuted that the condition couples into it by level exactly where it is not refuted that some law reading through its couplings responds to it by level. | S2 (@laws/5/en) |
| 经水平则水平得过，观察恰是它能过之处：被标记的形成观察一个条件时，取该条件水平的律经所识而读，并在每一步、每个条件在所需之内回应该水平；所以对部分之外的形成，它观察该条件不被否定，恰当某个经其所识而读的律经水平回应该条件不被否定。 | 经水平则水平得过，经水平耦合恰是它能过之处：一个条件经水平耦合入被标记的形成时，取该条件水平的律经耦合而读，并在每一步、每个条件在所需之内回应该水平；所以对部分之外的形成，该条件经水平耦合入它不被否定，恰当某个经其耦合而读的律经水平回应该条件不被否定。 | S2 (@laws/5/zh) |
| for laws through registration, the law reading through it and the formation outside the part; a covering schedule for reading; | for laws through the couplings, the law reading through them and the formation outside the part; a schedule covering what couples in, for reading; | S2 |

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|

| The least registration with any number of conditions | The least coupling with any number of conditions | S2 (@title/en) |
| 律在每个历史下读有限多个条件时，经其敏感而读：于双重否定下，敏感是它所经的最小的识。给定消失之判定（它给出有限全知原则），尾之律是反例；条件编号时，每个反例都给出弱有限全知原则。 | 律在每个历史下读有限多个条件时，经其敏感而读：于双重否定下，敏感是它所经的最小的耦合。给定消失之判定（它给出有限全知原则），尾之律是反例；条件编号时，每个反例都给出弱有限全知原则。 | S2 (@statement/zh) |
| A law reading finitely many conditions under each history reads through what it is sensitive to: up to double negation, sensitivity is the least registration. Given a decision of vanishing (giving LPO) the tail law is a counterexample; with numbered conditions every counterexample gives WLPO. | A law reading finitely many conditions under each history reads through what it is sensitive to: up to double negation, sensitivity is the least coupling. Given a decision of vanishing (giving LPO) the tail law is a counterexample; with numbered conditions every counterexample gives WLPO. | S2 (@statement/en) |
| 经每个观察其所敏感者的识而读，而它所经的每个识都于双重否定下含其敏感者：在这一意义下敏感是最小的识， | 经每个其所敏感者经水平耦合入的耦合而读，而它所经的每个耦合都于双重否定下含其敏感者：在这一意义下敏感是最小的耦合， | S2 |
| reads through every registration observing what it is sensitive to, and every one it reads through holds that up to double negation: sensitivity is the least registration in that sense, | reads through every coupling by which what it is sensitive to couples in by level, and every one it reads through holds that up to double negation: sensitivity is the least coupling in that sense, | S2 |
| 经识从任何一个条件起的全部条件而读 | 经从任何一个条件起的全部条件之耦合而读 | S2 |
| 它所经的识没有最小的。 | 它所经的耦合没有最小的。 | S2 |
| and the registrations it reads through have no least one. | and the couplings it reads through have no least one. | S2 |
| 条件无穷多时敏感是否仍是最小的识，或有反例。 | 条件无穷多时敏感是否仍是最小的耦合，或有反例。 | S2 |

## `record:contextual-registration`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| 语境中的识 | 语境中显示的主张 | S7 (@title/zh) |
| Contextual registration | Claims shown in context | S7 (@title/en) |
| 诸识恰在语境相交处一致时粘合，不依语境的主张的框架恰在处处粘合时合；有的世界在任何配置都不粘合（Specker 的盒子；两量子比特的 Mermin–Peres 方阵）。部分失去整体有两种方式：不给回整体，或没有整体。 | 诸语境所显示者恰在语境相交处一致时粘合，不依语境的主张的框架恰在处处粘合时合；有的世界在任何配置都不粘合（Specker 的盒子；两量子比特的 Mermin–Peres 方阵）。部分失去整体有两种方式：不给回整体，或没有整体。 | S7 (@statement/zh) |
| Registrations glue exactly where contexts agree where they meet, and a frame of context-free claims fits exactly where they glue everywhere; some worlds glue nowhere (Specker's boxes; the two-qubit Mermin–Peres square). Parts fail a whole in two ways: they do not give it back, or there is none. | What contexts show glues exactly where contexts agree where they meet, and a frame of context-free claims fits exactly where it glues everywhere; some worlds glue nowhere (Specker's boxes; the two-qubit Mermin–Peres square). Parts fail a whole in two ways: they do not give it back, or there is none. | S7 (@statement/en) |
| 在语境中被识的主张（Contextual S K C V）：within k c 说语境 k 识主张 c，shows s k c 是 k 在配置 s 处对 c 所识的值。在 s 处诸识粘合（Contextual.Glues），指有 v : C → V 与每个语境在其所识的每个主张上一致；它推出识同一主张的任意两个语境一致（Contextual.Glues.agree），每个主张有识它的语境时两者等价（Contextual.glues_iff_agree）。连同语境读，每次识是框架的主张（Contextual.indexed），以语境为索引的框架合于每次识（Contextual.indexed_fits）；不依语境的主张的框架合（Contextual.FitsFree），恰当诸识在每个配置粘合（Contextual.fitsFree_iff）。语境能显示什么只取决于它所识的主张（Local） | 在语境中显示的主张（Contextual S K C V）：within k c 说主张 c 在语境 k 之内，shows s k c 是 k 在配置 s 处显示的 c 的值。在 s 处诸语境所显示者粘合（Contextual.Glues），指有 v : C → V 与每个语境在其内的每个主张上一致；它推出同一主张所在的任意两个语境一致（Contextual.Glues.agree），每个主张在某个语境之内时两者等价（Contextual.glues_iff_agree）。连同语境读，所显示的每一个是框架的主张（Contextual.indexed），以语境为索引的框架合于所显示的每一个（Contextual.indexed_fits）；不依语境的主张的框架合（Contextual.FitsFree），恰当诸语境所显示者在每个配置粘合（Contextual.fitsFree_iff）。语境能显示什么只取决于其内的主张（Local） | S7 |
| Claims registered in contexts (Contextual S K C V): within k c says that context k registers claim c, and shows s k c is what k registers of c at configuration s. At s the registrations glue (Contextual.Glues) when some v : C → V agrees with every context on every claim it registers; this gives that any two contexts registering a claim agree (Contextual.Glues.agree), and where each claim has a context registering it the two are equivalent (Contextual.glues_iff_agree). Read with its context, every registration is a claim of a frame (Contextual.indexed), and the indexed frame fits every registration (Contextual.indexed_fits); a frame of context-free claims fits (Contextual.FitsFree) exactly where the registrations glue at every configuration (Contextual.fitsFree_iff). Where what a context can show depends only on the claims it registers (Local) | Claims shown in contexts (Contextual S K C V): within k c says that claim c lies within context k, and shows s k c is what k shows of c at configuration s. At s what the contexts show glues (Contextual.Glues) when some v : C → V agrees with every context on every claim within it; this gives that any two contexts a claim lies within agree (Contextual.Glues.agree), and where each claim lies within some context the two are equivalent (Contextual.glues_iff_agree). Read with its context, everything shown is a claim of a frame (Contextual.indexed), and the indexed frame fits everything shown (Contextual.indexed_fits); a frame of context-free claims fits (Contextual.FitsFree) exactly where what the contexts show glues at every configuration (Contextual.fitsFree_iff). Where what a context can show depends only on the claims within it (Local) | S7 |
| 要每个主张有一个识它的语境（home、covers）。 | 要每个主张在一个语境之内（home、covers）。 | S7 |
| and need a context registering each claim (home, covers). | and need a context each claim lies within (home, covers). | S7 |
| 量子的识属于不粘合的一类 | 量子测量所显示者属于不粘合的一类 | S7 |
| quantum registrations are of the kind that does not glue | what quantum measurements show is of the kind that does not glue | S7 |
| 值是在语境中的识，即关系； | 值是在语境中显示者，即关系； | S7 |
| a value is a registration in context, a relation; | a value is what a context shows, a relation; | S7 |
| 语境中的诸识总是一个世界的关系项 | 语境中所显示者总是一个世界的关系项 | S7 |
| Registrations in context are always the relata of one world | What contexts show is always the relata of one world | S7 |

## `record:selection-through-a-model-of-the-options`

| Current | Proposed | Sense |
|---|---|---|
| Selection through a model of the options | Selection through a map of the alternatives | ALT (@title/en) |
| 对相同已定的选项与给每个选项任何种类条目的模型：两个选项被每个经模型的选择选得相同，恰当模型使二者并列；至多选一个选项的这种选择能选出 k，恰当没有别的选项的条目等于 k 的。 | 对相同已定的备择与给每个备择任何种类条目的映射：两个备择被每个经映射的选择选得相同，恰当映射使二者并列；至多选一个备择的这种选择能选出 k，恰当没有别的备择的条目等于 k 的。 | ALT (@statement/zh) |
| For options with decided equality and a model giving each an entry of any kind: two options are selected alike by every selection through the model exactly when the model ties them, and such a selection selecting at most one option can select k exactly when no other option's entry equals k's. | For alternatives with decided equality and a map giving each an entry of any kind: two are selected alike by every selection through the map exactly when the map ties them, and such a selection selecting at most one alternative can select k exactly when no other alternative's entry equals k's. | ALT (@statement/en) |
| 交换两个选项（swap）是对合。选择、取值与选取经模型：交换两个选项的条目，即交换所选者、所取的值或所选取者。交换两个并列的选项，模型不变。 | 交换两个备择（swap）是对合。选择、取值与选取经映射：交换两个备择的条目，即交换所选者、所取的值或所选取者。交换两个并列的备择，映射不变。 | ALT (@laws/0/zh) |
| Exchanging two options (swap) is an involution. A selection, a valuation and a choice are through the model when exchanging two options' entries exchanges what is selected, the values or the choice. Exchanging two tied options leaves the model as it was. | Exchanging two alternatives (swap) is an involution. A selection, a valuation and a choice are through the map when exchanging two alternatives' entries exchanges what is selected, the values or the choice. Exchanging two tied alternatives leaves the map as it was. | ALT (@laws/0/en) |
| 并列的选项被选得相同；两个选项被每个经模型的选择选得相同，恰当模型使二者并列。见证是选出条目等于给定值的选项。 | 并列的备择被选得相同；两个备择被每个经映射的选择选得相同，恰当映射使二者并列。见证是选出条目等于给定值的备择。 | ALT (@laws/1/zh) |
| Tied options are selected alike, and two options are selected alike by every selection through the model exactly when the model ties them. The witness selects the options whose entry equals a given value. | Tied alternatives are selected alike, and two alternatives are selected alike by every selection through the map exactly when the map ties them. The witness selects the alternatives whose entry equals a given value. | ALT (@laws/1/en) |
| 至多选一个的经模型的选择不选并列的选项，两个以上选项全并列时不选。单独持有一个条目（Alone）是这样的选择，所以一个选项能被单独选出，恰当没有别的选项的条目与它相同。 | 至多选一个的经映射的选择不选并列的备择，两个以上备择全并列时不选。单独持有一个条目（Alone）是这样的选择，所以一个备择能被单独选出，恰当没有别的备择的条目与它相同。 | ALT (@laws/2/zh) |
| A selection through the model that selects at most one option selects no tied option, and none at a full tie of two or more. Holding an entry alone (Alone) is such a selection, so an option can be singled out exactly when no other option's entry ties it. | A selection through the map that selects at most one alternative selects no tied alternative, and none at a full tie of two or more. Holding an entry alone (Alone) is such a selection, so an alternative can be singled out exactly when no other alternative's entry ties it. | ALT (@laws/2/en) |
| 经模型的取值（如权重）给并列的选项相同的值，全并列时处处相同；取某值的选项是经模型的选择；常值经模型；两个选项被每个这样的取值取得相同，恰当二者并列。 | 经映射的取值（如权重）给并列的备择相同的值，全并列时处处相同；取某值的备择是经映射的选择；常值经映射；两个备择被每个这样的取值取得相同，恰当二者并列。 | ALT (@laws/3/zh) |
| A valuation through the model, such as a weight on each option, gives tied options equal values, and every option the same at a full tie; the options taking a value form a selection through the model; a constant valuation is one; two options are valued alike by every such valuation exactly when tied. | A valuation through the map, such as a weight on each alternative, gives tied alternatives equal values, and every alternative the same at a full tie; the alternatives taking a value form a selection through the map; a constant valuation is one; two alternatives are valued alike by every such valuation exactly when tied. | ALT (@laws/3/en) |
| 经模型、至多选取一个选项的选取，其所选取者是经模型的选择；它不选取并列的选项，全并列时不选取。 | 经映射、至多选取一个备择的选取，其所选取者是经映射的选择；它不选取并列的备择，全并列时不选取。 | ALT (@laws/4/zh) |
| A choice of at most one option through the model selects, through the model, the option it chooses; it chooses no tied option and withholds at a full tie. | A choice of at most one alternative through the map selects, through the map, the alternative it chooses; it chooses no tied alternative and withholds at a full tie. | ALT (@laws/4/en) |
| 模型是一个归并（Learning.Lumped）：经模型的选择是经其归并的选择，对要紧者（谓词 p 或取值 f）正确，只在要紧者经归并同去之处；要紧者分开模型所并的两个选项时，无一正确；要紧者从模型读出时，经模型读出即正确。 | 映射是一个归并（Learning.Lumped）：经映射的选择是经其归并的选择，对要紧者（谓词 p 或取值 f）正确，只在要紧者经归并同去之处；要紧者分开映射所并的两个备择时，无一正确；要紧者从映射读出时，经映射读出即正确。 | ALT (@laws/5/zh) |
| The model is a lumping (Learning.Lumped): a selection through it is a selection through its lumping, right about what matters (a predicate p or values f) only where what matters goes alike across the lumping; where what matters separates two options the model lumps, none is right; where it is read off the model, reading it through the model is right. | The map is a lumping (Learning.Lumped): a selection through it is a selection through its lumping, right about what matters (a predicate p or values f) only where what matters goes alike across the lumping; where what matters separates two alternatives the map lumps, none is right; where it is read off the map, reading it through the map is right. | ALT (@laws/5/en) |
| 实例（外围，自有检查器，亦编译审计原文件）：PluralRecords 的记录即 Fin n 上的选项模型，同等对待中心的规则即经模型的选取，TieBreaking 同等对待的抽签其权重经模型；七条定理由核心律给出，PluralRecords.pick 找出单独持有者，公理报告皆空。 | 实例（外围，自有检查器，亦编译审计原文件）：PluralRecords 的记录即 Fin n 上的备择映射，同等对待中心的规则即经映射的选取，TieBreaking 同等对待的抽签其权重经映射；七条定理由核心律给出，PluralRecords.pick 找出单独持有者，公理报告皆空。 | ALT (@laws/6/zh) |
| Instance (periphery, own checker, which also compiles and audits the originals): PluralRecords' records are models of the options Fin n, a rule treating the centers alike is a choice through the model, and a lottery treating them alike in TieBreaking has weights through it; seven theorems follow from the core, PluralRecords.pick finding the holder, every axiom report empty. | Instance (periphery, own checker, which also compiles and audits the originals): PluralRecords' records are maps of the alternatives Fin n, a rule treating the centers alike is a choice through the map, and a lottery treating them alike in TieBreaking has weights through it; seven theorems follow from the core, PluralRecords.pick finding the holder, every axiom report empty. | ALT (@laws/6/en) |
| 不加公理。选项的相同已定（交换两个选项需要它）；条目任意种类；选择、取值或选取经模型是假设；要紧者（p、f）是给出的。 | 不加公理。备择的相同已定（交换两个备择需要它）；条目任意种类；选择、取值或选取经映射是假设；要紧者（p、f）是给出的。 | ALT (@hypotheses/0/zh) |
| No axiom. The options' equality is decided (exchanging two options needs it); entries are of any kind; that a selection, valuation or choice is through the model is a hypothesis, and what matters (p, f) is given. | No axiom. The alternatives' equality is decided (exchanging two alternatives needs it); entries are of any kind; that a selection, valuation or choice is through the map is a hypothesis, and what matters (p, f) is given. | ALT (@hypotheses/0/en) |
| 单独选出的选项由一个选取函数找到：这需在列出的选项中搜索，外围用 PluralRecords.pick。 | 单独选出的备择由一个选取函数找到：这需在列出的备择中搜索，外围用 PluralRecords.pick。 | ALT (@does_not_show/0/zh) |
| That an option singled out is found by a choice function: that needs a search over listed options, the periphery's PluralRecords.pick. | That an alternative singled out is found by a choice function: that needs a search over listed alternatives, the periphery's PluralRecords.pick. | ALT (@does_not_show/0/en) |
| 选项的相同不判定时的形式：交换两个选项需要判定。 | 备择的相同不判定时的形式：交换两个备择需要判定。 | ALT (@does_not_show/2/zh) |
| A form without the options' equality decided: exchanging two options needs it. | A form without the alternatives' equality decided: exchanging two alternatives needs it. | ALT (@does_not_show/2/en) |
| 哪个模型对、什么要紧：p 与 f 是给出的；更细的模型从哪里来属于学习。 | 哪个映射对、什么要紧：p 与 f 是给出的；更细的映射从哪里来属于学习。 | ALT (@does_not_show/3/zh) |
| Which model is right, or what matters: p and f are given; where a finer model comes from belongs to learning. | Which map is right, or what matters: p and f are given; where a finer map comes from belongs to learning. | ALT (@does_not_show/3/en) |
| 独立审查；模型的条目为选项之间关系时的同一律。 | 独立审查；映射的条目为备择之间关系时的同一律。 | ALT (@next_delta/zh) |
| An independent review; the same law where the model's entries are relations between options. | An independent review; the same law where the map's entries are relations between alternatives. | ALT (@next_delta/en) |
| 模型是一个归并：经它的选择只在要紧者经归并同去之处正确，与 lumped_right_iff 同形。 | 映射是一个归并：经它的选择只在要紧者经归并同去之处正确，与 lumped_right_iff 同形。 | ALT (@related/1/detail/zh) |
| The model is a lumping: a selection through it is right only where what matters goes alike across the lumping, the form of lumped_right_iff. | The map is a lumping: a selection through it is right only where what matters goes alike across the lumping, the form of lumped_right_iff. | ALT (@related/1/detail/en) |
| 经选项模型的选择 | 经备择之映射的选择 | ALT (@title/zh) |
| Along a run: with the moments of a run as the options and the run's registered course as the selector's model, every selection, valuation and choice through the course treats alike the moments it registers alike and separates exactly what it registers apart; where the course registers one value throughout nothing is singled out; a selection or valuation through the course is right about a target exactly where the target goes alike across the registration, and not where the course parts moments the target tells apart. | Along a run: with the moments of a run as the alternatives and the run's course through a map as the selector's map, every selection, valuation and choice through the course treats alike the moments at which the map takes one value and separates exactly what the map separates; where the course takes one value throughout nothing is singled out; a selection or valuation through the course is right about a feature exactly where the feature goes alike across the map's lumping, and not where the course lumps moments the feature tells apart. | S1 (@laws/7/en) |
| 同一形式的律：经识的选择恰及所识（Web.selects_alike_iff），经模型的选择恰分开模型所分开者。 | 同一形式的律：经耦合的选择恰把经耦合无别者选得相同（Web.selects_alike_iff），经映射的选择恰分开映射所分开者。 | S3 (@related/0/detail/zh) |
| A law of the same form: selection through registration reaches exactly what registers (Web.selects_alike_iff), and selection through a model separates exactly what the model separates. | A law of the same form: selection through the couplings selects alike exactly what is alike through them (Web.selects_alike_iff), and selection through a map separates exactly what the map separates. | S3 (@related/0/detail/en) |
